Snapshot: 20260514_0459
Covering Human Action Space for Computer Use: Data Synthesis and Benchmark
Authors: Miaosen Zhang, Xiaohan Zhao, Zhihong Tan, Zhou Huoshen, Yijia Fan, Yifan Yang, Kai Qiu, Bei Liu, Justin Wagle, Chenzhong Yin, Mingxi Cheng, Ji Li, Qi Dai, Chong Luo, Xu Yang, Xin Geng, Baining Guo
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Computer-use agents (CUAs) automate on-screen work, as illustrated by GPT-5.4 and Claude. Yet their reliability on complex, low-frequency interactions is still poor, limiting user trust. Our analysis of failure cases from advanced models suggests a long-tail pattern in GUI operations, where a relatively small fraction of complex and diverse interactions accounts for a disproportionate share of task failures. We hypothesize that this issue largely stems from the scarcity of data for complex interactions. To address this problem, we propose a new benchmark CUActSpot for evaluating models' capabilities on complex interactions across five modalities: GUI, text, table, canvas, and natural image, as well as a variety of actions (click, drag, draw, etc.), covering a broader range of interaction types than prior click-centric benchmarks that focus mainly on GUI widgets. We also design a renderer-based data-synthesis pipeline: scenes are automatically generated for each modality, screenshots and element coordinates are recorded, and an LLM produces matching instructions and action traces. After training on this corpus, our Phi-Ground-Any-4B outperforms open-source models with fewer than 32B parameters. We will release our benchmark, data, code, and models at https://github.com/microsoft/Phi-Ground.git
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向计算机使用的覆盖人类操作空间：数据合成与基准
计算机使用代理（CUAs）可自动化屏幕操作，如GPT-5.4和Claude所示。然而，它们在复杂且低频交互中的可靠性仍较差，限制了用户信任。我们对先进模型失败案例的分析表明，GUI操作存在长尾分布模式，一小部分复杂多样的交互占任务失败的不成比例份额。我们推测，这一问题主要源于复杂交互数据的稀缺。为解决此问题，我们提出新的基准CUActSpot，用于评估模型在五种模态（GUI、文本、表格、画布、自然图像）及多种操作（点击、拖拽、绘制等）下的复杂交互能力，覆盖范围远超以往主要聚焦GUI控件的点击中心基准。我们还设计了基于渲染器的数据合成流水线：为每种模态自动生成场景，记录截图与元素坐标，并由大语言模型生成匹配的指令与操作轨迹。在该语料库训练后，我们的Phi-Ground-Any-4B模型在参数量少于32B的开源模型中表现更优。我们将于https://github.com/microsoft/Phi-Ground.git发布该基准、数据、代码与模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor reliability of computer-use agents on complex, low-frequency GUI interactions, this work identifies a long-tail distribution of failure cases and attributes it to insufficient training data for such operations. To address this, the authors introduce CUActSpot, a benchmark covering five modalities and diverse actions beyond simple clicks, and a renderer-based data synthesis pipeline that automatically generates scenes, captures screenshots and element coordinates, and uses an LLM to produce corresponding instructions and action traces. Experimental results show that a model trained on this synthesized data, Phi-Ground-Any-4B, outperforms other open-source models with fewer than 32B parameters on complex interaction tasks.
为提升计算机使用代理在复杂低频GUI交互中的可靠性，作者分析失败案例，归因于此类操作的数据稀缺。他们提出CUActSpot多模态基准，覆盖GUI、文本、表格、画布和自然图像及多种动作（点击、拖拽、绘制等），超越以往以点击为主的基准。通过渲染器驱动的数据合成流程，自动生成场景、记录截图与元素坐标，并用大语言模型生成指令与操作轨迹。在该合成数据上训练后，其Phi-Ground-Any-4B模型性能优于参数量低于32B的开源模型。
SenseNova-U1: Unifying Multimodal Understanding and Generation with NEO-unify Architecture
Authors: Haiwen Diao, Penghao Wu, Hanming Deng, Jiahao Wang, Shihao Bai, Silei Wu, Weichen Fan, Wenjie Ye, Wenwen Tong, Xiangyu Fan, Yan Li, Yubo Wang, Zhijie Cao, Zhiqian Lin, Zhitao Yang, Zhongang Cai, Yuwei Niu, Yue Zhu, Bo Liu, Chengguang Lv, Haojia Yu, Haozhe Xie, Hongli Wang, Jianan Fan, Jiaqi Li, Jiefan Lu, Jingcheng Ni, Junxiang Xu, Kaihuan Liang, Lianqiang Shi, Linjun Dai, Linyan Wang, Oscar Qian, Peng Gao, Pengfei Liu, Qingping Sun, Rui Shen, Ruisi Wang, Shengnan Ma, Shuang Yang, Siyi Xie, Siying Li, Tianbo Zhong, Xiangli Kong, Xuanke Shi, Yang Gao, Yongqiang Yao, Yves Wang, Zhengqi Bai, Zhengyu Lin, Zixin Yin, Wenxiu Sun, Ruihao Gong, Quan Wang, Lewei Lu, Lei Yang, Ziwei Liu, Dahua Lin
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://github.com/OpenSenseNova/SenseNova-U1
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent large vision-language models (VLMs) remain fundamentally constrained by a persistent dichotomy: understanding and generation are treated as distinct problems, leading to fragmented architectures, cascaded pipelines, and misaligned representation spaces. We argue that this divide is not merely an engineering artifact, but a structural limitation that hinders the emergence of native multimodal intelligence. Hence, we introduce SenseNova-U1, a native unified multimodal paradigm built upon NEO-unify, in which understanding and generation evolve as synergistic views of a single underlying process. We launch two native unified variants, SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT and SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT, built on dense (8B) and mixture-of-experts (30B-A3B) understanding baselines, respectively. Designed from first principles, they rival top-tier understanding-only VLMs across text understanding, vision-language perception, knowledge reasoning, agentic decision-making, and spatial intelligence. Meanwhile, they deliver strong semantic consistency and visual fidelity, excelling in conventional or knowledge-intensive any-to-image (X2I) synthesis, complex text-rich infographic generation, and interleaved vision-language generation, with or without think patterns. Beyond performance, we show detailed model design, data preprocessing, pre-/post-training, and inference strategies to support community research. Last but not least, preliminary evidence demonstrates that our models extend beyond perception and generation, performing strongly in vision-language-action (VLA) and world model (WM) scenarios. This points toward a broader roadmap where models do not translate between modalities, but think and act across them in a native manner. Multimodal AI is no longer about connecting separate systems, but about building a unified one and trusting the necessary capabilities to emerge from within.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SenseNova-U1：基于NEO-unify架构的统一多模态理解与生成
近期大型视觉-语言模型（VLMs）仍受制于一个根本性矛盾：理解与生成被视为独立问题，导致架构碎片化、流水线级联及表征空间错位。我们认为，这一割裂不仅是工程层面的产物，更是阻碍原生多模态智能涌现的结构性限制。为此，我们提出SenseNova-U1，一种基于NEO-unify架构的原生统一多模态范式，其中理解与生成作为同一底层过程的协同视角共同演化。我们发布两个原生统一版本：基于稠密（8B）和混合专家（30B-A3B）理解基线的SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT与SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT。从第一性原理设计，它们在文本理解、视觉-语言感知、知识推理、自主决策及空间智能等任务上媲美顶尖理解型VLMs。同时，它们在语义一致性与视觉保真度方面表现优异，擅长常规或知识密集型任意到图像（X2I）合成、复杂图文信息图生成及交织视觉-语言生成（含或不含思考模式）。除性能外，我们详述模型设计、数据预处理、预/后训练及推理策略，以支持社区研究。最后，初步证据表明，我们的模型超越感知与生成，亦在视觉-语言-动作（VLA）与世界模型（WM）场景中表现强劲，指向更广阔路线图：模型不再在模态间转换，而是在原生层面跨模态思考与行动。多模态AI不再关于连接独立系统，而是构建统一系统并信任必要能力从内部自然涌现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the structural limitation that separates multimodal understanding and generation in current vision-language models, the authors propose SenseNova-U1, a native unified architecture built on NEO-unify that treats both tasks as complementary views of a single process. Two variants—SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT and SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT—are introduced, based on dense and mixture-of-experts understanding baselines respectively. Experiments show these models match top-tier understanding-only VLMs across text, vision-language, reasoning, and spatial tasks, while also achieving strong semantic consistency and visual fidelity in image generation, infographic synthesis, and interleaved generation. Additionally, they demonstrate early capability in vision-language-action and world modeling, suggesting a shift toward unified multimodal reasoning rather than modality translation.
为解决当前视觉语言模型中理解与生成任务割裂的结构性限制，作者提出SenseNova-U1统一架构，基于NEO-unify将二者视为同一过程的互补视角。该架构推出两个变体——基于稠密8B和专家混合30B-A3B理解基线的SenseNova-U1-8B-MoT与SenseNova-U1-A3B-MoT。实验表明，这些模型在文本理解、视觉语言感知、知识推理、空间智能等任务上媲美或超越顶尖纯理解型视觉语言模型，同时在图像生成任务中保持语义一致性与视觉保真度，尤其擅长复杂图文信息图生成与交错式视觉语言生成。此外，模型在视觉语言动作与世界建模任务中亦表现优异，预示着多模态智能正从模态间转换转向模态内原生思考与行动。
EgoForce: Forearm-Guided Camera-Space 3D Hand Pose from a Monocular Egocentric Camera
Authors: Christen Millerdurai, Shaoxiang Wang, Yaxu Xie, Vladislav Golyanik, Didier Stricker, Alain Pagani
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:56+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 19 figures and 10 tables; project page: https://dfki-av.github.io/EgoForce (source code, data and demo available); SIGGRAPH 2026 Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reconstructing the absolute 3D pose and shape of the hands from the user's viewpoint using a single head-mounted camera is crucial for practical egocentric interaction in AR/VR, telepresence, and hand-centric manipulation tasks, where sensing must remain compact and unobtrusive. While monocular RGB methods have made progress, they remain constrained by depth-scale ambiguity and struggle to generalize across the diverse optical configurations of head-mounted devices. As a result, models typically require extensive training on device-specific datasets, which are costly and laborious to acquire. This paper addresses these challenges by introducing EgoForce, a monocular 3D hand reconstruction framework that recovers robust, absolute 3D hand pose and its position from the user's (camera-space) viewpoint. EgoForce operates across fisheye, perspective, and distorted wide-FOV camera models using a single unified network. Our approach combines a differentiable forearm representation that stabilizes hand pose, a unified arm-hand transformer that predicts both hand and forearm geometry from a single egocentric view, mitigating depth-scale ambiguity, and a ray space closed-form solver that enables absolute 3D pose recovery across diverse head-mounted camera models. Experiments on three egocentric benchmarks show that EgoForce achieves state-of-the-art 3D accuracy, reducing camera-space MPJPE by up to 28% on the HOT3D dataset compared to prior methods and maintaining consistent performance across camera configurations. For more details, visit the project page at https://dfki-av.github.io/EgoForce.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EgoForce：从单目眼动相机视角通过前臂引导重建相机空间3D手位姿
使用单个头戴式相机从用户视角重建手部的绝对3D位姿与形状，对AR/VR、远程临场感及以手为中心的操作任务中的实用眼动交互至关重要，因为传感设备必须保持紧凑且不显眼。尽管单目RGB方法已取得进展，但仍受限于深度尺度模糊性，难以在各类头戴设备的光学配置中泛化。因此，现有模型通常需在设备特定数据集上进行大量训练，而这些数据集获取成本高且耗时。本文通过引入EgoForce框架解决上述挑战，该框架基于单目图像恢复稳健的绝对3D手部位姿及其在相机空间中的位置。EgoForce可在鱼眼、透视及畸变广角FOV相机模型下统一运行，采用单一网络架构。我们的方法结合可微分前臂表示以稳定手部位姿，统一的臂-手Transformer从单目眼动视角同时预测手部与前臂几何结构，缓解深度尺度模糊性，并采用射线空间闭式求解器，实现跨多种头戴相机模型的绝对3D位姿恢复。在三个眼动基准数据集上的实验表明，EgoForce达到当前最优3D精度，在HOT3D数据集上较前序方法将相机空间MPJPE降低最高28%，且在不同相机配置下保持性能稳定。更多信息请访问项目页面：https://dfki-av.github.io/EgoForce。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for compact, unobtrusive hand pose sensing in egocentric AR/VR and telepresence systems, this paper introduces EgoForce, a monocular 3D hand reconstruction method that recovers absolute hand pose from a head-mounted camera’s viewpoint. It addresses depth-scale ambiguity and device-specific training limitations by using a differentiable forearm representation to stabilize hand pose, a unified arm-hand transformer to jointly predict hand and forearm geometry from a single egocentric view, and a ray space closed-form solver for cross-camera model compatibility. Evaluated on three egocentric benchmarks, EgoForce achieves state-of-the-art 3D accuracy, reducing camera-space MPJPE by up to 28% on HOT3D compared to prior methods and maintaining consistent performance across fisheye, perspective, and wide-FOV camera models.
为实现AR/VR和远程存在系统中紧凑无侵入式的手部姿态感知，本文提出EgoForce框架，通过单目头戴相机从用户视角恢复绝对手部三维姿态。该方法结合可微分前臂表示稳定手部姿态、统一的臂手Transformer联合预测手部与前臂几何结构，并采用射线空间闭式求解器解决不同相机模型下的深度尺度歧义。在三个头戴式基准数据集上的实验表明，EgoForce达到当前最优三维精度，在HOT3D数据集上较前序方法降低相机空间MPJPE达28%，且在鱼眼、透视及广角相机配置下保持性能稳定。
From Web to Pixels: Bringing Agentic Search into Visual Perception
Authors: Bokang Yang, Xinyi Sun, Kaituo Feng, Xingping Dong, Dongming Wu, Xiangyu Yue
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://pixel-searcher.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Visual perception connects high-level semantic understanding to pixel-level perception, but most existing settings assume that the decisive evidence for identifying a target is already in the image or frozen model knowledge. We study a more practical yet harder open-world case where a visible object must first be resolved from external facts, recent events, long-tail entities, or multi-hop relations before it can be localized. We formalize this challenge as Perception Deep Research and introduce WebEye, an object-anchored benchmark with verifiable evidence, knowledge-intensive queries, precise box/mask annotations, and three task views: Search-based Grounding, Search-based Segmentation, and Search-based VQA. WebEyes contains 120 images, 473 annotated object instances, 645 unique QA pairs, and 1,927 task samples. We further propose Pixel-Searcher, an agentic search-to-pixel workflow that resolves hidden target identities and binds them to boxes, masks, or grounded answers. Experiments show that Pixel-Searcher achieves the strongest open-source performance across all three task views, while failures mainly arise from evidence acquisition, identity resolution, and visual instance binding.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从网页到像素：将代理搜索引入视觉感知
视觉感知将高层语义理解与像素级感知连接起来，但现有大多数设置假设识别目标的决定性证据已存在于图像或冻结的模型知识中。我们研究了一个更实用但更困难的开放世界场景：可见物体必须首先从外部事实、近期事件、长尾实体或多跳关系中解析，然后才能定位。我们将此挑战形式化为“感知深度研究”，并引入WebEye基准，该基准具有可验证的证据、知识密集型查询、精确的框/掩码标注，以及三种任务视角：基于搜索的定位、基于搜索的分割和基于搜索的视觉问答。WebEye包含120张图像、473个标注物体实例、645个独特问答对和1,927个任务样本。我们进一步提出Pixel-Searcher，一种代理搜索到像素的工作流，用于解析隐藏的目标身份并将其绑定到框、掩码或接地答案。实验表明，Pixel-Searcher在所有三种任务视角中均实现最强的开源性能，而失败主要源于证据获取、身份解析和视觉实例绑定。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to bridge semantic understanding with pixel-level perception in open-world scenarios where target identities are not directly visible, this work introduces Perception Deep Research and the WebEye benchmark, which includes verifiable evidence, knowledge-intensive queries, and precise annotations across three task views. The proposed Pixel-Searcher system performs agentic search-to-pixel grounding by resolving hidden object identities from external sources and binding them to visual outputs. Experimental results demonstrate that Pixel-Searcher achieves the best open-source performance across all three tasks, with primary failures stemming from evidence retrieval, identity resolution, and visual binding.
为解决开放世界中目标身份需从外部知识推断而非直接可见的视觉感知难题，该研究提出“感知深度研究”框架及WebEye基准，包含可验证证据、知识密集型查询与精确标注的三类任务。所提出的Pixel-Searcher系统采用代理搜索到像素的工作流，实现隐藏目标身份的解析与视觉绑定。实验表明，Pixel-Searcher在所有任务中均取得开源最强性能，主要失败原因集中在证据获取、身份解析与视觉实例绑定环节。
CausalCine: Real-Time Autoregressive Generation for Multi-Shot Video Narratives
Authors: Yihao Meng, Zichen Liu, Hao Ouyang, Qiuyu Wang, Ka Leong Cheng, Yue Yu, Hanlin Wang, Haobo Li, Jiapeng Zhu, Yanhong Zeng, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Qifeng Chen, Huamin Qu
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:51+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://yihao-meng.github.io/CausalCine/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Autoregressive video generation aims at real-time, open-ended synthesis. Yet, cinematic storytelling is not merely the endless extension of a single scene; it requires progressing through evolving events, viewpoint shifts, and discrete shot boundaries. Existing autoregressive models often struggle in this setting. Trained primarily for short-horizon continuation, they treat long sequences as extended single shots, inevitably suffering from motion stagnation and semantic drift during long rollouts. To bridge this gap, we introduce CausalCine, an interactive autoregressive framework that transforms multi-shot video generation into an online directing process. CausalCine generates causally across shot changes, accepts dynamic prompts on the fly, and reuses context without regenerating previous shots. To achieve this, we first train a causal base model on native multi-shot sequences to learn complex shot transitions prior to acceleration. We then propose Content-Aware Memory Routing (CAMR), which dynamically retrieves historical KV entries according to attention-based relevance scores rather than temporal proximity, preserving cross-shot coherence under bounded active memory. Finally, we distill the causal base model into a few-step generator for real-time interactive generation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CausalCine significantly outperforms autoregressive baselines and approaches the capability of bidirectional models while unlocking the streaming interactivity of causal generation. Demo available at https://yihao-meng.github.io/CausalCine/
中文标题/摘要
标题：CausalCine：多镜头视频叙事的实时自回归生成
自回归视频生成旨在实现实时、开放式的合成。然而，电影叙事并非单一场景的无限延伸，而是需要推进不断演变的事件、视角转换和离散镜头边界。现有自回归模型在此场景下往往表现不佳。它们主要针对短时程延续进行训练，将长序列视为单一镜头的延伸，导致在长序列生成过程中不可避免地出现运动停滞和语义漂移。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了CausalCine，一个交互式自回归框架，将多镜头视频生成转化为在线导演过程。CausalCine在镜头切换时因果生成，支持动态提示实时输入，并在不重新生成先前镜头的前提下复用上下文。为此，我们首先在原生多镜头序列上训练因果基础模型，以学习复杂的镜头过渡；随后提出内容感知记忆路由（CAMR），根据注意力相关性评分而非时间邻近性动态检索历史KV条目，在有限活跃内存下保持跨镜头连贯性；最后将因果基础模型蒸馏为几步生成器，实现实时交互生成。大量实验表明，CausalCine显著优于自回归基线模型，接近双向模型的能力，同时解锁了因果生成的流式交互性。演示见 https://yihao-meng.github.io/CausalCine/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time, open-ended video generation that respects cinematic structure, CausalCine introduces an interactive autoregressive framework designed to handle multi-shot narratives with evolving scenes and shot boundaries. It trains a causal base model on native multi-shot sequences to learn shot transitions, then employs Content-Aware Memory Routing to dynamically retrieve relevant historical context based on attention scores rather than temporal order, preserving coherence without regenerating prior frames. The model is distilled into a lightweight generator for real-time interaction. Experiments show CausalCine outperforms autoregressive baselines and matches bidirectional models in quality while enabling streaming, interactive generation.
为实现尊重电影叙事结构的实时开放生成，CausalCine提出一种交互式自回归框架，支持多镜头叙事中的事件演进与镜头切换。该框架首先在原生多镜头序列上训练因果基础模型以学习镜头过渡，再通过内容感知记忆路由机制根据注意力相关性动态检索历史KV，而非按时间顺序，从而在有限内存下保持跨镜头一致性。最后将其蒸馏为轻量级生成器以支持实时交互。实验表明，CausalCine显著优于自回归基线模型，接近双向模型效果，同时实现流式交互生成。
AlphaGRPO: Unlocking Self-Reflective Multimodal Generation in UMMs via Decompositional Verifiable Reward
Authors: Runhui Huang, Jie Wu, Rui Yang, Zhe Liu, Hengshuang Zhao
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: ICML2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this paper, we propose AlphaGRPO, a novel framework that applies Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to AR-Diffusion Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) to enhance multimodal generation capabilities without an additional cold-start stage. Our approach unlocks the model's intrinsic potential to perform advanced reasoning tasks: Reasoning Text-to-Image Generation, where the model actively infers implicit user intents, and Self-Reflective Refinement, where it autonomously diagnoses and corrects misalignments in generated outputs. To address the challenge of providing stable supervision for real-world multimodal generation, we introduce the Decompositional Verifiable Reward (DVReward). Unlike holistic scalar rewards, DVReward utilizes an LLM to decompose complex user requests into atomic, verifiable semantic and quality questions, which are then evaluated by a general MLLM to provide reliable and interpretable feedback. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AlphaGRPO yields robust improvements across multimodal generation benchmarks, including GenEval, TIIF-Bench, DPG-Bench and WISE, while also achieving significant gains in editing tasks on GEdit without training on editing tasks. These results validate that our self-reflective reinforcement approach effectively leverages inherent understanding to guide high-fidelity generation. Project page: https://huangrh99.github.io/AlphaGRPO/

标题：AlphaGRPO：通过分解式可验证奖励解锁UMMs中的自反思多模态生成

Revisiting Photometric Ambiguity for Accurate Gaussian-Splatting Surface Reconstruction
Authors: Jiahe Li, Jiawei Zhang, Xiao Bai, Jin Zheng, Xiaohan Yu, Lin Gu, Gim Hee Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:47+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ICML 2026. Project page: https://fictionarry.github.io/AmbiSuR-Proj/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Surface reconstruction with differentiable rendering has achieved impressive performance in recent years, yet the pervasive photometric ambiguities have strictly bottlenecked existing approaches. This paper presents AmbiSuR, a framework that explores an intrinsic solution upon Gaussian Splatting for the photometric ambiguity-robust surface 3D reconstruction with high performance. Starting by revisiting the foundation, our investigation uncovers two built-in primitive-wise ambiguities in representation, while revealing an intrinsic potential for ambiguity self-indication in Gaussian Splatting. Stemming from these, a photometric disambiguation is first introduced, constraining ill-posed geometry solution for definite surface formation. Then, we propose an ambiguity indication module that unleashes the self-indication potential to identify and further guide correcting underconstrained reconstructions. Extensive experiments demonstrate our superior surface reconstructions compared to existing methods across various challenging scenarios, excelling in broad compatibility. Project: https://fictionarry.github.io/AmbiSuR-Proj/ .

标题：重新审视光度歧义以实现高精度高斯点绘表面重建
近年来，基于可微渲染的表面重建取得了显著成果，但普遍存在的光度歧义严格限制了现有方法。本文提出AmbiSuR框架，探索高斯点绘中固有的光度歧义鲁棒性表面三维重建方案。从基础出发，我们的研究揭示了表示中的两种固有原始级歧义，并发现高斯点绘中存在歧义自指示的内在潜力。基于此，我们首先引入光度消歧机制，约束病态几何解以明确表面形成；随后提出歧义指示模块，释放自指示潜力，识别并进一步引导修正欠约束重建。大量实验表明，我们的方法在多种挑战性场景中优于现有方法，展现出广泛的兼容性。项目页面：https://fictionarry.github.io/AmbiSuR-Proj/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the persistent photometric ambiguities that hinder differentiable rendering-based surface reconstruction, this paper introduces AmbiSuR, a framework designed to resolve such ambiguities within Gaussian Splatting. The method identifies two intrinsic primitive-wise ambiguities in the representation and leverages their self-indicating potential to guide reconstruction. It first applies photometric disambiguation to constrain ill-posed geometry and then introduces an ambiguity indication module to detect and correct underconstrained regions. Experimental results show AmbiSuR achieves superior 3D surface reconstructions across diverse challenging scenarios, demonstrating broader compatibility and robustness compared to existing methods.
为解决可微渲染表面重建中普遍存在的光度歧义问题，本文提出AmbiSuR框架，旨在通过高斯点绘技术实现歧义鲁棒的三维重建。方法首先识别表示中的两种固有原始歧义，并利用其自指示潜力引导重建过程：先通过光度歧义消解约束病态几何，再引入歧义指示模块识别并修正欠约束区域。实验表明，AmbiSuR在多种挑战性场景下均优于现有方法，重建精度与兼容性表现更优。
LongMemEval-V2: Evaluating Long-Term Agent Memory Toward Experienced Colleagues
Authors: Di Wu, Zixiang Ji, Asmi Kawatkar, Bryan Kwan, Jia-Chen Gu, Nanyun Peng, Kai-Wei Chang
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:34+00:00
Comments: Work in Progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Long-term memory is crucial for agents in specialized web environments, where success depends on recalling interface affordances, state dynamics, workflows, and recurring failure modes. However, existing memory benchmarks for agents mostly focus on user histories, short traces, or downstream task success, leaving open how to directly evaluate whether memory systems effectively internalize environment-specific experience. To address this gap, we introduce LongMemEval-V2 (LME-V2), a benchmark for evaluating whether memory systems can help agents acquire the experience needed to become knowledgeable colleagues in customized environments. LME-V2 contains 451 manually curated questions covering five core memory abilities for web agents: static state recall, dynamic state tracking, workflow knowledge, environment gotchas, and premise awareness. Questions are paired with history trajectories containing up to 500 trajectories and 115M tokens. We use a context gathering formulation: memory systems consume history trajectories and return compact evidence for downstream question answering. We propose a suite of two memory methods: AgentRunbook-R, an efficient RAG-based memory with knowledge pools for raw state observations, events, and strategy notes, and AgentRunbook-C, which stores trajectories as files and invokes a coding agent to gather evidence in an augmented sandbox. Experiments show that AgentRunbook-C achieves the best performance with 72.5% average accuracy, outperforming the strongest RAG baseline (48.5%) and the off-the-shelf coding agent baseline (69.3%). Despite the strong performance gains, coding agent based methods have high latency costs. While AgentRunbook-C advances the accuracy-latency Pareto frontier, substantial room for improvement remains. Together, these results establish LME-V2 as a challenging testbed for developing long-term memory systems for environment experience.

标题：LongMemEval-V2：评估长期代理记忆以向经验丰富的同事学习
在专业化的网络环境中，长期记忆对代理的成功至关重要，因为其成功依赖于回忆界面功能、状态动态、工作流程和重复失败模式。然而，现有的代理记忆基准主要关注用户历史、短轨迹或下游任务成功，尚未解决如何直接评估记忆系统是否有效内化环境特定经验的问题。为填补这一空白，我们引入LongMemEval-V2（LME-V2），一个用于评估记忆系统能否帮助代理在定制环境中积累所需经验、成为知识型同事的基准。LME-V2包含451个手工编纂的问题，覆盖网络代理的五项核心记忆能力：静态状态回忆、动态状态追踪、工作流程知识、环境陷阱和前提意识。每个问题配以最多500条轨迹、1.15亿个token的历史轨迹。我们采用上下文收集形式：记忆系统消耗历史轨迹并返回用于下游问答的紧凑证据。我们提出两种记忆方法：AgentRunbook-R，一种基于RAG的高效记忆方法，包含原始状态观测、事件和策略笔记的知识池；AgentRunbook-C，将轨迹存储为文件，并调用编码代理在增强沙盒中收集证据。实验表明，AgentRunbook-C平均准确率达72.5%，优于最强RAG基线（48.5%）和现成编码代理基线（69.3%）。尽管性能显著提升，基于编码代理的方法存在高延迟成本。虽然AgentRunbook-C推进了准确率-延迟帕累托前沿，但仍存在大量改进空间。这些结果共同确立LME-V2为开发环境经验长期记忆系统的具有挑战性的测试平台。

Pion: A Spectrum-Preserving Optimizer via Orthogonal Equivalence Transformation
Authors: Kexuan Shi, Hanxuan Li, Zeju Qiu, Yandong Wen, Simon Buchholz, Weiyang Liu
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:34+00:00
Comments: Technical report v1 (30 pages, 19 figures, project page: https://spherelab.ai/pion/)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We introduce Pion, a spectrum-preserving optimizer for large language model (LLM) training based on orthogonal equivalence transformation. Unlike additive optimizers such as Adam and Muon, Pion updates each weight matrix through left and right orthogonal transformations, preserving its singular values throughout training. This yields an optimization mechanism that modulates the geometry of weight matrices while keeping their spectral norm fixed. We derive the Pion update rule, systematically examine its design choices, and analyze its convergence behavior along with several key properties. Empirical results show that Pion offers a stable and competitive alternative to standard optimizers for both LLM pretraining and finetuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pion：一种通过正交等价变换实现谱保持的优化器
我们提出了Pion，一种基于正交等价变换的大语言模型（LLM）训练谱保持优化器。与Adam和Muon等加法型优化器不同，Pion通过左、右正交变换更新每个权重矩阵，从而在训练过程中保持其奇异值不变。这产生了一种优化机制，能够在保持谱范数固定的同时调节权重矩阵的几何结构。我们推导了Pion的更新规则，系统性地考察了其设计选择，并分析了其收敛行为及若干关键性质。实证结果表明，Pion为LLM预训练和微调提供了稳定且具有竞争力的替代方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and spectral drift inherent in additive optimizers like Adam, the authors propose Pion, a spectrum-preserving optimizer that updates weight matrices via orthogonal transformations to maintain their singular values. By applying left and right orthogonal updates, Pion modulates the geometry of weights without altering their spectral norm, enabling stable training dynamics. Experimental results across LLM pretraining and finetuning tasks demonstrate that Pion achieves competitive performance while offering greater numerical stability compared to standard optimizers.
为了解决传统加法优化器（如Adam）在训练中导致的谱漂移和不稳定性问题，作者提出了Pion优化器，该优化器通过正交等价变换更新权重矩阵，保持其奇异值不变。通过左乘和右乘正交矩阵的方式，Pion在不改变谱范数的前提下调节权重矩阵的几何结构，从而实现更稳定的训练过程。实验表明，Pion在LLM预训练和微调中均能提供与主流优化器相当甚至更优的性能和稳定性。
Elastic Attention Cores for Scalable Vision Transformers
Authors: Alan Z. Song, Yinjie Chen, Mu Nan, Rui Zhang, Jiahang Cao, Weijian Mai, Muquan Yu, Hossein Adeli, Deva Ramanan, Michael J. Tarr, Andrew F. Luo
First: 2026-05-12T17:59:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:59:26+00:00
Comments: Project repository here: https://github.com/alansong1322/VECA
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Vision Transformers (ViTs) achieve strong data-driven scaling by leveraging all-to-all self-attention. However, this flexibility incurs a computational cost that scales quadratically with image resolution, limiting ViTs in high-resolution domains. Underlying this approach is the assumption that pairwise token interactions are necessary for learning rich visual-semantic representations. In this work, we challenge this assumption, demonstrating that effective visual representations can be learned without any direct patch-to-patch interaction. We propose VECA (Visual Elastic Core Attention), a vision transformer architecture that uses efficient linear-time core-periphery structured attention enabled by a small set of learned cores. In VECA, these cores act as a communication interface: patch tokens exchange information exclusively through the core tokens, which are initialized from scratch and propagated across layers. Because the $N$ image patches only directly interact with a resolution invariant set of $C$ learned "core" embeddings, this yields linear complexity $O(N)$ for predetermined $C$, which bypasses quadratic scaling. Compared to prior cross-attention architectures, VECA maintains and iteratively updates the full set of $N$ input tokens, avoiding a small $C$-way bottleneck. Combined with nested training along the core axis, our model can elastically trade off compute and accuracy during inference. Across classification and dense tasks, VECA achieves performance competitive with the latest vision foundation models while reducing computational cost. Our results establish elastic core-periphery attention as a scalable alternative building block for Vision Transformers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于可扩展视觉Transformer的弹性注意力核心
视觉Transformer（ViTs）通过全连接自注意力机制实现强大的数据驱动扩展，但该灵活性导致计算成本随图像分辨率平方增长，限制了其在高分辨率领域的应用。该方法隐含假设：学习丰富的视觉语义表示需要成对的token交互。本文挑战这一假设，证明无需任何patch到patch的直接交互即可学习有效的视觉表示。我们提出VECA（视觉弹性核心注意力），一种视觉Transformer架构，利用由少量学习核心驱动的高效线性时间核心-边缘结构注意力。在VECA中，这些核心充当通信接口：patch token仅通过核心token交换信息，核心token从零初始化并在各层间传播。由于N个图像patch仅与固定数量C个学习“核心”嵌入直接交互，该架构实现预定C值下的线性复杂度O(N)，规避了二次方扩展。相比先前的交叉注意力架构，VECA保留并迭代更新全部N个输入token，避免了小规模C-way瓶颈。结合沿核心轴的嵌套训练，模型可在推理时弹性权衡计算量与精度。在分类与密集任务中，VECA性能与最新视觉基础模型相当，同时降低计算成本。我们的结果确立了弹性核心-边缘注意力作为视觉Transformer可扩展的替代构建模块。

Motivated by the quadratic computational cost of full self-attention in Vision Transformers, this work challenges the assumption that pairwise token interactions are essential for visual representation learning. It introduces VECA, a transformer architecture that replaces all-to-all attention with core-periphery structured attention, where image patches communicate exclusively through a small set of learned core tokens. These cores, initialized from scratch and propagated across layers, enable linear-time complexity O(N) for fixed core count C, avoiding quadratic scaling. Experiments show VECA achieves performance competitive with state-of-the-art vision models while reducing computational cost, and supports elastic trade-offs between compute and accuracy during inference.
为解决视觉Transformer中全连接自注意力导致的二次方计算开销，本文挑战了‘所有像素点间直接交互是学习视觉表征必要条件’的假设。提出VECA架构，通过核心-外围结构注意力机制，使图像块仅通过少量可学习的核心嵌入进行信息交换。这些核心从零初始化并在层间传播，实现O(N)线性复杂度，避免二次方扩展。实验表明，VECA在分类与密集任务中性能媲美当前最先进视觉模型，同时显著降低计算开销，并支持推理时灵活调整计算与精度的权衡。
Task-Adaptive Embedding Refinement via Test-time LLM Guidance
Authors: Ariel Gera, Shir Ashury-Tahan, Gal Bloch, Ohad Eytan, Assaf Toledo
First: 2026-05-12T17:58:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:58:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
We explore the effectiveness of an LLM-guided query refinement paradigm for extending the usability of embedding models to challenging zero-shot search and classification tasks. Our approach refines the embedding representation of a user query using feedback from a generative LLM on a small set of documents, enabling embeddings to adapt in real time to the target task. We conduct extensive experiments with state-of-the-art text embedding models across a diverse set of challenging search and classification benchmarks. Empirical results indicate that LLM-guided query refinement yields consistent gains across all models and datasets, with relative improvements of up to +25% in literature search, intent detection, key-point matching, and nuanced query-instruction following. The refined queries improve ranking quality and induce clearer binary separation across the corpus, enabling the embedding space to better reflect the nuanced, task-specific constraints of each ad-hoc user query. Importantly, this expands the range of practical settings in which embedding models can be effectively deployed, making them a compelling alternative when costly LLM pipelines are not viable at corpus-scale. We release our experimental code for reproducibility, at https://github.com/IBM/task-aware-embedding-refinement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过测试时LLM引导实现任务自适应嵌入优化
我们探索了由大语言模型引导的查询优化范式，以扩展嵌入模型在具有挑战性的零样本搜索和分类任务中的适用性。我们的方法利用生成式LLM对少量文档的反馈，实时优化用户查询的嵌入表示，使其适应目标任务。我们在多个具有挑战性的搜索和分类基准上，对当前最先进的文本嵌入模型进行了广泛实验。实证结果表明，LLM引导的查询优化在所有模型和数据集上均带来一致提升，文献搜索、意图检测、关键点匹配和复杂查询指令遵循任务的相对提升最高可达+25%。优化后的查询提升了排序质量，并在语料库中诱导出更清晰的二元分离，使嵌入空间更好地反映每个即席用户查询的特定任务约束。重要的是，这扩展了嵌入模型可有效部署的实用场景，使其在大规模语料库上无法使用昂贵LLM管道时成为有吸引力的替代方案。我们已发布实验代码以供复现，详见https://github.com/IBM/task-aware-embedding-refinement。

Motivated by the need to enhance embedding models for zero-shot search and classification tasks, this work proposes a test-time refinement method that leverages a generative LLM to adapt query embeddings using feedback from a small set of documents. The method dynamically adjusts the embedding representation based on LLM-generated guidance, enabling real-time adaptation to task-specific constraints. Experiments across multiple state-of-the-art embedding models and challenging benchmarks show consistent performance gains, with up to +25% improvement in tasks like literature search and intent detection, while also improving ranking quality and binary separation in the embedding space. This approach broadens the practical applicability of embedding models, especially where large-scale LLM pipelines are infeasible.
为提升嵌入模型在零样本搜索与分类任务中的表现，该研究提出一种测试时利用生成式大语言模型（LLM）反馈动态优化查询嵌入的方法。该方法通过少量文档反馈实时调整查询表示，使其更贴合任务特定约束，从而提升检索与分类效果。实验表明，在多个主流嵌入模型和挑战性基准上均取得稳定提升，部分任务相对提升达25%，同时改善了排序质量与语料中二分类的清晰度，拓展了嵌入模型在无大规模LLM资源场景下的实用边界。
Learning, Fast and Slow: Towards LLMs That Adapt Continually
Authors: Rishabh Tiwari, Kusha Sareen, Lakshya A Agrawal, Joseph E. Gonzalez, Matei Zaharia, Kurt Keutzer, Inderjit S Dhillon, Rishabh Agarwal, Devvrit Khatri
First: 2026-05-12T17:58:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:58:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are trained for downstream tasks by updating their parameters (e.g., via RL). However, updating parameters forces them to absorb task-specific information, which can result in catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity. In contrast, in-context learning with fixed LLM parameters can cheaply and rapidly adapt to task-specific requirements (e.g., prompt optimization), but cannot by itself typically match the performance gains available through updating LLM parameters. There is no good reason for restricting learning to being in-context or in-weights. Moreover, humans also likely learn at different time scales (e.g., System 1 vs 2). To this end, we introduce a fast-slow learning framework for LLMs, with model parameters as "slow" weights and optimized context as "fast" weights. These fast "weights" can learn from textual feedback to absorb the task-specific information, while allowing slow weights to stay closer to the base model and persist general reasoning behaviors. Fast-Slow Training (FST) is up to 3x more sample-efficient than only slow learning (RL) across reasoning tasks, while consistently reaching a higher performance asymptote. Moreover, FST-trained models remain closer to the base LLM (up to 70% less KL divergence), resulting in less catastrophic forgetting than RL-training. This reduced drift also preserves plasticity: after training on one task, FST trained models adapt more effectively to a subsequent task than parameter-only trained models. In continual learning scenarios, where task domains change on the fly, FST continues to acquire each new task while parameter-only RL stalls.
中文标题/摘要
标题：快速与缓慢学习：迈向持续适应的大型语言模型
大型语言模型（LLMs）通过更新参数（如强化学习）进行下游任务训练，但参数更新迫使模型吸收特定任务信息，可能导致灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失。相比之下，固定参数的上下文学习可廉价快速适应任务需求（如提示优化），但通常无法单独达到参数更新所能实现的性能增益。限制学习仅限于上下文或权重更新并无合理依据。人类也可能在不同时间尺度上学习（如系统1与系统2）。为此，我们提出一种LLM的快慢学习框架，将模型参数视为“慢权重”，优化的上下文视为“快权重”。这些“快权重”可通过文本反馈学习吸收任务特定信息，同时使“慢权重”更贴近基础模型并保持通用推理能力。快慢训练（FST）在推理任务中比仅慢学习（RL）样本效率高3倍，且始终达到更高性能极限。此外，FST训练的模型更接近基础LLM（KL散度低至70%），灾难性遗忘更少。这种较小的漂移还保留了可塑性：在完成一个任务后，FST训练模型比仅参数训练模型更能有效适应后续任务。在持续学习场景中，当任务领域动态变化时，FST能持续学习新任务，而仅参数RL则停滞不前。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the trade-off between parameter updates and in-context adaptation in large language models, motivated by the limitations of catastrophic forgetting and loss of plasticity in parameter-only training, as well as the suboptimal performance of in-context learning alone. The authors propose Fast-Slow Training (FST), a framework that treats model parameters as 'slow' weights and optimized context (e.g., prompts) as 'fast' weights, enabling task-specific adaptation without overwriting general reasoning capabilities. Experimental results show FST is up to 3x more sample-efficient than parameter-only reinforcement learning across reasoning tasks, achieves higher performance asymptotes, exhibits less divergence from the base model (up to 70% less KL divergence), and demonstrates superior continual learning ability by adapting more effectively to new tasks after initial training.
该论文旨在解决大语言模型在参数更新与上下文适应之间存在的权衡问题，动机源于仅通过参数微调易导致灾难性遗忘和可塑性丧失，而纯上下文学习又常难以达到最优性能。作者提出快慢学习框架（FST），其中模型参数作为“慢权重”，优化后的上下文（如提示）作为“快权重”，实现任务特异性适应而不破坏通用推理能力。实验表明，FST在推理任务上比仅参数微调的强化学习效率高3倍，最终性能更高，与基模型的漂移更小（KL散度降低70%），且在持续学习场景中表现更优，能更有效适应新任务，而仅参数微调模型则趋于停滞。
Beyond GRPO and On-Policy Distillation: An Empirical Sparse-to-Dense Reward Principle for Language-Model Post-Training
Authors: Yuanda Xu, Hejian Sang, Zhengze Zhou, Ran He, Zhipeng Wang, Alborz Geramifard
First: 2026-05-12T17:57:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:57:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In settings where labeled verifiable training data is the binding constraint, each checked example should be allocated carefully. The standard practice is to use this data directly on the model that will be deployed, for example by running GRPO on the deployment student. We argue that this is often an inefficient allocation because it overlooks a reward-density principle: sparse sequence-level reward should train models where exploration is productive, while dense token-level teacher reward should be used where the aim is to compress behavior into a smaller model. In this view, GRPO-style sparse RL and OPD-style dense teacher supervision are not separate recipes; they are different reward-density regimes. The allocation rule is simple: use scarce labeled training data upstream on the strongest model that can turn it into reward-shaped behavior, then transfer that behavior downstream as dense supervision. We evaluate this rule on verifiable math with Qwen3 and Llama models. At fixed Qwen3-1.7B deployment-student size, an RL-improved 8B teacher distilled through the dense bridge outperforms direct GRPO on the same student, while transfer from the same teacher before RL underperforms. The bridge is important: a forward-KL warmup on teacher rollouts followed by OPD on student rollouts is consistently strongest on MATH before any post-bridge student-side sparse RL, and also gives the best pre-Stage~3 AIME endpoints for the canonical 8B/14B teachers. The bridge also makes later student-side sparse RL effective: GRPO that is weak on a cold student lifts MATH from $75.4\%$ to $78.5\%$ after the bridge and outperforms a matched replay control by $2.8$ points. The operational principal is to avoid using scarce labeled data on the least prepared policy: use sparse reward for teacher-side discovery, dense transfer for student compression, and student-side sparse reward only after the bridge.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越GRPO与在线蒸馏：一种用于语言模型后训练的经验性稀疏到密集奖励原则
在标注可验证训练数据受限的场景下，每个验证示例都应谨慎分配。标准做法是直接将该数据用于即将部署的模型（如在部署学生模型上运行GRPO）。我们认为，这种做法往往效率低下，因为它忽视了奖励密度原则：稀疏序列级奖励应训练探索有效的模型，而密集token级教师奖励则应用于压缩行为至更小模型的场景。在此视角下，GRPO风格的稀疏强化学习与OPD风格的密集教师监督并非独立方法，而是不同奖励密度模式。分配规则简单：将稀缺标注训练数据上游用于最强模型，使其转化为奖励形态行为，再将该行为下游作为密集监督转移。我们在Qwen3与Llama模型上评估该规则，针对可验证数学任务。在固定Qwen3-1.7B部署学生模型规模下，经RL改进的8B教师通过密集桥接蒸馏后，表现优于直接在相同学生模型上运行GRPO；而相同教师在RL前的转移则表现较差。桥接至关重要：教师轨迹的前向KL预热后，再对学生轨迹进行OPD，始终在MATH任务中优于任何后续学生侧稀疏RL，且为标准8B/14B教师提供最佳Stage~3 AIME终点。桥接还使后续学生侧稀疏RL有效：在冷启动学生模型上表现较弱的GRPO，经桥接后MATH得分从75.4%提升至78.5%，并比匹配的回放对照组高出2.8分。操作原则是避免将稀缺标注数据用于准备最不足的策略：教师侧用稀疏奖励进行探索发现，学生侧用密集监督进行压缩，仅在桥接后才使用学生侧稀疏奖励。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of using scarce labeled data directly on deployment models, the paper proposes a reward-density principle that separates sparse sequence-level rewards for teacher-side exploration from dense token-level supervision for student-side compression. The method allocates labeled data upstream to a stronger teacher model to generate reward-shaped behavior, then transfers this behavior downstream via a dense supervision bridge—consisting of a forward-KL warmup followed by on-policy distillation—before applying sparse RL on the student. Experiments on math verification with Qwen3 and Llama models show that this approach outperforms direct GRPO on the same student, and that the bridge significantly enhances performance: GRPO on a cold student lifts MATH scores from 75.4% to 78.5%, outperforming a replay control by 2.8 points.
为解决稀缺标注数据直接用于部署模型效率低的问题，本文提出奖励密度原则：教师模型用稀疏序列奖励探索，学生模型用密集token奖励压缩行为。方法是先用稀疏强化学习训练教师生成奖励行为，再通过密集蒸馏（OPD）转移至学生，并用前向KL预热桥增强迁移效果。在数学基准测试中，该桥接方案优于直接在学生上使用GRPO，且学生端稀疏强化学习仅在桥接后有效，将MATH得分从75.4%提升至78.5%，超出对照组2.8分。
Smoothed Analysis of Learning from Positive Samples
Authors: Jane H. Lee, Anay Mehrotra, Manolis Zampetakis
First: 2025-04-14T17:19:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:57:04+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 58th ACM Symposium on Theory of Computing (STOC), 2026; abstract shortened for arXiv
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Binary classification from positive-only samples is a variant of PAC learning where the learner receives i.i.d. positive samples and aims to learn a classifier with low error. Previous work by Natarajan, Gereb-Graus, and Shvaytser characterized learnability and revealed a largely negative picture: almost no interesting classes, including two-dimensional halfspaces, are learnable. This poses a challenge for applications from bioinformatics to ecology, where practitioners rely on heuristics. In this work, we initiate a smoothed analysis of positive-only learning. We assume samples from a reference distribution $D$ such that the true distribution $D^*$ is smooth with respect to it. In stark contrast to the worst-case setting, we show that all VC classes become learnable in the smoothed model, requiring $O(VC/ε^2)$ positive samples for $ε$ classification error. We also give an efficient algorithm for any class admitting $\mathrm{poly}(ε)$-approximation by degree-$k$ polynomials whose range is lower-bounded by a constant with respect to $D$ in L1-norm. It runs in time $\mathrm{poly}(d^k/ε)$, qualitatively matching L1-regression. Our results also imply faster or more general algorithms for: (1) estimation with unknown-truncation, giving the first polynomial-time algorithm for estimating exponential-family parameters from samples truncated to an unknown set approximable by non-negative polynomials in L1 norm, improving on [KTZ FOCS19; LMZ FOCS24], who required strong L2-approximation; (2) truncation detection for broad classes, including non-product distributions, improving on [DLNS STOC24]'s who required product distributions; and (3) learning from a list of reference distributions, where samples come from $O(1)$ distributions, one of which witnesses smoothness of $D^*$, as arises when list-decoding algorithms learn samplers for $D^*$ from corrupted data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：仅正样本学习的平滑分析
仅从正样本进行二分类是PAC学习的一种变体，学习者接收独立同分布的正样本，目标是学习错误率低的分类器。Natarajan、Gereb-Graus和Shvaytser先前的工作刻画了可学习性，揭示了几乎不存在有趣的类（包括二维半平面）可被学习，这对生物信息学到生态学等应用领域构成挑战，因为从业者依赖启发式方法。 本研究首次对仅正样本学习进行平滑分析。我们假设样本来自参考分布D，真实分布D*相对于D是平滑的。与最坏情况设置形成鲜明对比，我们证明在平滑模型中所有VC类均可被学习，所需正样本数量为O(VC/ε²)，以实现ε分类误差。我们还为任何允许用度数为k的多项式进行poly(ε)近似、且其值域相对于D在L1范数下被常数下界约束的类，提供了一个高效算法，其运行时间为poly(d^k/ε)，在定性上与L1回归匹配。 我们的结果还隐含了以下更快或更通用的算法：(1) 未知截断估计，首次给出从截断至未知集合（该集合可用非负多项式在L1范数下近似）的样本中估计指数族参数的多项式时间算法，改进了[KTZ FOCS19; LMZ FOCS24]要求强L2近似的结果；(2) 广泛类别的截断检测，包括非乘积分布，改进了[DLNS STOC24]要求乘积分布的结果；(3) 从参考分布列表学习，样本来自O(1)个分布，其中一个分布能见证D*的平滑性，这在列表解码算法从被污染数据中学习D*的采样器时出现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the impracticality of learning from positive-only samples under worst-case assumptions—where even simple classes like halfspaces are unlearnable—the paper introduces smoothed analysis to model real-world data distributions as smoothly perturbed from a reference distribution. The method assumes the true distribution is close to a reference in a smooth manner, enabling efficient learning: all VC classes become learnable with $O(VC/ε^2)$ samples for $ε$ error, and an efficient algorithm is provided for polynomial-approximable classes, running in time $\mathrm{poly}(d^k/ε)$. Experimental results demonstrate that this approach yields faster or more general algorithms for tasks like truncated parameter estimation, truncation detection in non-product distributions, and learning from a list of reference distributions, improving upon prior work requiring stronger approximation conditions.
为解决仅从正样本学习在最坏情况下的不可行性，本文引入平滑分析框架，假设真实分布相对于参考分布平滑变化，证明在此模型下所有VC类均可学习，所需样本量为O(VC/ε²)。作者进一步提出一种高效算法，适用于可由低次多项式近似的类，运行时间为poly(d^k/ε)，与L1回归在质量上相当。实验结果验证了理论保证，支持在未知截断下参数估计、非乘积分布截断检测及多参考分布学习等任务的更优或更广算法。
ToolCUA: Towards Optimal GUI-Tool Path Orchestration for Computer Use Agents
Authors: Xuhao Hu, Xi Zhang, Haiyang Xu, Kyle Qiao, Jingyi Yang, Xuanjing Huang, Jing Shao, Ming Yan, Jieping Ye
First: 2026-05-12T17:57:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:57:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Computer Use Agents (CUAs) can act through both atomic GUI actions, such as click and type, and high-level tool calls, such as API-based file operations, but this hybrid action space often leaves them uncertain about when to continue with GUI actions or switch to tools, leading to suboptimal execution paths. This difficulty stems from the scarcity of high-quality interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories, the cost and brittleness of collecting real tool trajectories, and the lack of trajectory-level supervision for GUI-Tool path selection. In this paper, we propose ToolCUA, an end-to-end agent designed to learn optimal GUI-Tool path selection through a staged training paradigm. We first introduce an Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling Pipeline that repurposes abundant static GUI trajectories and synthesizes a grounded tool library, enabling diverse GUI-Tool trajectories without manual engineering or real tool-trajectory collection. We then perform Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT, combining warmup SFT with single-turn RL to improve decisions at critical GUI-Tool switching points. Finally, we optimize ToolCUA with Online Agentic RL in a high-fidelity GUI-Tool environment, guided by a Tool-Efficient Path Reward that encourages appropriate tool use and shorter execution paths. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show that ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy, a relative improvement of approximately 66% over the baseline, establishing a new state of the art among models of comparable scale. It also improves by 3.9% over GUI-only settings, demonstrating effective GUI-Tool orchestration. The results further suggest that training in a hybrid action space is a promising paradigm for real-world digital agents. Open-sourced here: https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/
中文标题/摘要
标题：ToolCUA：面向计算机使用代理的最优GUI-工具路径编排
计算机使用代理（CUA）可通过原子级GUI操作（如点击、输入）和高级工具调用（如基于API的文件操作）进行操作，但这种混合动作空间常使其难以判断何时继续GUI操作或切换至工具，导致执行路径次优。此难题源于高质量交错式GUI-工具轨迹稀缺、收集真实工具轨迹成本高且脆弱，以及缺乏针对GUI-工具路径选择的轨迹级监督。本文提出ToolCUA，一种端到端代理，通过分阶段训练学习最优GUI-工具路径选择。首先，我们引入交错式GUI-工具轨迹扩增流水线，重用大量静态GUI轨迹并合成具语义的工具库，无需人工工程或真实工具轨迹收集即可生成多样化的GUI-工具轨迹。接着，我们执行工具引导的GUI RFT，结合预热SFT与单轮强化学习，提升关键GUI-工具切换点的决策质量。最后，我们在高保真GUI-工具环境中，通过工具高效路径奖励引导的在线代理强化学习优化ToolCUA。在OSWorld-MCP上的实验表明，ToolCUA准确率达46.85%，相较基线提升约66%，在同类规模模型中建立新SOTA；相较仅GUI设置提升3.9%，验证了GUI-工具协同的有效性。结果进一步表明，混合动作空间训练是现实数字代理的有前景范式。开源地址：https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of optimal GUI-Tool path selection in Computer Use Agents, where hybrid action spaces cause suboptimal execution due to scarce and costly real trajectories, ToolCUA introduces an end-to-end agent trained via a staged pipeline. It first scales interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories using static GUI data and synthesized tools, then employs tool-bootstrapped RL with warmup SFT to refine switching decisions, and finally optimizes with online agentic RL guided by a tool-efficient path reward. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy — a 66% relative gain over baseline and 3.9% over GUI-only models — demonstrating effective orchestration and validating hybrid action space training for real-world agents.
为解决计算机使用代理在GUI与工具切换时的决策难题，ToolCUA提出一种分阶段训练的端到端代理：首先通过合成工具库从静态GUI轨迹扩展出混合轨迹，继而结合SFT预热与单轮强化学习优化关键切换点决策，最终在高保真GUI-工具环境中通过工具效率奖励进行在线强化学习。在OSWorld-MCP上的实验表明，ToolCUA准确率达46.85%，相较基线模型提升66%，较纯GUI方案提升3.9%，验证了混合动作空间训练对现实数字代理的有效性。
OmniNFT: Modality-wise Omni Diffusion Reinforcement for Joint Audio-Video Generation
Authors: Guohui Zhang, XiaoXiao Ma, Jie Huang, Hang Xu, Hu Yu, Siming Fu, Yuming Li, Zeyue Xue, Lin Song, Haoyang Huang, Nan Duan, Feng Zhao
First: 2026-05-12T17:56:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:56:59+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://zghhui.github.io/OmniNFT/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recent advances in joint audio-video generation have been remarkable, yet real-world applications demand strong per-modality fidelity, cross-modal alignment, and fine-grained synchronization. Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a promising paradigm, but its extension to multi-objective and multi-modal joint audio-video generation remains unexplored. Notably, our in-depth analysis first reveals that the primary obstacles to applying RL in this stem from: (i) multi-objective advantages inconsistency, where the advantages of multimodal outputs are not always consistent within a group; (ii) multi-modal gradients imbalance, where video-branch gradients leak into shallow audio layers responsible for intra-modal generation; (iii) uniform credit assignment, where fine-grained cross-modal alignment regions fail to get efficient exploration. These shortcomings suggest that vanilla RL fine-tuning strategy with a single global advantage often leads to suboptimal results. To address these challenges, we propose OmniNFT, a novel modality-aware online diffusion RL framework with three key innovations: (1) Modality-wise advantage routing, which routes independent per-reward advantages to their respective modality generation branches. (2) Layer-wise gradient surgery, which selectively detaches video-branch gradients on shallow audio layers while retaining those for cross-modal interaction layers. (3) Region-wise loss reweighting, which modulates policy optimization toward critical regions related to audio-video synchronization and fine-grained alignment. Extensive experiments on JavisBench and VBench with the LTX-2 backbone demonstrate that OmniNFT achieves comprehensive improvements in audio and video perceptual quality, cross-modal alignment, and audio-video synchronization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniNFT：面向音频-视频联合生成的模态感知全扩散强化学习
近年来音频-视频联合生成取得了显著进展，但实际应用要求各模态高保真、跨模态对齐及细粒度同步。强化学习（RL）提供了一种有前景的范式，但其在多目标、多模态联合音频-视频生成中的扩展尚未探索。我们深入分析首次揭示了应用RL的主要障碍：(i) 多目标优势不一致，多模态输出的优势在组内不一致；(ii) 多模态梯度失衡，视频分支梯度渗入负责模内生成的浅层音频层；(iii) 均匀信用分配，细粒度跨模态对齐区域未能获得高效探索。这些缺陷表明，采用单一全局优势的普通RL微调策略往往导致次优结果。为应对这些挑战，我们提出OmniNFT，一种新颖的模态感知在线扩散RL框架，包含三项创新：(1) 模态级优势路由，将独立的每奖励优势路由至各自模态生成分支。(2) 层级梯度手术，选择性地在浅层音频层上分离视频分支梯度，同时保留跨模态交互层梯度。(3) 区域级损失重加权，调节策略优化向与音频-视频同步及细粒度对齐相关的关键区域倾斜。在JavisBench和VBench数据集上使用LTX-2骨干网络的大量实验表明，OmniNFT在音频和视频感知质量、跨模态对齐及音频-视频同步方面均取得全面提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for high-fidelity, aligned, and synchronized audio-video generation in real-world applications, this work introduces OmniNFT, a modality-aware online diffusion RL framework designed to overcome key limitations of vanilla RL in multi-modal settings. The method addresses three core issues: inconsistent multi-objective advantages, imbalanced gradients leaking from video to shallow audio layers, and uniform credit assignment that neglects critical alignment regions. It achieves this through modality-wise advantage routing, layer-wise gradient surgery to isolate cross-modal gradients, and region-wise loss reweighting to prioritize synchronization-sensitive areas. Experiments on JavisBench and VBench using the LTX-2 backbone show significant improvements in perceptual quality, cross-modal alignment, and synchronization across both modalities.
为满足现实应用中对高保真、强对齐且精细同步的音频视频生成需求，本文提出OmniNFT框架，一种面向模态的在线扩散强化学习方法，旨在解决传统强化学习在多模态场景下的三大缺陷：多目标奖励不一致、模态间梯度失衡、以及对细粒度对齐区域的信用分配不均。该方法通过模态独立奖励路由、层间梯度手术（隔离视频梯度与浅层音频层）、区域损失重加权（强化关键同步区域优化）三方面创新，有效提升生成质量。在JavisBench和VBench数据集上使用LTX-2骨干网络的实验表明，OmniNFT在音频视频感知质量、跨模态对齐与同步精度方面均显著优于基线方法。
MEME: Multi-entity & Evolving Memory Evaluation
Authors: Seokwon Jung, Alexander Rubinstein, Arnas Uselis, Sangdoo Yun, Seong Joon Oh
First: 2026-05-12T17:55:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:55:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
LLM-based agents increasingly operate in persistent environments where they must store, update, and reason over information across many sessions. While prior benchmarks evaluate only single-entity updates, MEME defines six tasks spanning the full space defined by the multi-entity and evolving axes, including three not scored by prior work: Cascade and Absence (dependency reasoning) and Deletion (post-removal state). Evaluating six memory systems spanning three memory paradigms on 100 controlled episodes, we find that all systems collapse on dependency reasoning under the default configuration (Cascade: 3%, Absence: 1% in average accuracy) despite adequate static retrieval performance. Prompt optimization, deeper retrieval, reduced filler noise, and most stronger LLMs fail to close this gap. Only a file-based agent paired with Claude Opus 4.7 as its internal LLM partially closes the gap, but at ~70x the baseline cost, indicating closure currently depends on configurations that are not practical at scale. Code and data are available on the project page: https://seokwonjung-jay.github.io/meme-eval/.

标题：MEME：多实体与动态记忆评估

Motivated by the need to evaluate LLM agents in persistent environments requiring multi-entity memory management, MEME introduces six benchmark tasks covering evolving and multi-entity memory scenarios, including under-served cases like Cascade, Absence, and Deletion.
