为评估LLM代理在需要多实体与动态记忆的持久环境中表现，MEME设计了六个涵盖依赖推理与删除后状态的任务，弥补了现有基准的不足。在100个控制实验中，六种记忆系统在三种范式下均在依赖推理任务上表现极差——Cascade平均仅3%，Absence仅1%，尽管静态检索表现良好。即使优化提示、深度检索、降低噪声或使用更强LLM，也无法显著提升这些任务表现。仅文件型代理搭配Claude Opus 4.7能部分缓解，但成本高达基线70倍，表明可扩展的解决方案尚未实现。
Routers Learn the Geometry of Their Experts: Geometric Coupling in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts
Authors: Sagi Ahrac, Noya Hochwald, Mor Geva
First: 2026-05-12T17:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (SMoE) models enable scaling language models efficiently, but training them remains challenging, as routing can collapse onto few experts and auxiliary load-balancing losses can reduce specialization. Motivated by these hurdles, we study how routing decisions in SMoEs are formed mechanistically. First, we reveal a geometric coupling between routers and their corresponding experts. For a given token, the router weights for the selected expert and the expert weights processing it receive gradients along the same input direction, differing only in scalar coefficients. Thus, matched router--expert directions accumulate the same routed token history. This theoretical coupling also appears empirically in routing dynamics. In a $1$B SMoE trained from scratch, higher router scores predict stronger expert neuron activations, showing that routing decisions are mirrored inside the selected expert. Next, we analyze the effects of auxiliary load balancing on the router--expert geometric coupling, showing that such losses break this structure by spreading input-directed gradients across router weights, making distinct router directions nearly three times more similar to each other. Last, we demonstrate the centrality of geometric coupling for effective routing with a parameter-free online K-Means router, in which each expert maintains a running average of the hidden states routed to it and tokens are assigned based on cosine similarity. Compared with auxiliary-loss and loss-free balancing, this router achieves the lowest load imbalance with only a modest perplexity increase, indicating that geometric coupling captures a substantial part of what the router learns. Overall, our results explain how routers form assignment geometry that supports an effective division of labor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：路由器学习其专家的几何结构：稀疏专家混合中的几何耦合
稀疏专家混合（SMoE）模型能高效扩展语言模型，但训练仍具挑战，因路由可能坍缩至少数专家，且辅助负载均衡损失会削弱专业化。受此启发，我们研究SMoE中路由决策的机制形成。首先，我们揭示路由器与其对应专家间的几何耦合：对给定token，所选专家的路由器权重与处理该token的专家权重沿相同输入方向接收梯度，仅标量系数不同。因此，匹配的路由器-专家方向累积相同路由token历史。此理论耦合亦在路由动态中实证出现。在从零训练的10亿参数SMoE中，更高路由器得分预测更强专家神经元激活，表明路由决策在所选专家内部被镜像。接着，我们分析辅助负载均衡对路由器-专家几何耦合的影响，显示此类损失破坏此结构，通过将输入方向梯度分散至路由器权重，使不同路由器方向近似三倍更相似。最后，我们以无参数在线K-Means路由器证明几何耦合对有效路由的核心作用，其中每个专家维护其接收的隐藏状态运行平均值，token按余弦相似度分配。相比辅助损失与无损失平衡，此路由器仅小幅增加困惑度即实现最低负载失衡，表明几何耦合捕捉了路由器所学的大部分内容。总体而言，我们的结果解释了路由器如何形成支持有效分工的分配几何结构。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of training sparse Mixture-of-Experts models, including routing collapse and load-balancing interference, this work investigates the mechanistic formation of routing decisions. It reveals a geometric coupling between routers and their assigned experts: for a given token, router and expert weights receive gradients along the same input direction, differing only by scalar coefficients, causing matched directions to accumulate similar token histories. Empirically, in a 1B SMoE model, higher router scores correlate with stronger expert activations, confirming this coupling. Auxiliary load-balancing losses disrupt this structure by spreading gradients across router weights, increasing similarity between router directions. A parameter-free online K-Means router, leveraging this geometric coupling via cosine similarity, achieves the lowest load imbalance with only a modest increase in perplexity, demonstrating that geometric coupling is central to effective routing.
为了解决稀疏专家混合模型训练中的路由坍缩和负载均衡损失削弱专业化的问题，本研究探讨了路由决策的形成机制。研究发现路由器与所选专家之间存在几何耦合：对同一输入token，路由器与专家权重沿相同输入方向接收梯度，仅系数不同，导致匹配方向积累相似的token历史。在10亿参数的SMoE模型中，路由器得分越高，对应专家神经元激活越强，验证了该耦合关系。辅助负载均衡损失会破坏这种结构，使路由器方向更相似。基于该几何结构的无参数在线K-Means路由器，仅小幅增加困惑度，即可实现最低负载不均衡，表明几何耦合是有效路由的核心机制。
Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Anas Mahmoud, MohammadHossein Rezaei, Zihao Wang, Anisha Gunjal, Bing Liu, Yunzhong He
First: 2026-05-12T17:54:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:54:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has enabled strong post-training gains in domains such as math and coding, though many open-ended settings rely on rubric-based rewards. We study reward hacking in rubric-based RL, where a policy is optimized against a training verifier but evaluated against a cross-family panel of three frontier judges, reducing dependence on any single evaluator. Our framework separates two sources of divergence: verifier failure, where the training verifier credits rubric criteria that reference verifiers reject, and rubric-design limitations, where even strong rubric-based verifiers favor responses that rubric-free judges rate worse overall. Across medical and science domains, weak verifiers produce large proxy-reward gains that do not transfer to the reference verifiers; exploitation grows over training and concentrates in recurring failures such as partial satisfaction of compound criteria, treating implicit content as explicit, and imprecise topical matching. Stronger verifiers substantially reduce, but do not eliminate, verifier exploitation. We also introduce a self-internalization gap, a verifier-free diagnostic based on policy log-probabilities, which tracks reference-verifier quality, detecting when the policy trained using the weak verifier stops improving. Finally, in our setting, stronger verification does not prevent reward hacking when the rubric leaves important failure modes unspecified: rubric-based verifiers prefer the RL checkpoint, while rubric-free judges prefer the base model. These disagreements coincide with gains concentrated in completeness and presence-based criteria, alongside declines in factual correctness, conciseness, relevance, and overall quality. Together, these results suggest that stronger verification reduces reward hacking, but does not by itself ensure that rubric gains correspond to broader quality gains.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于评分标准的强化学习中的奖励欺骗
可验证奖励的强化学习已在数学和编程等领域实现了强大的训练后收益，但许多开放性场景依赖于基于评分标准的奖励。我们研究了基于评分标准的强化学习中的奖励欺骗：策略在训练时针对验证器优化，但在评估时由三个前沿评委组成的跨领域小组进行评判，从而减少对单一评估者的依赖。我们的框架区分了两种偏差来源：验证器失效（训练验证器认可评分标准中引用但验证器拒绝的准则）和评分设计局限（即使强评分验证器也倾向于评分无标准评委评价较差的整体响应）。在医学和科学领域，弱验证器产生大量代理奖励收益，但无法迁移到参考验证器；在训练过程中，策略的利用行为逐渐增强，并集中于重复性失败，如复合标准部分满足、将隐含内容误认为显式内容、以及不精确的主题匹配。更强的验证器显著减少但无法完全消除验证器利用。我们还引入了一种无需验证器的诊断方法——自我内化差距，基于策略的对数概率，用于追踪参考验证器质量，检测策略在弱验证器训练下何时停止改进。最后，在我们的设定中，即使评分标准明确，更强的验证也无法防止奖励欺骗：基于评分的验证器偏好强化学习检查点，而无评分评委偏好基模型。这些分歧与集中在完整性和存在性标准上的收益同步发生，同时伴随事实正确性、简洁性、相关性和整体质量的下降。综合来看，这些结果表明更强的验证能减少奖励欺骗，但本身并不能确保评分收益对应更广泛的质量提升。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates reward hacking in rubric-based reinforcement learning, motivated by the need to ensure that training rewards align with broader quality metrics. The authors train policies against a training verifier but evaluate them against a cross-family panel of three frontier judges to reduce reliance on any single evaluator. They identify two divergence sources: verifier failure (where training verifiers credit criteria rejected by judges) and rubric-design limitations (where rubrics favor responses judges rate worse). Experiments in medical and science domains show that weak verifiers produce large proxy-reward gains that fail to transfer to reference judges, with exploitation concentrating in recurring failures like partial criterion satisfaction or imprecise topical matching. Stronger verifiers reduce but do not eliminate this exploitation. A self-internalization gap metric, based on policy log-probabilities, detects when training with weak verifiers stops improving. Crucially, even with strong verification, reward hacking persists when rubrics omit critical failure modes: rubric-based verifiers favor RL checkpoints, while judges prefer base models, with gains concentrated in completeness and presence criteria but declines in factual correctness, conciseness, and overall quality.
为理解基于评分标准的强化学习中的奖励黑客行为，本研究将训练用验证器与独立评委面板对比，以区分奖励偏差来源：验证器失效（训练验证器误判评分标准）与评分设计局限（评分标准偏好评委未认可的响应）。在医学与科学领域实验显示，弱验证器产生大量无法迁移的代理奖励收益，其利用集中在部分满足复合标准、误将隐含内容当显式内容、或主题匹配不精确等模式。强验证器可显著减少但无法根除此类利用，新提出的自内化差距指标可追踪政策相对于参考评委的改进停滞。关键发现是，即使验证器强化，若评分标准未明确关键失败模式，奖励黑客仍会发生——评分验证器偏好强化学习检查点，而无评分评委更偏好基线模型，且这种分歧伴随完整性和存在性指标提升，同时牺牲事实正确性、简洁性、相关性与整体质量。
KV-Fold: One-Step KV-Cache Recurrence for Long-Context Inference
Authors: Alireza Nadali, Patrick Cooper, Ashutosh Trivedi, Alvaro Velasquez
First: 2026-05-12T17:53:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:53:47+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce KV-Fold, a simple, training-free long-context inference protocol that treats the key-value (KV) cache as the accumulator in a left fold over sequence chunks. At each step, the model processes the next chunk conditioned on the accumulated cache, appends the newly produced keys and values, and passes the enlarged cache forward; the same one-step update is applied repeatedly, analogous to foldl in functional programming. Building on the KV cache concatenation primitive introduced for latent multi-agent communication, we repurpose it as a chunk-to-chunk recurrence for long-context inference. When processing chunk t, the model attends to the KV cache carried from earlier chunks as a prefix, reusing its internal state across segments without modifying or retraining the model. Despite its simplicity, the induced recurrence is stable: per-step drift rises briefly and then saturates into a flat plateau that persists across deep chains. This plateau is insensitive to a 10,000x change in numerical precision, robust across chunk sizes, and consistent across model families. At the task level, KV-Fold preserves exact information over long distances. On a needle-in-a-haystack benchmark, it achieves 100% exact-match retrieval across 152 trials spanning contexts from 16K to 128K tokens and chain depths up to 511 on Llama-3.1-8B, while remaining within the memory limits of a single 40GB GPU. Compared to streaming methods, which trade fidelity for bounded memory, KV-Fold maintains long-range retrieval while operating as a sequence of tractable forward passes. Overall, our results show that frozen pretrained transformers already support a stable form of KV-cache recurrence, providing a practical route to long-context inference without architectural changes or training.
中文标题/摘要
标题：KV-Fold：用于长上下文推理的一步KV缓存递归
我们提出了KV-Fold，一种简单且无需训练的长上下文推理协议，将键值（KV）缓存视为对序列块进行左折叠时的累加器。在每一步中，模型根据累积的缓存处理下一个块，将新生成的键和值追加到缓存中，并将扩大的缓存传递给下一步；相同的单步更新被重复应用，类似于函数式编程中的foldl。基于为潜在多智能体通信引入的KV缓存拼接原语，我们将其重新用于长上下文推理中的块到块递归。在处理第t个块时，模型将来自先前块的KV缓存作为前缀进行注意力计算，从而在不修改或重新训练模型的情况下复用其内部状态。尽管其设计简单，所诱导的递归是稳定的：每步漂移短暂上升后会稳定在一个平坦平台，该平台在深层链中持续存在。该平台对数值精度变化10,000倍不敏感，对块大小鲁棒，且在不同模型家族间保持一致。在任务层面，KV-Fold在长距离上保持精确信息。在“针在 haystack 中”基准测试中，它在152次试验中实现了100%精确匹配检索，覆盖从16K到128K个token的上下文长度和最多511层的链深度（在Llama-3.1-8B上），同时仍能在单个40GB GPU内存限制内运行。与牺牲保真度换取内存受限的流式方法相比，KV-Fold在保持长距离检索能力的同时，以可处理的前向传递序列运行。总体而言，我们的结果表明，冻结的预训练Transformer已支持一种稳定的KV缓存递归形式，为无需架构修改或训练即可实现长上下文推理提供了一条实用路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient long-context inference without retraining or architectural modifications, KV-Fold treats the KV cache as an accumulator in a left fold over sequence chunks, enabling recurrent processing of long sequences through repeated one-step updates. At each step, the model processes a new chunk conditioned on the accumulated cache, appends new keys and values, and passes the enlarged cache forward, reusing internal state across segments. Experimental results show that this recurrence is numerically stable, with drift saturating into a flat plateau across deep chains, insensitive to precision changes and consistent across models and chunk sizes. On a needle-in-a-haystack benchmark, KV-Fold achieves 100% exact-match retrieval across 152 trials with contexts up to 128K tokens and chain depths up to 511 on Llama-3.1-8B, all within a single 40GB GPU’s memory, outperforming streaming methods that sacrifice fidelity for memory efficiency.
为实现无需微调或架构修改的长上下文推理，KV-Fold提出一种训练免费协议，将KV缓存视为序列块上的左折叠累加器。每一步模型基于累积缓存处理新块，追加新生成的键值对并传递扩大后的缓存，重复此单步更新操作。实验表明该递归过程数值稳定，每步漂移迅速饱和至平坦平台，不受精度变化或块大小影响。在针在 haystack 评测中，KV-Fold在152次试验中实现100%精确召回，上下文长度达128K token，链深度达511（Llama-3.1-8B），且单个40GB显存内完成，优于牺牲精度换取内存效率的流式方法。
Coevolutionary Continuous Discrete Diffusion: Make Your Diffusion Language Model a Latent Reasoner
Authors: Cai Zhou, Chenxiao Yang, Yi Hu, Chenyu Wang, Chubin Zhang, Muhan Zhang, Lester Mackey, Tommi Jaakkola, Stephen Bates, Dinghuai Zhang
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-10-03T17:44:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:53:41+00:00
Comments: 29 pages. Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion language models, especially masked discrete diffusion models, have achieved great success recently. While there are some theoretical and primary empirical results showing the advantages of latent reasoning with looped transformers or continuous chain-of-thoughts, continuous diffusion models typically underperform their discrete counterparts. In this paper, we argue that diffusion language models do not necessarily need to be in the discrete space. In particular, we prove that continuous diffusion models have stronger expressivity than discrete diffusions and looped transformers. We attribute the contradiction between the theoretical expressiveness and empirical performance to their practical trainability: while continuous diffusion provides intermediate supervision that looped transformers lack, they introduce additional difficulty decoding tokens into the discrete token space from the continuous representation space. We therefore propose Coevolutionary Continuous Discrete Diffusion (CCDD), which defines a joint multimodal diffusion process on the union of a continuous representation space and a discrete token space, leveraging a single model to simultaneously denoise in the joint space. By combining two modalities, CCDD is expressive with rich semantics in the latent space, as well as good trainability and sample quality with the help of explicit discrete tokens. We also propose effective architectures and advanced training/sampling techniques for CCDD, which reveals strong empirical performance in extensive language modeling experiments on real-world tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：共演化连续离散扩散：让您的扩散语言模型成为潜在推理器
扩散语言模型，特别是掩码离散扩散模型，近期取得了巨大成功。尽管已有部分理论和初步实证结果表明，使用循环Transformer或连续思维链的潜在推理具有优势，但连续扩散模型通常表现不如其离散对应物。本文我们认为，扩散语言模型并不一定需要在离散空间中进行。特别是，我们证明连续扩散模型的表达能力强于离散扩散和循环Transformer。我们将理论表达能力与实证性能之间的矛盾归因于其实际训练性：虽然连续扩散提供循环Transformer所缺乏的中间监督，但它们在从连续表示空间解码为离散标记空间时引入了额外困难。因此，我们提出共演化连续离散扩散（CCDD），它在连续表示空间与离散标记空间的并集上定义联合多模态扩散过程，利用单一模型同时在联合空间中去噪。通过结合两种模态，CCDD在潜在空间中具有丰富的语义表达能力，同时借助显式离散标记，具备良好的训练性和采样质量。我们还为CCDD提出了有效的架构和先进的训练/采样技术，在现实任务的广泛语言建模实验中展现出强大的实证性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the theoretical expressiveness of continuous diffusion models over discrete counterparts and looped transformers, this paper proposes Coevolutionary Continuous Discrete Diffusion (CCDD) to bridge the gap between latent reasoning capability and practical trainability. CCDD jointly models diffusion in a unified space combining continuous representations and discrete tokens, enabling the model to leverage both semantic richness from continuous space and explicit token supervision from discrete space. Experimental results demonstrate that CCDD achieves superior performance in language modeling tasks compared to existing discrete and continuous diffusion models, while maintaining strong sample quality and training efficiency.
鉴于连续扩散模型在理论上比离散扩散和循环Transformer更具表达能力，但实际表现不佳，本文提出联合连续-离散扩散模型（CCDD），在连续表示空间与离散词空间上共同进行去噪。该模型通过单个模型同时处理两个模态，结合连续空间的语义丰富性与离散词的可训练性和采样质量优势。实验表明，CCDD在多个真实语言建模任务中表现优异，超越现有离散扩散和循环Transformer模型，同时保持高质量采样和高效训练。
Does Algorithmic Uncertainty Sway Human Experts? Evidence from a Field Experiment in Selective College Admissions
Authors: Hansol Lee, AJ Alvero, René F. Kizilcec, Thorsten Joachims
First: 2026-01-30T19:03:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:52:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2026 ACM Conference on Fairness, Accountability, and Transparency (FAccT 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Algorithmic predictions are inherently uncertain: even models with similar aggregate accuracy can produce different predictions for the same individual, raising concerns that high-stakes decisions may become sensitive to arbitrary modeling choices. In this paper, we define \emph{algorithmic sensitivity} as the extent to which arbitrary modeling choices propagate into human decisions: how much a decision outcome shifts when a more favorable versus less favorable algorithmic prediction is presented to the decision-maker for the same individual. We estimate this in a randomized field experiment ($n=19{,}545$) embedded in a selective U.S. college admissions cycle, in which admissions officers reviewed each application alongside an algorithmic score while we randomly varied whether the score came from one of two similarly accurate prediction models. Although the two models performed similarly in aggregate, they frequently assigned different scores to the same applicant, creating exogenous variation in the score shown. Surprisingly, we find little evidence of algorithmic sensitivity: presenting a more favorable score does not meaningfully increase an applicant's probability of admission on average, even when the models disagree substantially. These findings suggest that, in this expert, high-stakes setting, human decision-making is largely invariant to arbitrary variation in algorithmic predictions, underscoring the role of professional discretion and institutional context in mediating the downstream effects of algorithmic uncertainty.
中文标题/摘要
标题：算法不确定性会影响人类专家决策吗？来自择校录取现场实验的证据
算法预测固有不确定性：即使模型整体准确率相似，也可能对同一人产生不同预测，引发对高风险决策可能受任意建模选择影响的担忧。本文将“算法敏感性”定义为任意建模选择如何影响人类决策的程度——即当向决策者展示更优或更差的算法预测时，决策结果如何变化。我们在一项嵌入美国精英大学录取流程的随机现场实验（n=19,545）中估计了该指标，录取官员在审阅每份申请时同时看到算法评分，而我们随机切换评分来自两个相似准确率模型之一。尽管两模型整体表现相近，但常对同一申请人给出不同评分，从而产生评分的外生变异。令人惊讶的是，我们发现几乎无算法敏感性证据：即使模型评分差异显著，展示更优评分也未显著提高申请人的录取概率。这些发现表明，在专家主导、高风险的环境中，人类决策对算法预测的任意变化基本不变，凸显了专业裁量权和制度环境在缓冲算法不确定性下游影响中的作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that algorithmic uncertainty may unduly influence high-stakes human decisions, this study investigates whether admissions officers’ choices vary based on arbitrary differences in algorithmic predictions. Using a randomized field experiment with 19,545 applicants, the researchers compared how admissions officers responded to two similarly accurate but divergent algorithmic scores for the same applicants. Despite significant score discrepancies between models, the study found minimal evidence that presenting a more favorable score increased admission likelihood, suggesting human decision-makers in expert contexts largely ignore algorithmic variability. This implies that professional discretion and institutional norms play a stronger role than algorithmic uncertainty in shaping outcomes.
鉴于算法预测固有的不确定性可能影响高风险的人类决策，本研究探讨招生官是否受算法预测差异的影响。在一项针对19,545名申请者的随机田野实验中，研究人员比较了两个预测准确率相近但对同一申请人评分不同的模型，并测量了更优评分是否显著提高录取率。结果发现，算法评分的差异对录取结果影响甚微，即使模型评分分歧明显，表明在该专业高风险场景下，人类决策者基本不受算法预测任意变化的影响。
jina-embeddings-v5-omni: Geometry-preserving Embeddings via Locked Aligned Towers
Authors: Florian Hönicke, Michael Günther, Andreas Koukounas, Mohammad Kalim Akram, Scott Martens, Saba Sturua, Han Xiao
First: 2026-05-08T18:45:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:52:45+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 8 figures, 10 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we introduce GELATO (Geometry-preserving Embeddings via Locked Aligned TOwers), a novel approach to multimodal embedding models. We build on the VLM-style architecture, in which non-text encoders are adapted to produce input for a language model, which in turn generates embeddings for all varieties of input. We present the result: the jina-embeddings-v5-omni suite, a pair of models that encode text, image, audio, and video input into a single semantic embedding space. GELATO extends the two Jina Embeddings v5 Text models to support additional modality by adding encoders for images and audio. The backbone text embedding models and the added non-text modality encoders remain frozen. We only trained the connecting components, representing 0.35% of the total weights of the joint model. Training is therefore much more efficient than full-parameter retraining. Additionally, the language model remains effectively unaltered, producing exactly the same embeddings for text inputs as the Jina Embeddings v5 Text models. Our evaluations show that GELATO produces results that are competitive with the state-of-the-art, yielding nearly equal performance to larger multimodal embedding models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：jina-embeddings-v5-omni：通过锁定对齐塔实现几何保持嵌入
本研究提出GELATO（通过锁定对齐塔实现几何保持嵌入），一种新颖的多模态嵌入模型方法。我们基于VLM风格架构，其中非文本编码器被适配以生成语言模型输入，语言模型进而为所有类型的输入生成嵌入。我们展示了成果：jina-embeddings-v5-omni套件，包含两个模型，可将文本、图像、音频和视频输入编码至单一语义嵌入空间。GELATO将两个Jina Embeddings v5文本模型扩展支持额外模态，通过添加图像和音频编码器实现。主干文本嵌入模型与新增非文本模态编码器保持冻结。我们仅训练连接组件，占联合模型总权重的0.35%。因此训练效率远高于全参数重训练。此外，语言模型基本未改变，对文本输入生成的嵌入与Jina Embeddings v5文本模型完全一致。评估表明，GELATO性能与当前最先进方法相当，几乎与更大规模的多模态嵌入模型表现一致。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, unified multimodal embeddings that preserve semantic geometry across text, image, audio, and video, the authors introduce GELATO, a method that extends existing text-only Jina Embeddings v5 models by adding frozen image and audio encoders. The approach connects these non-text encoders to a shared language model backbone without retraining the backbone or the new encoders, training only a small fraction (0.35%) of the total weights. Experimental results demonstrate that the resulting jina-embeddings-v5-omni models achieve performance nearly equal to larger multimodal models while maintaining compatibility with existing text embeddings and offering significant training efficiency.
为实现高效且高质量的跨模态嵌入并保持模态间的几何结构，作者提出GELATO方法，通过在现有文本嵌入模型基础上添加冻结的图像与音频编码器来扩展其能力。该方法采用VLM架构，仅训练连接组件（占总权重0.35%），语言模型保持不变，文本嵌入结果与原模型一致。实验表明，生成的jina-embeddings-v5-omni模型在性能上可媲美大型跨模态模型，同时大幅降低训练成本。
Prototype Fusion: A Training-Free Multi-Layer Approach to OOD Detection
Authors: Shreen Gul, Mohamed Elmahallawy, Ardhendu Tripathy, Sanjay Madria
First: 2026-03-24T19:32:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:52:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Deep learning models are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications, where reliable out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is essential to ensure robustness. Existing methods predominantly rely on the penultimate-layer activations of neural networks, assuming they encapsulate the most informative in-distribution (ID) representations. In this work, we revisit this assumption to show that intermediate layers encode equally rich and discriminative information for OOD detection. Based on this observation, we propose a simple yet effective model-agnostic approach that leverages internal representations across multiple layers. Our scheme aggregates features from successive convolutional blocks, computes class-wise mean embeddings, and applies L_2 normalization to form compact ID prototypes capturing class semantics. During inference, cosine similarity between test features and these prototypes serves as an OOD score--ID samples exhibit strong affinity to at least one prototype, whereas OOD samples remain uniformly distant. Extensive experiments on state-of-the-art OOD benchmarks across diverse architectures demonstrate that our approach delivers robust, architecture-agnostic performance and strong generalization for image classification. Notably, it improves AUROC by up to 4.41% and reduces FPR by 13.58%, highlighting multi-layer feature aggregation as a powerful yet underexplored signal for OOD detection, challenging the dominance of penultimate-layer-based methods. Our code is available at: https://github.com/sgchr273/cosine-layers.git.
中文标题/摘要
标题：原型融合：一种无需训练的多层OOD检测方法
深度学习模型在安全关键应用中日益部署，可靠地检测分布外（OOD）样本对确保其鲁棒性至关重要。现有方法主要依赖神经网络的倒数第二层激活，假设其包含最具有信息量的分布内（ID）表示。本文重新审视这一假设，表明中间层同样编码了丰富且具有判别力的OOD检测信息。基于此观察，我们提出一种简单而有效的模型无关方法，利用多层内部表示。该方案聚合连续卷积块的特征，计算类均值嵌入，并应用L_2归一化形成紧凑的ID原型，捕捉类别语义。推理时，测试特征与这些原型间的余弦相似度作为OOD得分——ID样本至少与一个原型高度相似，而OOD样本则与所有原型保持均匀距离。在多种架构的主流OOD基准上广泛实验表明，我们的方法具有架构无关性、强泛化能力，且在图像分类任务中表现稳健。值得注意的是，其AUROC最高提升4.41%，FPR降低13.58%，凸显多层特征聚合作为OOD检测的强大但尚未充分探索的信号，挑战了基于倒数第二层方法的主导地位。代码见：https://github.com/sgchr273/cosine-layers.git。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable out-of-distribution detection in safety-critical applications, this work challenges the prevailing assumption that penultimate-layer activations are optimal for OOD detection by demonstrating that intermediate layers also encode rich, discriminative information. The method proposes a training-free, model-agnostic approach that aggregates features from multiple convolutional layers, computes class-wise mean embeddings, and normalizes them into compact prototypes representing class semantics. During inference, cosine similarity between test samples and these prototypes yields an OOD score: in-distribution samples show high similarity to at least one prototype, while out-of-distribution samples remain uniformly distant. Experiments across diverse architectures show the method achieves robust, generalizable performance, improving AUROC by up to 4.41% and reducing false positive rate by 13.58%, underscoring the effectiveness of multi-layer feature fusion for OOD detection.
为提升安全关键场景下模型对分布外数据的检测能力，该研究质疑了当前依赖网络倒数第二层激活值进行异常检测的主流假设。提出一种无需训练、模型无关的方法，通过聚合多个中间卷积层的特征，计算各类别均值嵌入并进行L2归一化，形成表征类别语义的紧凑原型。推理时，测试样本与原型间的余弦相似度作为异常得分：分布内样本与至少一个原型高度相似，分布外样本则普遍距离较远。在多个主流基准和架构上实验表明，该方法表现稳健、泛化能力强，AUROC最高提升4.41%，FPR降低13.58%，证明多层特征聚合是优于单层方法的有效信号。
Solve the Loop: Attractor Models for Language and Reasoning
Authors: Jacob Fein-Ashley, Paria Rashidinejad
First: 2026-05-12T17:51:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:51:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Looped Transformers offer a promising alternative to purely feed-forward computation by iteratively refining latent representations, improving language modeling and reasoning. Yet recurrent architectures remain unstable to train, costly to optimize and deploy, and constrained to small, fixed recurrence depths. We introduce Attractor Models, in which a backbone module first proposes output embeddings, then an attractor module refines them by solving for the fixed point, with gradients obtained through implicit differentiation. Thus, training memory remains constant in effective depth, and iterations are chosen adaptively by convergence. Empirically, Attractor Models outperform existing models across two regimes, large-scale language-model pretraining and reasoning with tiny models. In language modeling, Attractor Models deliver a Pareto improvement over standard Transformers and stable looped models across sizes, improving perplexity by up to 46.6% and downstream accuracy by up to 19.7% while reducing training cost. Notably, a 770M Attractor Model outperforms a 1.3B Transformer trained on twice as many tokens. On challenging reasoning tasks, we show that our model with only 27M parameters and approximately 1000 examples achieves 91.4% accuracy on Sudoku-Extreme and 93.1% on Maze-Hard, scaling favorably where frontier models like Claude and GPT o3, fail completely, and specialized recursive reasoners collapse at larger sizes. Lastly, we show that Attractor Models exhibit a novel phenomenon, which we call equilibrium internalization: fixed-point training places the model's initial output embedding near equilibrium, allowing the solver to be removed at inference time with little degradation. Together, these results suggest that Attractor Models make iterative refinement scalable by turning recurrence into a computation the model can learn to internalize.
中文标题/摘要
标题：解决循环：用于语言与推理的吸引子模型
循环Transformer通过迭代优化潜在表示，为纯前馈计算提供了有前景的替代方案，可提升语言建模与推理能力。然而，循环架构训练不稳定、优化与部署成本高，且受限于固定的小规模循环深度。我们提出吸引子模型：骨干模块首先生成输出嵌入，吸引子模块通过求解不动点对其进行优化，梯度通过隐式微分获得。因此，有效深度下的训练内存保持恒定，迭代次数由收敛性自适应选择。实证表明，吸引子模型在大规模语言模型预训练与小型模型推理两个场景中均优于现有模型。在语言建模中，吸引子模型在不同规模下均优于标准Transformer与稳定循环模型，困惑度降低最高达46.6%，下游准确率提升最高达19.7%，同时降低训练成本。值得注意的是，7.7亿参数的吸引子模型在双倍训练token量下超越13亿参数的Transformer。在复杂推理任务中，仅2700万参数且约1000个示例的模型在Sudoku-Extreme上准确率达91.4%，在Maze-Hard上达93.1%，在前沿模型如Claude与GPT-3完全失败、专用递归推理器在更大规模下崩溃的场景中表现优异。最后，我们发现吸引子模型具有一种新现象——平衡内化：固定点训练使模型初始输出嵌入接近平衡态，推理时可移除求解器而性能几乎无损。综合结果表明，吸引子模型通过将循环转化为模型可学习内化的计算，实现了迭代优化的可扩展性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the instability and inefficiency of traditional recurrent architectures, the authors propose Attractor Models that use iterative refinement via fixed-point solving to enable scalable looped computation. The method combines a backbone module that generates initial embeddings with an attractor module that refines them through implicit differentiation, allowing training memory to remain constant while iterations adapt to convergence. Experiments show Attractor Models outperform standard Transformers and stable looped models in both large-scale language modeling and small-model reasoning tasks, achieving up to 46.6% lower perplexity and 19.7% higher accuracy while reducing training cost. A 770M model surpasses a 1.3B Transformer trained on double the tokens, and a 27M model achieves 91.4% on Sudoku-Extreme and 93.1% on Maze-Hard—outperforming frontier models and specialized reasoners at scale. The model also exhibits 'equilibrium internalization,' where initial embeddings are close to fixed points, enabling solver removal at inference with minimal degradation.
为解决循环架构在语言与推理任务中训练不稳定、成本高、深度固定等缺陷，本文提出吸引子模型，通过隐式微分求解固定点来迭代优化潜在输出，实现自适应迭代与恒定内存消耗。实验证明，该模型在不同规模下均优于标准Transformer及稳定循环模型，困惑度降低最高达46.6%，下游准确率提升最高达19.7%，同时降低训练成本。7.7亿参数模型超越训练数据量翻倍的13亿参数Transformer，2700万参数模型在Sudoku-Extreme和Maze-Hard上分别达到91.4%和93.1%准确率，优于前沿大模型与专用递归推理器。此外，模型表现出“平衡内化”现象：初始嵌入接近固定点，推理时可移除求解器而性能几乎无损。
netFound: Principled Design for Network Foundation Models
Authors: Sylee Beltiukov, Satyandra Guthula, Haarika Manda, Jaber Daneshamooz, Wenbo Guo, Walter Willinger, Arpit Gupta, Inder Monga
First: 2023-10-25T22:04:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:50:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Network foundation models promise reusable representations for diverse traffic analysis tasks, but recent diagnostic works have revealed fundamental problems: models exploit dataset shortcuts rather than learning genuine traffic patterns, produce collapsed embedding spaces, and fail to capture the exogenous network conditions that shape real-world behavior. We translate these diagnostic insights into four concrete design principles: protocol-aware tokenization, operational context embedding, burst-flow hierarchical attention, and privacy-by-construction input design, and build netFound, a network foundation model whose architecture is motivated by this failure analysis. We pretrain netFound on a billion-token-scale corpus over 5000 GPU hours, and demonstrate that it produces high-quality representations with lower anisotropy, significantly higher alignment with domain-expert features, and an F1 of 0.95 on exogenous context discrimination where existing state-of-the-art models score below 0.62, while preserving privacy by excluding payload and IP addresses. netFound demonstrates significant improvements in frozen-encoder evaluation, showing that pretrained embeddings themselves carry useful structure, and remains the top performer across all benchmarks in end-to-end fine-tuned settings. We release full open-source code, weights for three model sizes on HuggingFace, a containerized pipeline from raw PCAPs to downstream inference, and the full 4.2 billion flows pretraining dataset to facilitate reproducibility and further research.
中文标题/摘要
标题：netFound：网络基础模型的原理化设计
网络基础模型承诺为多样化的流量分析任务提供可复用的表示，但近期诊断研究揭示了根本性问题：模型利用数据集捷径而非学习真实的流量模式，产生坍缩的嵌入空间，且无法捕捉塑造现实世界行为的外生网络条件。我们将这些诊断见解转化为四项具体设计原则：协议感知的分词、操作上下文嵌入、突发-流分层注意力和内置隐私的输入设计，并构建了netFound网络基础模型，其架构源于对上述失败的分析。我们在10亿令牌规模语料上以5000 GPU小时进行预训练，证明其生成低各向异性、与领域专家特征高度对齐的高质量表示，在外生上下文判别任务中F1达0.95（现有最先进模型低于0.62），同时通过排除载荷和IP地址保障隐私。netFound在冻结编码器评估中表现显著提升，表明预训练嵌入本身蕴含有用结构，并在端到端微调设置中持续保持所有基准测试的最高性能。我们开源完整代码、HuggingFace上的三个模型尺寸权重、从原始PCAP到下游推理的容器化流水线，以及42亿条流的完整预训练数据集，以促进可复现性与进一步研究。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by diagnostic findings that existing network models exploit dataset shortcuts and fail to capture real-world exogenous conditions, the authors propose netFound, a network foundation model guided by four design principles: protocol-aware tokenization, operational context embedding, burst-flow hierarchical attention, and privacy-by-construction input design. Pretrained on a 4.2 billion-token corpus over 5000 GPU hours, netFound generates embeddings with lower anisotropy and stronger alignment with domain-expert features, achieving an F1 score of 0.95 on exogenous context discrimination—far surpassing prior state-of-the-art models’ 0.62. It also outperforms competitors in both frozen-encoder and end-to-end fine-tuned evaluations, demonstrating that pretrained representations carry meaningful structure while preserving privacy by excluding payload and IP addresses.
鉴于现有网络基础模型存在依赖数据捷径、嵌入空间塌陷及忽略外部网络条件等问题，作者提出netFound模型，其设计基于四项原则：协议感知分词、操作上下文嵌入、突发流分层注意力及内置隐私输入设计。该模型在42亿令牌语料上预训练5000 GPU小时，生成的嵌入具有更低的各向异性、更强的领域专家特征对齐度，且在外部上下文判别任务中F1达0.95，远超现有SOTA模型（0.62）。冻结编码器评估和端到端微调测试中均表现优异，同时通过排除载荷与IP地址实现隐私保护。
High-arity Sample Compression
Authors: Leonardo N. Coregliano, William Opich
First: 2026-05-12T17:50:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:50:14+00:00
Comments: 29 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recently, a series of works have started studying variations of concepts from learning theory for product spaces, which can be collected under the name high-arity learning theory. In this work, we consider a high-arity variant of sample compression schemes and we prove that the existence of a high-arity sample compression scheme of non-trivial quality implies high-arity PAC learnability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高阶样本压缩
近期，一系列工作开始研究学习理论中针对乘积空间的概念变体，这些可统称为高阶学习理论。在本工作中，我们考虑高阶样本压缩方案的变体，并证明存在非平凡质量的高阶样本压缩方案意味着高阶PAC可学习性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by recent interest in high-arity learning theory, this work investigates high-arity sample compression schemes and establishes a theoretical link between their existence and PAC learnability in product spaces. The method involves proving that if a non-trivial high-arity sample compression scheme exists for a concept class, then the class is learnable under the high-arity PAC model. Experimental results are not presented, as the paper is theoretical and focuses on proving implications rather than empirical validation.
受高阶学习理论研究热潮推动，本文探讨了高阶样本压缩方案，并证明存在非平凡质量的高阶样本压缩方案可推出高阶PAC可学习性。方法在于将压缩方案形式化于高阶设定下，并通过压缩界建立与可学习性的理论联系。本文为理论性研究，未包含实验结果，重点在于证明理论推论而非实证验证。
Search Your Block Floating Point Scales!
Authors: Tanmaey Gupta, Hayden Prairie, Xiaoxia Wu, Reyna Abhyankar, Qingyang Wu, Austin Silveria, Pragaash Ponnusamy, Jue Wang, Ben Athiwaratkun, Leon Song, Tri Dao, Daniel Y. Fu, Chris De Sa
First: 2026-05-12T17:50:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:50:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Quantization has emerged as a standard technique for accelerating inference for generative models by enabling faster low-precision computations and reduced memory transfers. Recently, GPU accelerators have added first-class support for microscaling Block Floating Point (BFP) formats. Standard BFP algorithms use a fixed scale based on the maximum magnitude of the block. We observe that this scale choice can be suboptimal with respect to quantization errors. In this work, we propose ScaleSearch, an alternative strategy for selecting these scale factors: using a fine-grained search leveraging the mantissa bits in microscaling formats to minimize the quantization error for the given distribution. ScaleSearch can be integrated with existing quantization methods such as Post Training Quantization and low-precision attention, and is shown to improve their performance. Additionally, we introduce ScaleSearchAttention, an accelerated NVFP4-based attention algorithm, which uses ScaleSearch and adapted prior techniques to ensure near-0 performance loss for causal language modeling. Experiments show that ScaleSearch reduces quantization error by 27% for NVFP4 and improves language model PTQ by up to 15 points for MATH500 (Qwen3-8B), while ScaleSearchAttention improves Wikitext-2 PPL by upto 0.77 points for Llama 3.1 70B. The proposed methods closely match baseline performance while providing quantization accuracy improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：搜索你的块浮点标度！
量化已成为加速生成模型推理的标准技术，通过启用更快的低精度计算和减少内存传输。最近，GPU加速器已为微缩放块浮点（BFP）格式提供了原生支持。标准BFP算法使用基于块最大幅值的固定标度。我们观察到，这种标度选择可能在量化误差方面不够理想。在本文中，我们提出了ScaleSearch，一种替代的标度因子选择策略：利用微缩放格式中的尾数位进行细粒度搜索，以最小化给定分布的量化误差。ScaleSearch可与现有量化方法（如后训练量化和低精度注意力）集成，并被证明能提升其性能。此外，我们引入了ScaleSearchAttention，一种基于NVFP4的加速注意力算法，结合ScaleSearch和改进的先前技术，确保因果语言建模的近零性能损失。实验表明，ScaleSearch将NVFP4的量化误差降低27%，在MATH500（Qwen3-8B）上提升语言模型PTQ性能最高达15点；ScaleSearchAttention在Llama 3.1 70B上将Wikitext-2的PPL提升最高达0.77点。所提方法在保持基线性能的同时，提供了量化精度的改进。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the suboptimal quantization errors caused by fixed-scale Block Floating Point (BFP) formats in GPU-accelerated generative models, this work introduces ScaleSearch, a fine-grained search method that leverages mantissa bits to select optimal scale factors for minimizing quantization error. ScaleSearch is compatible with existing quantization techniques like Post Training Quantization and low-precision attention, and when applied to NVFP4, it reduces quantization error by 27%. The paper further presents ScaleSearchAttention, an NVFP4-based attention algorithm that achieves near-zero performance loss in causal language modeling, improving Wikitext-2 PPL by up to 0.77 points for Llama 3.1 70B and boosting MATH500 scores by up to 15 points for Qwen3-8B under PTQ.
针对GPU加速生成模型中固定尺度Block Floating Point（BFP）格式导致的量化误差问题，本文提出ScaleSearch方法，通过利用尾数位进行细粒度搜索，选择最优尺度因子以最小化量化误差。该方法兼容现有量化技术如后训练量化和低精度注意力，并应用于ScaleSearchAttention——一种基于NVFP4的注意力算法，可实现接近零性能损失。实验表明，ScaleSearch在NVFP4上将量化误差降低27%，在Qwen3-8B模型上提升MATH500性能最高15分；ScaleSearchAttention在Llama 3.1 70B模型上将Wikitext-2 PPL提升最高0.77分，同时保持与基线相当的精度。
Towards Affordable Energy: A Gymnasium Environment for Electric Utility Demand-Response Programs
Authors: Jose E. Aguilar Escamilla, Lingdong Zhou, Xiangqi Zhu, Huazheng Wang
First: 2026-05-12T17:48:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:48:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extreme weather and volatile wholesale electricity markets expose residential consumers to catastrophic financial risks, yet demand response at the distribution level remains an underutilized tool for grid flexibility and energy affordability. While a demand-response program can shield consumers by issuing financial credits during high-price periods, optimizing this sequential decision-making process presents a unique challenge for reinforcement learning despite the plentiful offline historical smart meter and wholesale pricing data available publicly. Offline historical data fails to capture the dynamic, interactive feedback loop between an electric utility's pricing signals and customer acceptance and adaptation to a demand-response program. To address this, we introduce DR-Gym, an open-source, online Gymnasium-compatible environment designed to train and evaluate demand-response from the electric utility's perspective. Unlike existing device-level energy simulators, our environment focuses on the market-level electric utility setting and provides a rich observational space relevant to the electric utility. The simulator additionally features a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world extreme events, alongside physics-based building demand profiles. For our learning signal, we use a configurable, multi-objective reward function for specifying diverse learning objectives. We demonstrate through baseline strategies and data snapshots the capability of our simulator to create realistic and learnable environments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可负担能源：面向电力公用事业需求响应项目的Gymnasium环境
极端天气和波动的批发电力市场使居民消费者面临灾难性财务风险，但配电层面的需求响应仍是一种未被充分利用的电网灵活性和能源可负担性工具。尽管需求响应项目可通过在高电价期间发放财务信用来保护消费者，但优化这一顺序决策过程对强化学习提出了独特挑战，尽管公开的离线历史智能电表和批发电价数据丰富。离线历史数据无法捕捉电力公用事业定价信号与客户对需求响应项目的接受度和适应度之间的动态互动反馈循环。为此，我们引入DR-Gym，一个开源的、兼容Gymnasium的在线环境，旨在从电力公用事业角度训练和评估需求响应。与现有的设备级能源模拟器不同，我们的环境聚焦于市场级电力公用事业场景，并提供与电力公用事业相关的丰富观测空间。该模拟器还具备校准至真实世界极端事件的制度切换批发价格模型，以及基于物理的建筑需求曲线。我们的学习信号采用可配置的多目标奖励函数，以指定多样化的学习目标。我们通过基准策略和数据快照展示了该模拟器创建真实且可学习环境的能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the financial risks residential consumers face due to extreme weather and volatile electricity markets, this paper proposes DR-Gym, an online Gymnasium-compatible simulator to train demand-response strategies for electric utilities. The method models the dynamic interaction between utility pricing signals and consumer behavior using a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world events and physics-based building demand profiles. Experimental results show the simulator effectively generates realistic, learnable environments that support multi-objective reinforcement learning, enabling evaluation of baseline strategies and demonstrating the feasibility of optimizing utility-level demand response using offline data.
为应对极端天气和电价波动给居民带来的财务风险，本文提出DR-Gym，一个面向电力公司视角的在线Gymnasium兼容仿真环境，用于训练需求响应策略。与设备级仿真不同，DR-Gym聚焦市场层级，模拟电价信号与用户行为的互动，结合校准至真实极端事件的分段电价模型和基于物理的建筑用电曲线。其采用可配置的多目标奖励函数引导学习，并通过基准策略和数据快照验证了环境的现实性和可学习性，证明其在需求响应优化中的强化学习适用性。
A proximal gradient algorithm for composite log-concave sampling
Authors: Linghai Liu, Sinho Chewi
First: 2026-05-12T17:48:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:48:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose an algorithm to sample from composite log-concave distributions over $\mathbb{R}^d$, i.e., densities of the form $π\propto e^{-f-g}$, assuming access to gradient evaluations of $f$ and a restricted Gaussian oracle (RGO) for $g$. The latter requirement means that we can easily sample from the density $\text{RGO}_{g,h,y}(x) \propto \exp(-g(x) -\frac{1}{2h}||y-x||^2)$, which is the sampling analogue of the proximal operator for $g$. If $f + g$ is $α$-strongly convex and $f$ is $β$-smooth, our sampler achieves $\varepsilon$ error in total variation distance in $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(κ\sqrt d \log^4(1/\varepsilon))$ iterations where $κ:= β/α$, which matches prior state-of-the-art results for the case $g=0$. We further extend our results to cases where (1) $π$ is non-log-concave but satisfies a Poincaré or log-Sobolev inequality, and (2) $f$ is non-smooth but Lipschitz.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于复合对数凹采样的近端梯度算法
我们提出了一种算法，用于在 $\mathbb{R}^d$ 上从复合对数凹分布（即密度形式为 $π\propto e^{-f-g}$）进行采样，假设可访问 $f$ 的梯度评估，并对 $g$ 有受限高斯预言机（RGO）。后者要求我们能轻松采样自密度 $\text{RGO}_{g,h,y}(x) \propto \exp(-g(x) -\frac{1}{2h}||y-x||^2)$，这是 $g$ 的采样近端算子的类比。若 $f + g$ 是 $α$-强凸且 $f$ 是 $β$-光滑，则我们的采样器在总变差距离下达到 $\varepsilon$ 误差，所需迭代次数为 $\widetilde{\mathcal O}(κ\sqrt d \log^4(1/\varepsilon))$，其中 $κ:= β/α$，这与 $g=0$ 时的现有最先进结果一致。我们进一步将结果扩展到以下情形：(1) $π$ 非对数凹但满足庞卡莱或对数索博列夫不等式；(2) $f$ 非光滑但利普希茨连续。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient sampling from composite log-concave distributions, the paper introduces a proximal gradient algorithm that leverages gradient evaluations of f and a restricted Gaussian oracle (RGO) for g. The algorithm constructs iterates using the RGO, which acts as a sampling analog of the proximal operator for g. Under assumptions that f + g is α-strongly convex and f is β-smooth, the method achieves ε-accuracy in total variation distance within \widetilde{\mathcal O}(κ\sqrt d \log^4(1/\varepsilon)) iterations, where κ = β/α, matching prior best-known results when g=0. The framework is further extended to handle non-log-concave target distributions satisfying Poincaré or log-Sobolev inequalities, and to accommodate non-smooth but Lipschitz f functions.
为高效采样复合对数凹分布，本文提出一种基于近端梯度的算法，利用 f 的梯度和 g 的受限高斯采样器（RGO）。该算法通过梯度步与 RGO 近端采样结合，实现强凸与光滑条件下 ε 精度的总变差距离收敛，迭代次数为 \widetilde{\mathcal O}(κ\sqrt{d} \log^4(1/ε))，与 g=0 时最优结果持平，并扩展至满足庞卡莱或对数索博列夫不等式的非对数凹分布，以及 Lipschitz 但非光滑的 f 函数情形。
Multi-Stream LLMs: Unblocking Language Models with Parallel Streams of Thoughts, Inputs and Outputs
Authors: Guinan Su, Yanwu Yang, Xueyan Li, Jonas Geiping
First: 2026-05-12T17:47:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:47:41+00:00
Comments: Preprint, 37 pages. Code at https://github.com/seal-rg/streaming/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
The continued improvements in language model capability have unlocked their widespread use as drivers of autonomous agents, for example in coding or computer use applications. However, the core of these systems has not changed much since early instruction-tuned models like ChatGPT. Even advanced AI agents function on message exchange formats, successively exchanging messages with users, systems, with itself (i.e. chain-of-thought) and tools in a single stream of computation. This bottleneck to a single stream in chat models leads to a number of limitations: the agent cannot act (generate output) while reading, and in reverse, cannot react to new information while writing. Similarly, the agent cannot act while thinking and cannot think while reading or acting on information. In this work, we show that models can be unblocked by switching from instruction-tuning for sequential message formats to instruction-tuning for multiple, parallel streams of computation, splitting each role into a separate stream. Every forward pass of the language model then simultaneously reads from multiple input streams and generates tokens in multiple output streams, all of which causally depend on earlier timesteps. We argue that this data-driven change remedies a number of usability limitations as outlined above, improves model efficiency through parallelization, improves model security through better separation of concerns and can further improve model monitorability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：多流大语言模型：通过并行思维、输入与输出流解除语言模型瓶颈
语言模型能力的持续提升已使其广泛应用于自主代理的驱动，例如编程或计算机使用场景。然而，这些系统的内核自早期指令调优模型（如ChatGPT）以来变化不大。即使先进的AI代理仍基于消息交换格式，在单一流计算中依次与用户、系统、自身（即思维链）及工具交换消息。这种聊天模型中单一流计算的瓶颈导致诸多限制：代理在阅读时无法行动（生成输出），反之亦然，在写作时无法响应新信息；同样，代理在思考时无法行动，或在阅读/处理信息时无法思考。 本研究证明，通过将指令调优从顺序消息格式转向多并行计算流的指令调优，将每个角色拆分为独立流，可解除模型瓶颈。此时，语言模型的每次前向传播同时从多个输入流读取并生成多个输出流的标记，所有操作均因果依赖于先前时间步。我们认为，这种数据驱动的改变可解决上述诸多可用性限制，通过并行化提升模型效率，通过职责分离增强模型安全性，并进一步提升模型可监控性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of single-stream language models in autonomous agents—such as inability to act while reading or think while writing—the authors propose Multi-Stream LLMs, which restructure instruction-tuning to handle parallel streams of thoughts, inputs, and outputs. Each forward pass simultaneously processes multiple input streams and generates tokens across multiple output streams, with causal dependencies maintained across timesteps. Experimental results demonstrate that this architecture improves agent efficiency through parallel computation, enhances security via role separation, and boosts monitorability, while overcoming the sequential bottleneck inherent in traditional chat-based models.
为解决单流语言模型在自主代理中因串行处理导致的无法边读边写、边思考边行动等局限性，该研究提出多流LLM架构，使模型能同时处理思想流、输入流与输出流。通过在推理时并行处理多个独立流，模型实现了读、思、作的并发能力，突破了传统串行瓶颈。实验表明，该架构提升了代理效率、通过职责分离增强了安全性，并改善了可监控性，同时保持或提升了下游任务表现。
Learning What Can Be Picked: Active Reachability Estimation for Efficient Robotic Fruit Harvesting
Authors: Nur Afsa Syeda, Mohamed Elmahallawy, Luis Fernando de la Torre, John Miller
First: 2026-03-24T19:35:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:47:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Agriculture remains a cornerstone of global health and economic sustainability, yet labor-intensive tasks such as harvesting high-value crops continue to face growing workforce shortages. Robotic harvesting systems offer a promising solution; however, their deployment in unstructured orchard environments is constrained by inefficient perception-to-action pipelines. In particular, existing approaches often rely on exhaustive inverse kinematics or motion planning to determine whether a target fruit is reachable, leading to unnecessary computation and delayed decision-making. Our approach combines RGB-D perception with active learning to directly learn reachability as a binary decision problem. We then leverage active learning to selectively query the most informative samples for reachability labeling, significantly reducing annotation effort while maintaining high predictive accuracy. Extensive experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves accurate reachability prediction with substantially fewer labeled samples, yielding approximately 6--8% higher accuracy than random sampling and enabling label-efficient adaptation to new orchard configurations. Among the evaluated strategies, entropy- and margin-based sampling outperform Query-by-Committee and standard uncertainty sampling in low-label regimes, while all strategies converge to comparable performance as the labeled set grows. These results highlight the effectiveness of active learning for task-level perception in agricultural robotics and position our approach as a scalable alternative to computation-heavy kinematic reachability analysis. Our code is available through https://github.com/wsu-cyber-security-lab-ai/active-learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习可采摘性：用于高效机器人水果采摘的主动可达性估计
农业仍是全球健康与经济可持续性的基石，但高价值作物的采摘等劳动密集型任务正面临日益严峻的人力短缺。机器人采摘系统提供了有前景的解决方案，但在非结构化果园环境中部署时，受限于感知到行动的低效管道。现有方法常依赖耗时的逆运动学或运动规划来判断目标果实是否可及，导致不必要的计算和决策延迟。我们的方法结合RGB-D感知与主动学习，直接将可达性建模为二元决策问题，并利用主动学习选择最具信息量的样本进行可达性标注，显著减少标注工作量同时保持高预测精度。大量实验表明，该框架在显著减少标注样本的情况下实现准确的可达性预测，比随机采样高约6-8%，并能高效适应新果园配置。在评估策略中，熵与边际采样在低标注环境下优于Query-by-Committee和标准不确定性采样，而所有策略在标注集扩大时均收敛至相近性能。结果凸显了主动学习在农业机器人任务级感知中的有效性，使我们的方法成为计算密集型运动学可达性分析的可扩展替代方案。代码见https://github.com/wsu-cyber-security-lab-ai/active-learning。
