Driven by labor shortages in agriculture and inefficiencies in robotic fruit harvesting systems, this work proposes an active learning framework that directly learns reachability as a binary classification task using RGB-D perception. By selectively querying the most informative samples for labeling, the method drastically reduces annotation effort while preserving high accuracy. Experimental results show the approach achieves 6–8% higher accuracy than random sampling and converges to comparable performance across sampling strategies as labeled data increases, demonstrating its efficiency and scalability for real-world orchard environments.
为应对农业劳动力短缺和机器人采摘中感知到动作的低效问题，该研究提出一种基于主动学习的框架，利用RGB-D感知直接学习可达性二分类任务。通过主动选择最具信息量的样本进行标注，显著降低标注成本同时保持高精度。实验表明，该方法比随机采样高6–8%的准确率，在标注样本较少时熵与边界采样策略优于其他方法，随着标注数据增加各策略性能趋同，证明其在农业机器人任务感知中可替代传统计算密集型运动规划。
TextSeal: A Localized LLM Watermark for Provenance & Distillation Protection
Authors: Tom Sander, Hongyan Chang, Tomáš Souček, Tuan Tran, Valeriu Lacatusu, Sylvestre-Alvise Rebuffi, Alexandre Mourachko, Surya Parimi, Christophe Ropers, Rashel Moritz, Vanessa Stark, Hady Elsahar, Pierre Fernandez
First: 2026-05-12T17:44:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:44:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce TextSeal, a state-of-the-art watermark for large language models. Building on Gumbel-max sampling, TextSeal introduces dual-key generation to restore output diversity, along with entropy-weighted scoring and multi-region localization for improved detection. It supports serving optimizations such as speculative decoding and multi-token prediction, and does not add any inference overhead. TextSeal strictly dominates baselines like SynthID-text in detection strength and is robust to dilution, maintaining confident localized detection even in heavily mixed human/AI documents. The scheme is theoretically distortion-free, and evaluation across reasoning benchmarks confirms that it preserves downstream performance; while a multilingual human evaluation (6000 A/B comparisons, 5 languages) shows no perceptible quality difference. Beyond its use for provenance detection, TextSeal is also ``radioactive'': its watermark signal transfers through model distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TextSeal：一种用于溯源与蒸馏保护的本地化大语言模型水印
我们提出了TextSeal，一种先进的大语言模型水印方案。基于Gumbel-max采样，TextSeal引入双密钥生成以恢复输出多样性，并结合熵加权评分与多区域定位以提升检测效果。支持推测解码与多令牌预测等服务优化，且不增加任何推理开销。TextSeal在检测强度上严格优于SynthID-text等基线方法，对稀释具有鲁棒性，即使在人类与AI文本高度混合的文档中仍能保持可靠的局部检测。该方案理论上无失真，跨推理基准的评估证实其保持下游性能；而6000组A/B对比的多语言人工评估（5种语言）显示无明显质量差异。除用于溯源检测外，TextSeal还具有“放射性”特性：其水印信号可通过模型蒸馏传递，从而实现对未经授权使用的检测。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to protect LLM provenance and prevent unauthorized distillation, TextSeal introduces a localized watermarking scheme using Gumbel-max sampling with dual-key generation to preserve output diversity, entropy-weighted scoring, and multi-region localization for robust detection. It supports efficient serving via speculative decoding and multi-token prediction without inference overhead. Experiments show it outperforms SynthID-text in detection strength and remains effective even in heavily mixed human-AI text, while preserving downstream task performance and perceptual quality across five languages in human evaluations. Additionally, its watermark transfers through distillation, enabling detection of unauthorized model usage.
为保护大语言模型的溯源信息并防止未经授权的蒸馏，TextSeal提出一种基于Gumbel-max采样、双密钥生成、熵加权评分和多区域定位的局部水印方案，以保持输出多样性并提升检测鲁棒性。该方案支持推测解码和多token预测，无推理开销。实验表明其检测能力显著优于SynthID-text，即使在人类与AI文本高度混合的场景下仍能稳定检测，同时在下游任务性能和五语言6000人主观评测中无明显质量下降。此外，其水印信号可传递至蒸馏模型，实现对非法使用行为的检测。
AI Native Asset Intelligence
Authors: Gal Engelberg, Leon Goldberg, Konstantin Koutsyi, Boris Plotnikov, Tiltan Gilat, Ben Benhemo
First: 2026-05-09T18:56:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:44:26+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables. Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern security environments generate fragmented signals across cloud resources, identities, configurations, and third-party security tools. Although AI-native security assistants improve access to this data, they remain largely reactive: users must ask the right questions and interpret disconnected findings. This does not scale in enterprise environments, where signal importance depends on exposure, exploitability, dependencies, and business context. Repeated AI queries may therefore produce unstable prioritization without a structured basis for comparing assets. This paper introduces AI-native asset intelligence, a framework that transforms heterogeneous security data into a structured intelligence layer for consistent, contextual, and proactive asset-level reasoning. The framework combines a modeling layer, representing assets, identities, relationships, controls, attack vectors, and blast-radius patterns, with a scoring layer that converts fragmented signals into a normalized measure of asset importance. The scoring system separates intrinsic exposure, based on misconfigurations and attack-vector evidence, from contextual importance, based on anomaly, blast radius, business criticality, and data criticality. AI contextualization refines severity and business/data classifications, while deterministic aggregation preserves consistency. We evaluate the scoring system on a production snapshot with 131,625 resources across 15 vendors and 178 asset types. Sensitivity analyses and ablations show that severity mappings control finding sensitivity, AI severity adjustment refines prioritization, attack-vector scoring responds to rare exploitability evidence, and contextual modulation selectively modifies exposed resources based on business or data importance. The results support AI-native asset intelligence as a foundation for stable prioritization and proactive security-posture reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：原生AI资产智能
现代安全环境在云资源、身份、配置和第三方安全工具中产生分散的信号。尽管原生AI安全助手提升了对这些数据的访问，但它们仍主要处于被动响应状态：用户必须提出正确的问题并解读分散的发现。在企业环境中，这种模式难以扩展，因为信号的重要性取决于暴露面、可利用性、依赖关系和业务背景。重复的AI查询可能因此导致资产优先级不稳定，缺乏结构化基础来比较资产。本文提出原生AI资产智能框架，将异构安全数据转化为结构化智能层，以实现一致、上下文相关和主动的资产级推理。该框架结合建模层（表示资产、身份、关系、控制措施、攻击路径和影响范围模式）与评分层（将分散信号转化为资产重要性的标准化度量）。评分系统将内在暴露（基于配置错误和攻击路径证据）与上下文重要性（基于异常、影响范围、业务关键性和数据关键性）分离。AI上下文化可优化严重性和业务/数据分类，而确定性聚合则确保一致性。我们在包含15家厂商、178类资产、131,625个资源的生产快照上评估评分系统。敏感性分析和消融实验表明：严重性映射控制发现敏感性，AI严重性调整优化优先级，攻击路径评分响应罕见可利用性证据，上下文调制根据业务或数据重要性选择性调整暴露资源。结果支持原生AI资产智能作为稳定优先级和主动安全态势推理的基础。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to manage fragmented security signals across cloud and third-party tools, this paper proposes AI-native asset intelligence—a framework that structures heterogeneous security data into a consistent, context-aware intelligence layer for proactive asset prioritization. It combines a modeling layer capturing assets, identities, relationships, and attack vectors with a scoring layer that normalizes asset importance by separating intrinsic exposure (from misconfigurations and attack evidence) from contextual factors (anomaly, blast radius, business/data criticality). Evaluated on 131,625 production resources across 15 vendors, the system demonstrates that severity mappings govern sensitivity, AI refines prioritization, attack-vector scoring responds to rare exploitability, and contextual modulation adjusts exposure based on business relevance—enabling stable, scalable, and proactive security posture reasoning.
为解决当前AI原生安全工具碎片化且被动响应的问题，本文提出AI原生资产智能框架，以在复杂企业环境中实现资产的稳定、主动优先级排序。该方法结合建模层（结构化资产、身份、关系、攻击路径等）与评分层（将资产重要性分为内在暴露度与上下文重要度），前者基于配置错误与攻击证据，后者基于异常、影响范围、业务与数据关键性。AI上下文化优化严重性与分类，确定性聚合保障一致性。在15家厂商、178类资产、131,625个资源的生产环境中评估显示，严重性映射控制敏感度，AI调整优化优先级，攻击路径评分响应罕见可利用性，上下文调制按业务或数据重要性选择性调整暴露资产。
Enabling AI-Native Mobility in 6G: A Real-World Dataset for Handover, Beam Management, and Timing Advance
Authors: Mannam Veera Narayana, Rohit Singh, Deepa M. R, Radha Krishna Ganti
First: 2026-05-12T17:43:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:43:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
To address the issues of high interruption time and measurement report overhead under user equipment (UE) mobility especially in high speed 5G use cases the use of AI/ML techniques (AI/ML beam management and mobility procedures) have been proposed. These techniques rely heavily on data that are most often simulated for various scenarios and do not accurately reflect real deployment behavior or user traffic patterns. Therefore, there is an utmost need for realistic datasets under various conditions. This work presents a dataset collected from a commercially deployed network across various modes of mobility (pedestrian, bike, car, bus, and train) and at multiple speeds to depict real time UE mobility. When collecting the dataset, we focused primarily on handover (HO) scenarios, with the aim of reducing the HO interruption time and maintaining continuous throughput during and immediately after HO execution. To support this research, the dataset includes timing advance (TA) measurements at various signaling events such as RACH trigger, MAC CE, and PDCCH grant which are typically missing in existing works. We cover a detailed description of the creation of the dataset; experimental setup, data acquisition, and extraction. We also cover an exploratory analysis of the data, with a primary focus on mobility, beam management, and TA. We discuss multiple use cases in which the proposed dataset can facilitate understanding of the inference of the AI/ML model. One such use case is to train and evaluate various AI/ML models for TA prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向6G的AI原生移动性：用于切换、波束管理和定时提前的现实世界数据集
为解决用户设备（UE）在高速5G场景下移动时高中断时间和测量报告开销问题，已提出采用AI/ML技术（如AI/ML波束管理和移动性流程）。这些技术高度依赖数据，而现有数据多为模拟生成，无法准确反映真实部署行为或用户流量模式。因此，亟需在多种条件下获取真实数据集。本工作提供了一个从商业部署网络收集的数据集，涵盖多种移动模式（步行、骑行、汽车、公交车、火车）及多种速度，以真实反映UE实时移动性。数据采集时主要聚焦切换（HO）场景，旨在降低HO中断时间并确保切换期间及切换后持续吞吐量。为支持本研究，数据集包含在RACH触发、MAC CE、PDCCH授权等典型信令事件中的定时提前（TA）测量值，这些在现有研究中通常缺失。我们详细描述了数据集的创建过程，包括实验设置、数据采集与提取。同时，我们对数据进行了探索性分析，重点聚焦移动性、波束管理与TA。我们讨论了该数据集可支持的多种AI/ML模型推理理解用例，例如用于TA预测的AI/ML模型训练与评估。
Summary / 总结
To tackle high interruption times and excessive measurement overhead during user equipment mobility in high-speed 5G scenarios, this work introduces a real-world dataset collected from a commercial network across diverse mobility modes and speeds. The dataset focuses on handover events to minimize interruption and maintain throughput, and includes critical timing advance measurements at key signaling points often absent in prior studies. Experimental analysis reveals mobility patterns, beam management behaviors, and TA dynamics under real deployment conditions, enabling accurate training and evaluation of AI/ML models for predictive tasks such as TA forecasting.
为降低高速5G场景下切换中断时间和测量开销，本文提出一个从商用网络采集的真实世界数据集，涵盖步行、骑行、驾车、公交和列车等多种移动模式及速度。该数据集聚焦切换场景，并包含RACH触发、MAC CE和PDCCH授权等关键信令事件的定时提前测量，填补了现有研究的空白。实验分析揭示了移动性、波束管理和定时行为的规律，支持AI/ML模型训练与评估，例如定时提前预测，从而助力6G移动性管理的智能化演进。
Dispatch-Aware Ragged Attention for Pruned Vision Transformers
Authors: Seifeldin Abdellatif, Ahmad Almasri
First: 2026-04-16T15:48:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:43:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Token pruning methods for Vision Transformers (ViTs) promise quadratic reductions in attention FLOPs by dropping uninformative patches. Yet standard variable-length attention APIs -- including FlashAttention-2's varlen and PyTorch's NestedTensor SDPA -- fail to translate these savings into proportional wall-clock gains at the short post-pruning sequence lengths typical of ViTs ($\leq$197 tokens). We identify a dispatch-overhead bottleneck: at these lengths, host-side kernel dispatch consumes ${\sim}$50\,$μ$s regardless of workload, exceeding the actual GPU compute time at moderate-to-high pruning rates. We present a lightweight bidirectional Triton attention kernel whose dispatch floor is ${\sim}$24\,$μ$s -- roughly 2.17$\times$ lower than FlashAttention-2 varlen -- allowing pruning savings to become visible in wall-clock time. Integrated into a complete pack-attend-unpack pipeline and evaluated on an NVIDIA RTX 4000 Ada Generation GPU, our system achieves 1.88$\times$ end-to-end throughput over padded PyTorch SDPA at standard 224$\times$224 inputs, scaling to 2.51$\times$ at 384$\times$384. Against FlashAttention-2 varlen -- the strongest baseline -- our kernel delivers 9-12\% higher throughput at serving batch sizes (BS=1-4), and 2.17$\times$ lower kernel latency at 80\% token pruning. Numerical correctness is verified with max absolute logit difference $<$0.004 and bit-exact top-1 predictions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向剪枝视觉Transformer的调度感知不规则注意力
视觉Transformer（ViT）的令牌剪枝方法通过移除无信息的补丁，有望实现注意力FLOPs的二次方级减少。然而，标准的可变长度注意力API（包括FlashAttention-2的varlen和PyTorch的NestedTensor SDPA）在ViT典型的短序列长度（≤197个令牌）下，无法将这些节省转化为成比例的壁钟时间收益。我们识别出调度开销瓶颈：在这些长度下，主机侧内核调度消耗约50μs，无论工作负载如何，超过中高剪枝率下的实际GPU计算时间。我们提出了一种轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，其调度下限约为24μs——比FlashAttention-2 varlen低约2.17倍，从而使剪枝节省在壁钟时间中显现。该内核集成于完整的打包-注意-解包流水线，并在NVIDIA RTX 4000 Ada Generation GPU上评估，于标准224×224输入下，端到端吞吐量比填充PyTorch SDPA提升1.88倍，384×384输入下提升至2.51倍。与最强基线FlashAttention-2 varlen相比，我们的内核在服务批大小（BS=1-4）下吞吐量高9-12%，在80%令牌剪枝下内核延迟低2.17倍。数值正确性通过最大绝对logit差值<0.004及位精确的top-1预测验证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of standard variable-length attention APIs in translating token pruning savings into real-time performance gains for Vision Transformers, the paper introduces a lightweight bidirectional Triton attention kernel designed to reduce dispatch overhead. At short sequence lengths typical of ViTs, traditional kernels suffer from high dispatch latency (~50μs), which outweighs GPU compute time at moderate pruning rates. The new kernel reduces this dispatch floor to ~24μs, enabling measurable wall-clock speedups. Evaluated on an NVIDIA RTX 4000 Ada GPU, the system achieves 1.88x throughput over padded PyTorch SDPA at 224x224 inputs and scales to 2.51x at 384x384. Against FlashAttention-2 varlen, it delivers 9–12% higher throughput at small batch sizes and 2.17x lower kernel latency at 80% pruning, with numerical correctness confirmed via small logit differences and bit-exact top-1 predictions.
为了解决视觉Transformer中令牌剪枝虽能减少计算量但无法转化为实际运行时间提升的问题，本文提出一种轻量级双向Triton注意力内核，旨在降低调度开销。在典型ViT序列长度（≤197个令牌）下，传统内核因调度延迟（约50μs）掩盖了剪枝带来的计算节省。新内核将调度延迟降至约24μs，使剪枝收益得以体现。在NVIDIA RTX 4000 Ada GPU上测试，该系统在224x224输入下比填充PyTorch SDPA快1.88倍，384x384时提升至2.51倍。相比FlashAttention-2 varlen，其在小批量（BS=1-4）下吞吐量高9–12%，在80%剪枝率下内核延迟低2.17倍，且数值正确性通过极小对数损失和完全一致的Top-1预测验证。
Stochastic tensor space feature theory with applications to robust machine learning
Authors: Julio Enrique Castrillon-Candas, Kaili Shi, Dingning Liu, Sicheng Yang, Xiaoling Zhang, Mark Kon, the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative
First: 2021-10-04T22:01:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:42:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this paper we develop a Multilevel Orthogonal Subspace (MOS) Karhunen-Loeve feature theory based on stochastic tensor spaces, for the construction of robust machine learning features. Training data are treated as instances of a random field within a relevant Bochner space. Our key observation is that separate machine learning classes can reside predominantly in mostly distinct subspaces. Using the Karhunen-Loeve expansion and a hierarchical expansion of the first (nominal) class, a MOS is constructed to detect anomalous signal components, treating the second class as an outlier of the first. The projection coefficients of the input data into these subspaces are then used to train a Machine Learning (ML) classifier. These coefficients become new features from which much clearer separation surfaces can arise for the underlying classes. Tests in the blood plasma dataset (Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative) show dramatic increases in accuracy. This contrast to popular ML methods such as Gradient Boosting, RUS Boost, Random Forest and Neural Networks. We show that with a non-invasive blood test, high-accuracy results can be obtained for predicting AD stages such as cognitive normal, mild cognitive impairment and dementia.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于随机张量空间的随机张量特征理论及其在鲁棒机器学习中的应用
本文提出了一种基于随机张量空间的多级正交子空间（MOS）Karhunen-Loeve特征理论，用于构建鲁棒机器学习特征。将训练数据视为相关Bochner空间内随机场的实例。我们的关键观察是，不同机器学习类别主要分布在几乎互异的子空间中。利用Karhunen-Loeve展开及第一类（名义类）的分层展开，构建MOS以检测异常信号成分，将第二类视为第一类的离群值。随后，将输入数据投影到这些子空间的系数用于训练机器学习（ML）分类器。这些系数成为新特征，可产生更清晰的类别分离面。在血浆数据集（阿尔茨海默病神经影像倡议）上的测试显示准确率显著提升，优于主流ML方法如梯度提升、RUS Boost、随机森林和神经网络。我们证明，通过无创血液检测，可高精度预测阿尔茨海默病各阶段，如认知正常、轻度认知障碍和痴呆。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust machine learning features that can distinguish complex biological classes, this paper introduces a stochastic tensor space framework based on Multilevel Orthogonal Subspace (MOS) Karhunen-Loeve theory. Training data are modeled as random field instances in a Bochner space, and the method constructs orthogonal subspaces where distinct classes predominantly reside. By expanding the first class hierarchically and treating the second as an outlier, the MOS detects anomalous components, whose projection coefficients serve as new features for classification. Experiments on Alzheimer’s blood plasma data show significantly higher accuracy compared to popular ML methods like Gradient Boosting and Random Forest, enabling precise prediction of cognitive stages using non-invasive blood tests.
为解决在噪声数据中构建鲁棒机器学习特征以区分不同类别的需求，本文提出了一种基于随机张量空间的多层级正交子空间（MOS）Karhunen-Loeve特征理论。该方法将训练数据视为Bochner空间中随机场的实现，并构建正交子空间，使不同类别主要分布于不同子空间。通过层级展开第一类并视第二类为异常，MOS检测异常信号分量。输入数据投影到这些子空间后，其投影系数作为新特征用于训练分类器。在阿尔茨海默病神经影像倡议（ADNI）血浆数据集上的实验表明，该方法在准确率上显著优于梯度提升、RUS Boost、随机森林和神经网络等主流机器学习方法，实现了通过无创血液检测高精度预测阿尔茨海默病不同阶段的能力。
The Algorithmic Caricature: Auditing LLM-Generated Political Discourse Across Crisis Events
Authors: Gunjan, Sidahmed Benabderrahmane, Talal Rahwan
First: 2026-05-12T17:42:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:42:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) can generate fluent political text at scale, raising concerns about synthetic discourse during crises and social conflict. Existing AI-text detection often focuses on sentence-level cues such as perplexity, burstiness, or token irregularities, but these signals may weaken as generative systems improve. We instead adopt a Computational Social Science perspective and ask whether synthetic political discourse behaves like an observed online population. We construct a paired corpus of 1,789,406 posts across nine crisis events: COVID-19, the Jan. 6 Capitol attack, the 2020 and 2024 U.S. elections, Dobbs/Roe v. Wade, the 2020 BLM protests, U.S. midterms, the Utah shooting, and the U.S.-Iran war. For each event, we compare observed discourse from social platforms with synthetic discourse generated for the same context. We evaluate four dimensions: emotional intensity, structural regularity, lexical-ideological framing, and cross-event dependency, using mean gaps and dispersion evidence. Across events, synthetic discourse is fluent but population-level unrealistic. It is generally more negative and less dispersed in sentiment, structurally more regular, and lexically more abstract than observed discourse. Observed discourse instead shows broader emotional variation, longer-tailed structural distributions, and more context-specific, colloquial lexical markers. These differences are event-dependent: larger for fast-moving, decentralized crises and smaller for formal or institutionally mediated events. We summarize them with a simple event-level measure, the Caricature Gap. Our findings suggest that the main limitation of synthetic political discourse is not grammar or fluency, but reduced population realism. Population-level auditing complements traditional text-detection and provides a CSS framework for evaluating the social realism of generated discourse.
中文标题/摘要
标题：算法漫画：审计大语言模型在危机事件中生成的政治话语
大语言模型（LLMs）可大规模生成流畅的政治文本，引发人们对危机与社会冲突期间合成话语的担忧。现有AI文本检测多聚焦于句子层面的线索，如困惑度、突发性或令牌异常，但这些信号可能随着生成系统改进而减弱。我们转而采用计算社会科学视角，探讨合成政治话语是否与在线真实人群行为相似。我们构建了一个包含九个危机事件（COVID-19、1月6日国会山袭击、2020年和2024年美国大选、多布斯/罗诉韦德案、2020年BLM抗议、美国中期选举、犹他州枪击案、美伊战争）的178.9万条配对语料库。对每个事件，我们比较社交平台上的真实话语与针对相同情境生成的合成话语。从四个维度评估：情感强度、结构规律性、词汇-意识形态框架、跨事件依赖性，使用均值差距与分布证据。总体而言，合成话语流畅但缺乏人群层面的真实性：情感上更负面、分布更集中，结构更规律，词汇更抽象；真实话语则情感更广泛、结构分布更长尾、词汇更具体口语化。这些差异事件依赖：快速分散的危机事件差异更大，正式或机构调解事件差异较小。我们用“漫画差距”这一简单事件级指标总结。研究发现，合成政治话语的主要局限并非语法或流畅性，而是人群真实性的降低。人群层面审计可补充传统文本检测，为评估生成话语的社会现实性提供CSS框架。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns that large language models may produce misleading political discourse during crises, this study shifts focus from traditional sentence-level detection to a Computational Social Science lens, comparing synthetic LLM-generated political text with real-world social media discourse across nine major events. Using a paired corpus of 1.7 million posts, the researchers evaluate four dimensions—emotional intensity, structural regularity, lexical-ideological framing, and cross-event dependency—finding synthetic text tends to be more negative, structurally uniform, and lexically abstract, while real discourse exhibits greater emotional diversity, structural variability, and context-specific language. These differences are more pronounced in fast-moving, decentralized crises and less so in formal events, summarized via a new metric, the Caricature Gap, indicating that LLMs’ main limitation lies not in fluency but in failing to replicate population-level social realism.
鉴于大语言模型在危机期间可能生成误导性政治话语，本研究转而采用计算社会科学视角，考察合成政治文本是否能反映真实在线人群特征。研究构建了涵盖九个危机事件的179万条配对文本语料库，对比LLM生成内容与真实社交媒体文本。从情感强度、结构规律性、词汇意识形态框架和跨事件依赖性四个维度评估，发现合成文本普遍更负面、结构更规整、词汇更抽象，而真实文本则情感更丰富、结构更长尾、语言更口语化、具情境性。差异程度因事件而异，快节奏、去中心化危机中更显著。研究提出“漫画差距”作为衡量合成文本社会现实感缺失的简单指标，结论是合成政治话语的核心缺陷在于缺乏社会现实感，而非语法流畅性。
FuTCR: Future-Targeted Contrast and Repulsion for Continual Panoptic Segmentation
Authors: Nicholas Ikechukwu, Keanu Nichols, Deepti Ghadiyaram, Bryan A. Plummer
First: 2026-05-12T17:41:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:41:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Continual Panoptic Segmentation (CPS) requires methods that can quickly adapt to new categories over time. The nature of this dense prediction task means that training images may contain a mix of labeled and unlabeled objects. As nothing is known about these unlabeled objects a priori, existing methods often simply group any unlabeled pixel into a single "background" class during training. In effect, during training, they repeatedly tell the model that all the different background categories are the same (even when they aren't). This makes learning to identify different background categories as they are added challenging since these new categories may require using information the model was previously told was unimportant and ignored. Thus, we propose a Future-Targeted Contrastive and Repulsive (FuTCR) framework that addresses this limitation by restructuring representations before new classes are introduced. FuTCR first discovers confident future-like regions by grouping model-predicted masks whose pixels are consistently classified as background but exhibit non-background logits. Next, FuTCR applies pixel-to-region contrast to build coherent prototypes from these unlabeled regions, while simultaneously repelling background features away from known-class prototypes to explicitly reserve representational space for future categories. Experiments across six CPS settings and a range of dataset sizes show FuTCR improves relative new-class panoptic quality over the state-of-the-art by up to 28%, while preserving or improving base-class performance with gains up to 4%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FuTCR：面向未来的对比与排斥机制用于持续全景分割
持续全景分割（CPS）需要能够随时间快速适应新类别的方法。由于该密集预测任务的特性，训练图像中可能包含标注和未标注物体的混合。由于对这些未标注物体事先一无所知，现有方法通常在训练时将所有未标注像素简单归入单一“背景”类别。实际上，这相当于反复告诉模型所有不同的背景类别都是相同的（即使它们并非如此）。这使得学习识别新增的背景类别变得困难，因为这些新类别可能需要利用模型此前被指示忽略的信息。因此，我们提出了一种面向未来的对比与排斥（FuTCR）框架，通过在引入新类别前重构表示来解决这一限制。FuTCR 首先通过将模型预测掩码中像素被一致分类为背景但具有非背景logits的区域进行分组，发现具有未来相似性的置信区域。接着，FuTCR 通过像素到区域的对比构建来自这些未标注区域的连贯原型，同时将背景特征排斥远离已知类别原型，以明确为未来类别保留表示空间。在六个CPS设置和多种数据集规模上的实验表明，FuTCR 相较于当前最优方法在新类别全景质量上提升最高达28%，同时保持或提升了基础类别性能，提升最高达4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge that existing continual panoptic segmentation methods collapse unlabeled background regions into a single class, hindering learning of new background categories, FuTCR introduces a future-targeted contrastive and repulsive framework. It first identifies confident future-like regions by grouping model-predicted masks with consistent background labels but non-background logits. Then, it applies pixel-to-region contrast to form coherent prototypes from these regions while repelling background features from known-class prototypes to reserve space for future categories. Experiments across six settings and varying dataset sizes show FuTCR improves new-class panoptic quality by up to 28% over state-of-the-art methods, while maintaining or enhancing base-class performance by up to 4%.
为了解决现有连续全景分割方法将未知背景区域统一归类导致新背景类别难以学习的问题，FuTCR提出一种面向未来的对比与排斥框架。该方法首先通过聚合模型预测的、被标记为背景但具有非背景logits的掩码，识别出潜在未来区域；随后通过像素到区域的对比学习构建这些区域的原型，同时将背景特征与已知类别原型排斥，为未来类别预留表征空间。在六个实验设置和不同数据规模下，FuTCR相较当前最优方法将新类别全景质量提升最高达28%，同时保持或提升基础类别性能最高达4%。
LychSim: A Controllable and Interactive Simulation Framework for Vision Research
Authors: Wufei Ma, Chloe Wang, Siyi Chen, Jiawei Peng, Patrick Li, Alan Yuille
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:40:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:40:38+00:00
Comments: 3D-LLM/VLA Workshop at CVPR 2026. Project page: https://lychsim.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While self-supervised pretraining has reduced vision systems' reliance on synthetic data, simulation remains an indispensable tool for closed-loop optimization and rigorous out-of-distribution (OOD) evaluation. However, modern simulation platforms often present steep technical barriers, requiring extensive expertise in computer graphics and game development. In this work, we present LychSim, a highly controllable and interactive simulation framework built upon Unreal Engine 5 to bridge this gap. LychSim is built around three key designs: (1) a streamlined Python API that abstracts away underlying engine complexities; (2) a procedural data pipeline capable of generating diverse, high-fidelity environments with varying out-of-distribution (OOD) visual challenges, paired with rich 2D and 3D ground truths; and (3) a native integration of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) that transforms the simulator into a dynamic, closed-loop playground for reasoning agentic LLMs. We further annotate scene-level procedural rules and object-level pose alignments to enable semantically aligned 3D ground truths and automated scene modification. We demonstrate LychSim's capability across multiple downstream applications, including serving as a synthetic data engine, powering reinforcement learning-based adversarial examiners, and facilitating interactive, language-driven scene layout generation. To benefit the broader vision community, LychSim will be made publicly available, including full source code and various data annotations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LychSim：面向视觉研究的可控交互式仿真框架
尽管自监督预训练减少了视觉系统对合成数据的依赖，仿真仍是闭环优化和严格分布外（OOD）评估不可或缺的工具。然而，现代仿真平台常存在较高的技术门槛，需精通计算机图形学与游戏开发。本文提出LychSim，基于Unreal Engine 5构建，旨在弥合这一差距。LychSim包含三大核心设计：（1）简化Python API，屏蔽底层引擎复杂性；（2）程序化数据管道，可生成多样、高保真环境及不同OOD视觉挑战，搭配丰富2D/3D真实标注；（3）原生集成模型上下文协议（MCP），将仿真器转化为支持推理代理LLM的动态闭环实验场。我们进一步标注场景级程序规则与物体级姿态对齐，实现语义一致的3D真实标注与自动化场景修改。我们展示了LychSim在多个下游应用中的能力，包括作为合成数据引擎、驱动基于强化学习的对抗性评估器，以及支持交互式语言驱动场景布局生成。为惠及更广泛的视觉研究社区，LychSim将公开源码及多种数据标注。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accessible simulation tools to support vision research despite the rise of self-supervised pretraining, LychSim introduces a controllable and interactive framework built on Unreal Engine 5. It employs a simplified Python API to abstract engine complexity, a procedural pipeline for generating diverse and high-fidelity environments with varied out-of-distribution visual challenges, and native Model Context Protocol integration to enable closed-loop agent reasoning. Experimental results show LychSim effectively supports synthetic data generation, adversarial RL-based evaluation, and language-driven scene layout, with annotated procedural rules and pose alignments enabling semantically consistent 3D ground truths and automated scene modification.
为应对自监督预训练兴起后仍需模拟环境进行闭环优化与OOD评估的现实，LychSim基于Unreal Engine 5构建了一个可控制且交互式的仿真框架。其通过简化Python API、可生成多样OOD环境并提供丰富2D/3D标注的程序化数据管道，以及原生MCP集成以支持代理推理闭环，实现了易用性与功能性的平衡。实验表明，该框架可有效支持合成数据生成、基于强化学习的对抗性评估及语言驱动的场景布局生成，展现出广泛适用性。
ORCE: Order-Aware Alignment of Verbalized Confidence in Large Language Models
Authors: Chen Li, Xiaoling Hu, Songzhu Zheng, Jiawei Zhou, Chao Chen
First: 2026-05-12T17:39:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:39:43+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) often produce answers with high certainty even when they are incorrect, making reliable confidence estimation essential for deployment in real-world scenarios. Verbalized confidence, where models explicitly state their confidence in natural language, provides a flexible and user-facing uncertainty signal that can be applied even when token logits are unavailable. However, existing verbalized-confidence methods often optimize answer generation and confidence generation jointly, which can cause confidence-alignment objectives to interfere with answer accuracy. In this work, we propose a decoupled and order-aware framework for verbalized confidence calibration. Our method first generates an answer and then estimates confidence conditioned on the fixed question--answer pair, allowing confidence optimization without directly perturbing the answer-generation process. To align confidence with correctness likelihood, we construct a sampling-based surrogate from multiple model completions and optimize rank-based reinforcement learning objectives that encourage responses with higher estimated correctness likelihood to receive higher verbalized confidence. Experiments on reasoning and knowledge-intensive benchmarks show that our method improves calibration and failure prediction performance while largely preserving answer accuracy. These results demonstrate that verbalized confidence can be more reliably aligned by decoupling confidence estimation from answer generation and optimizing the relative ordering of confidence across responses.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORCE：大语言模型中语言化置信度的有序对齐
大语言模型（LLMs）常在错误时仍输出高置信度答案，因此在实际部署中可靠置信度估计至关重要。语言化置信度使模型以自然语言明确表达置信度，提供灵活且面向用户的不确定性信号，即使在无法获取token logits时亦可应用。然而，现有方法常联合优化答案生成与置信度生成，导致置信度对齐目标干扰答案准确性。本文提出一种解耦且有序感知的语言化置信度校准框架：首先生成答案，再基于固定问题-答案对估计置信度，使置信度优化不直接干扰答案生成过程。为使置信度与正确性概率对齐，我们构建基于多模型补全的采样代理，并优化基于排序的强化学习目标，鼓励高正确性概率估计的响应获得更高语言化置信度。在推理与知识密集型基准测试中，实验表明该方法提升校准与失败预测性能，同时基本保持答案准确性。结果表明，解耦置信度估计与答案生成，并优化置信度在响应间的相对排序，可更可靠地对齐语言化置信度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable confidence estimation in large language models to avoid overconfident incorrect answers, this work introduces ORCE, a decoupled and order-aware framework for verbalized confidence calibration. It first generates an answer and then estimates confidence conditioned on the fixed question-answer pair, preventing interference between confidence and answer optimization. Confidence is aligned with correctness likelihood via a sampling-based surrogate and rank-based reinforcement learning, encouraging higher confidence for more likely correct responses. Experiments show improved calibration and failure prediction while preserving answer accuracy across reasoning and knowledge-intensive benchmarks.
为解决大语言模型在错误答案上过度自信的问题，本文提出ORCE框架，通过解耦答案生成与置信度估计，实现对语言化置信度的精准校准。该方法先生成答案，再基于固定问答对估计置信度，并通过采样构建代理目标和排序强化学习优化置信度与正确性关联。实验表明，该方法显著提升校准与失败预测能力，同时保持答案准确性。
Local and Mixing-Based Algorithms for Gaussian Graphical Model Selection from Glauber Dynamics
Authors: Vignesh Tirukkonda, Anirudh Rayas, Gautam Dasarathy
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2024-12-24T18:49:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:35:05+00:00
Comments: Major revision. Corrects the earlier local ratio-estimator analysis by replacing it with a local product estimator; adds a burn-in/thinning estimator based on total-variation decoupling for Gaussian Gibbs samplers; strengthens the lower bounds; adds experiments; and compares with the related ICML 2026 work of Shen, Wu, Majid, and Moitra
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Gaussian graphical model selection is usually studied under independent sampling, but in many applications observations arise from dependent dynamics. We study structure learning when the data consist of a single trajectory of Gaussian Glauber dynamics. We develop two complementary approaches. The first is a local edge-testing estimator based on an appropriately designed correlation test that reveals edges. This estimator does not require waiting for the chain to mix and admits an embarrassingly parallel edgewise implementation. The second is a burn-in/thinning reduction: under a Dobrushin contraction condition, we prove that a suitably subsampled Gaussian Gibbs trajectory is close in total variation to an i.i.d. product sample, allowing standard i.i.d. Gaussian graphical model learners to be used as black boxes. The key technical ingredient, which may be of independent interest, is a high-dimensional total-variation bound for random-scan Gaussian Gibbs samplers, obtained by combining Wasserstein contraction with an approximate Lipschitz smoothing argument. We prove finite-sample recovery guarantees for both approaches, establish information-theoretic lower bounds on the observation time, and empirically compare the resulting sample-computation tradeoffs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于局部与混合的高斯图模型选择算法：来自Glauber动力学的研究
高斯图模型选择通常在独立采样下研究，但在许多应用中观测数据来自依赖动力学。我们研究当数据由单条高斯Glauber动力学轨迹组成时的结构学习问题。我们提出两种互补方法：第一种是基于适当设计的相关性检验的局部边检测估计器，可揭示边结构，无需等待马尔可夫链混合，且支持 embarrassingly parallel 的边级并行实现；第二种是烧入/稀疏化降维方法：在Dobrushin收缩条件下，我们证明适当子采样的高斯Gibbs轨迹在总变差距离上接近独立同分布的乘积样本，从而可将标准独立同分布高斯图模型学习器作为黑盒使用。关键技术贡献（可能独立有趣）是通过结合Wasserstein收缩与近似Lipschitz平滑论证，获得随机扫描高斯Gibbs采样器的高维总变差界。我们为两种方法证明了有限样本恢复保证，建立了观测时间的信息论下界，并实证比较了所得的样本-计算权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to learn Gaussian graphical models from dependent data arising in Glauber dynamics, this paper proposes two complementary algorithms: a local edge-testing estimator that identifies edges via correlation tests without requiring mixing, enabling embarrassingly parallel computation, and a burn-in/thinning reduction that, under Dobrushin contraction, transforms a Gaussian Gibbs trajectory into a sample close to i.i.d. in total variation, allowing standard i.i.d. learners to be applied. Both methods are theoretically grounded with finite-sample recovery guarantees, information-theoretic lower bounds on observation time, and empirical comparisons showing favorable sample-computation tradeoffs.
为了解决从Glauber动力学产生的依赖数据中学习高斯图模型的问题，本文提出两种互补方法：一种是基于相关性检验的局部边检测估计器，无需等待链混合即可识别边，支持并行计算；另一种是烧入/稀疏采样方法，在Dobrushin收缩条件下，将高斯Gibbs轨迹转化为与独立同分布样本在总变差距离上接近的样本，从而可直接使用标准独立同分布图模型学习器。两种方法均具备有限样本恢复保证、观测时间的信息下界，并通过实验比较了样本量与计算成本之间的权衡。
A Causal Language Modeling Detour Improves Encoder Continued Pretraining
Authors: Rian Touchent, Eric de la Clergerie
First: 2026-05-12T17:34:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:34:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When adapting an encoder to a new domain, the standard approach is to continue training with Masked Language Modeling (MLM). We show that temporarily switching to Causal Language Modeling (CLM) followed by a short MLM decay improves downstream performance. On biomedical texts with ModernBERT, this CLM detour outperforms MLM baselines trained on identical data and compute across 8 French and 11 English biomedical tasks, by +1.2-2.8pp and +0.3-0.8pp respectively, depending on model size. We investigate the reasons for these gains. We find that CLM's dense supervision impacts low transformer layers (0-7) far more than MLM does. Freezing low layers during CLM eliminates the downstream benefit; freezing mid layers preserves it. The representational changes persist through the MLM decay phase, even when it matches the CLM phase in length, and they scale with model capacity. We release ModernCamemBERT-bio and ModernBERT-bio as state-of-the-art biomedical encoders in Base and Large sizes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：因果语言建模的绕行策略提升编码器持续预训练效果
在将编码器适配到新领域时，标准方法是继续使用掩码语言建模（MLM）。我们发现，临时切换至因果语言建模（CLM），随后进行短暂的MLM衰减，可提升下游性能。在现代BERT上对生物医学文本进行实验，该CLM绕行策略在8个法语和11个英语生物医学任务中，分别比使用相同数据和计算资源训练的MLM基线模型高出+1.2-2.8个百分点和+0.3-0.8个百分点（取决于模型规模）。我们探究了这些增益的原因，发现CLM的密集监督对Transformer低层（0-7层）影响远大于MLM。在CLM阶段冻结低层会消除下游收益，而冻结中层则保留该收益。这些表征变化在MLM衰减阶段仍持续存在，即使衰减阶段与CLM阶段长度相同，且随模型容量增大而增强。我们发布了ModernCamemBERT-bio和ModernBERT-bio，作为当前最佳的生物医学编码器（Base和Large规模）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve encoder adaptation to new domains, the paper proposes a detour strategy that temporarily switches from Masked Language Modeling (MLM) to Causal Language Modeling (CLM) before resuming MLM, which enhances downstream performance. Experiments on biomedical texts using ModernBERT show that this CLM detour yields consistent gains—+1.2 to 2.8 percentage points in French and +0.3 to 0.8 percentage points in English tasks—across multiple benchmarks, depending on model size. The authors attribute the improvement to CLM’s stronger supervision in early transformer layers (0–7), which persists even after MLM resumes and scales with model capacity; freezing low layers during CLM eliminates the benefit, while freezing mid layers preserves it.
为提升编码器在新领域的适应能力，该研究提出在继续预训练前先短暂切换至因果语言建模（CLM），再回归掩码语言建模（MLM）。在生物医学文本上使用ModernBERT进行实验，结果显示该CLM绕行策略在19个任务中均优于标准MLM基线，法语任务提升1.2–2.8个百分点，英语任务提升0.3–0.8个百分点，具体取决于模型规模。研究发现CLM对前7层Transformer的监督更强，且该效果在MLM恢复阶段仍持续存在，随模型容量增大而增强；冻结前层会消除增益，冻结中层则保留增益。
3D Gaussian Splatting for Efficient Retrospective Dynamic Scene Novel View Synthesis with a Standardized Benchmark
Authors: Yunxiao Zhang, Suryansh Kumar
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:33:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:33:14+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication at CVPR 2026; 4D World Models Workshop. Draft info: 14 pages, 4 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrospective novel view synthesis (NVS) of dynamic scenes is fundamental to applications such as sports. Recent dynamic 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) approaches introduce temporally coupled formulations to enforce motion coherence across time. In this paper, we argue that, in a synchronized multi-view (MV) setting typical of sports, the dynamic scene at each time step is already strongly geometrically constrained. We posit that the availability of calibrated, synchronized viewpoints provides sufficient spatial consistency, and therefore, explicit temporal coupling, or complex multi-body constraints seems unnecessary for retrospective NVS. To this end, we propose an approach tailored for synchronized MV dynamic scene. By initializing the SfM-derived point cloud at the start time and propagating optimized Gaussians over time, we show that efficient retrospective NVS can be achieved without imposing a temporal deformation constraint. Complementing our methodological contribution, we introduce a Dynamic MV dataset framework built on Blender for reproducible NeRF and 3DGS research. The framework generates high-quality, synchronized camera rigs and exports training-ready datasets in standard formats, eliminating inconsistencies in coordinate conventions and data pipelines. Using the framework, we construct a dynamic benchmark suite and evaluate representative NeRF and 3DGS approaches under controlled conditions. Together, we show that, under a synchronized MV setup, efficient retrospective dynamic scene NVS can be achieved using 3DGS. At the same time, the dataset-generation framework enables reproducible and principled benchmarking of dynamic NVS methods.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于高效回顾性动态场景新视角合成的3D高斯点绘与标准化基准
动态场景的回顾性新视角合成（NVS）是体育等应用的基础。近期的动态3D高斯点绘（3DGS）方法引入了时间耦合公式以确保时间上的运动一致性。本文我们认为，在体育等典型的同步多视角（MV）设置中，每个时间步的动态场景已具有强几何约束。我们主张，校准且同步的视角提供了足够的空间一致性，因此在回顾性NVS中无需显式的时间耦合或复杂的多体约束。为此，我们提出了一种针对同步MV动态场景的专用方法：通过在起始时间初始化SfM导出的点云，并随时间传播优化后的高斯点，我们证明无需施加时间形变约束即可实现高效的回顾性NVS。此外，我们引入了一个基于Blender的动态MV数据集框架，以支持可复现的NeRF和3DGS研究。该框架生成高质量的同步相机阵列，并导出标准格式的训练数据集，消除了坐标约定和数据流程的不一致。利用该框架，我们构建了动态基准套件，并在受控条件下评估代表性NeRF和3DGS方法。综上，我们表明，在同步MV设置下，3DGS可实现高效的回顾性动态场景NVS；同时，数据集生成框架支持动态NVS方法的可复现与原则性基准评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient retrospective novel view synthesis in synchronized multi-view dynamic scenes like sports, this paper argues that explicit temporal coupling is unnecessary when calibrated, synchronized viewpoints provide sufficient spatial consistency. The method initializes a 3D Gaussian Splatting model from an SfM-derived point cloud at the first time step and propagates optimized Gaussians across subsequent frames without enforcing temporal deformation constraints. Experimental results using a newly introduced Dynamic MV dataset framework built on Blender demonstrate that this approach achieves competitive performance in retrospective dynamic scene synthesis, outperforming or matching state-of-the-art NeRF and 3DGS methods under controlled, reproducible conditions.
为实现同步多视角动态场景（如体育赛事）的高效回溯式新视角合成，本文认为在已具备校准同步视角的条件下，无需显式的时间耦合或复杂的多体约束。方法从SfM生成的点云初始化3D高斯点，并在时间维度上传播优化后的高斯点，不施加时间形变约束。实验结果表明，在基于Blender构建的动态多视角数据集上，该方法能有效实现回溯式新视角合成，证明3D高斯点在同步多视角环境下无需复杂时序建模即可取得良好效果。
CAAFC: Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker for misinformation / non-factual hallucination detection and correction
Authors: Islam Eldifrawi, Shengrui Wang, Amine Trabelsi
First: 2026-05-12T17:32:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:32:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
With the vast amount of content uploaded every hour, along with the AI generated content that can include hallucinations, Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) has become increasingly vital, as it is infeasible for human fact-checkers to manually verify the sheer volume of information generated online. Professional fact-checkers have identified several gaps in existing AFC systems, noting a misalignment between how these systems operate and how fact-checking is performed in practice. In this paper, we introduce CAAFC (Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker), a frame-work designed to bridge these gaps. It surpasses SOTA AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmark datasets. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, enabling it not only to detect factual errors and hallucinations, but also to correct them by providing actionable justifications supported by primary information sources. Furthermore, CAAFC can update evidence and knowledge bases by incorporating recent and contextual information when necessary, thereby enhancing the reliability of fact verification.

Summary / 总结
Driven by the overwhelming volume of online content and the rise of AI-generated hallucinations, this paper proposes CAAFC, a chronological, actionable automated fact-checker designed to address practical gaps in existing systems. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, detecting factual errors and hallucinations while generating corrective justifications backed by primary sources. It further enhances reliability by dynamically updating evidence and knowledge bases with contextual information. Experimental results show CAAFC outperforms state-of-the-art AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmarks, demonstrating superior accuracy and actionable output.
面对海量在线内容和AI生成的虚假信息，本文提出CAAFC——一种按时间顺序、可操作的自动化事实核查框架，旨在弥补现有系统与实际核查流程之间的差距。CAAFC基于声明、对话和会话，不仅能检测事实错误和幻觉，还能提供基于原始资料的修正依据，并根据最新上下文动态更新证据库和知识库。实验表明，CAAFC在多个基准数据集上显著超越当前最先进事实核查与幻觉检测系统，展现出更高的准确性和实际应用价值。
Environment-Adaptive Preference Optimization for Wildfire Prediction
Authors: Enyi Jiang, Wu Sun
First: 2026-05-12T17:31:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:31:00+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Predicting rare extreme events such as wildfires from meteorological data requires models that remain reliable under evolving environmental conditions. This problem is inherently long-tailed: wildfire events are rare but high-impact, while most observations correspond to non-fire conditions, causing standard learning objectives to underemphasize the minority class (fire) that matters most. In addition, models trained on historical distributions often fail under distribution shifts, exhibiting degraded performance in new environments. To this end, we propose Environment-Adaptive Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework that adapts prediction to the target environment with long-tail distribution. Given a new input distribution, we first construct distribution-aligned datasets via $k$-nearest neighbor retrieval. We then perform a hybrid fine-tuning procedure on this local manifold, combining supervised learning with preference optimization, as well as emphasizing on rare extreme events. EAPO refines decision boundaries while avoiding conflicting signals from heterogeneous training data. We evaluate EAPO on a real-world wildfire prediction task with environmental shifts. EAPO achieves robust performance (ROC-AUC 0.7310) and improves detection in extreme regimes, demonstrating its effectiveness in dynamic wildfire prediction systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：环境自适应偏好优化用于野火预测
从气象数据预测罕见极端事件（如野火）需要在环境条件变化时仍保持可靠性的模型。该问题本质上是长尾分布：野火事件罕见但影响重大，而大多数观测对应非火灾条件，导致标准学习目标低估最重要的少数类（火灾）。此外，基于历史分布训练的模型在分布偏移下常表现不佳，在新环境中性能下降。为此，我们提出环境自适应偏好优化（EAPO）框架，用于在长尾分布的目标环境中自适应调整预测。给定新输入分布，我们首先通过k近邻检索构建分布对齐的数据集，然后在该局部流形上进行混合微调，结合监督学习与偏好优化，并强调罕见极端事件。EAPO在优化决策边界的同时避免异质训练数据带来的冲突信号。我们在具有环境变化的真实野火预测任务中评估EAPO，其表现稳健（ROC-AUC 0.7310），并在极端情境下提升检测能力，证明其在动态野火预测系统中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of predicting rare but critical wildfire events under changing environmental conditions, this paper introduces Environment-Adaptive Preference Optimization (EAPO), a framework designed to handle long-tailed distributions where fire events are sparse but high-impact. EAPO adapts to new environments by first constructing distribution-aligned datasets using k-nearest neighbor retrieval, then applying a hybrid fine-tuning strategy that combines supervised learning with preference optimization to emphasize rare events. Experimental results on real-world wildfire prediction show EAPO achieves a robust ROC-AUC of 0.7310 and significantly improves detection in extreme regimes, demonstrating its effectiveness in dynamic, shifting environments.
为应对在环境变化下预测罕见但高影响的野火事件的挑战，本文提出环境自适应偏好优化（EAPO）框架，用于处理野火预测中的长尾分布问题。EAPO首先通过k近邻检索构建与目标分布对齐的数据集，然后采用结合监督学习与偏好优化的混合微调策略，重点强化罕见火灾事件的识别。在真实野火数据上的实验表明，EAPO在动态环境中表现稳健，ROC-AUC达0.7310，并显著提升极端情况下的检测能力，验证了其在变化环境中的有效性。
Reconstructing Sepsis Trajectories from Clinical Case Reports using LLMs: the Textual Time Series Corpus for Sepsis
Authors: Shahriar Noroozizadeh, Jeremy C. Weiss
First: 2025-04-12T03:07:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:30:39+00:00
Comments: Conference on Health, Inference, and Learning (CHIL 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Clinical case reports and discharge summaries may be the most complete and accurate summarization of patient encounters, yet they are finalized, i.e., timestamped after the encounter. Complementary structured data streams become available sooner but suffer from incompleteness. To train models and algorithms on more complete and temporally fine-grained data, we construct a pipeline to phenotype, extract, and annotate time-localized findings within case reports using large language models. We apply our pipeline to generate an open-access textual time series corpus for Sepsis-3 comprising 2,139 case reports from the PubMed-Open Access (PMOA) Subset. To validate our system, we apply it to PMOA and timeline annotations from i2b2/MIMIC-IV and compare the results to physician-expert annotations. We show high recovery rates of clinical findings (event match rates: GPT-5--0.93, Llama 3.3 70B Instruct--0.76) and strong temporal ordering (concordance: GPT-5--0.965, Llama 3.3 70B Instruct--0.908). Our work characterizes the ability of LLMs to time-localize clinical findings in text, illustrating the limitations of LLM use for temporal reconstruction and providing several potential avenues of improvement via multimodal integration.

标题：利用大语言模型从临床病例报告重建脓毒症轨迹：脓毒症文本时间序列语料库
临床病例报告和出院摘要可能是患者就诊经历最完整、最准确的总结，但它们在就诊后才最终确定并带有时间戳。互补的结构化数据流虽可更早获取，但存在不完整问题。为训练模型和算法以获取更完整且时间粒度更细的数据，我们构建了一个流程，利用大语言模型对病例报告中的时间定位发现进行表型、提取和标注。我们将该流程应用于生成一个开放获取的脓毒症-3文本时间序列语料库，包含来自PubMed开放获取（PMOA）子集的2,139份病例报告。为验证系统，我们将其应用于PMOA及i2b2/MIMIC-IV的时间线标注，并与医生专家标注结果进行比较。结果显示，临床发现的恢复率高（事件匹配率：GPT-5为0.93，Llama 3.3 70B Instruct为0.76），时间顺序性强（一致性：GPT-5为0.965，Llama 3.3 70B Instruct为0.908）。本研究阐明了大语言模型在文本中时间定位临床发现的能力，揭示了其在时间重建中的局限性，并提出了通过多模态整合改进的若干潜在途径。
