GaitProtector: Impersonation-Driven Gait De-Identification via Training-Free Diffusion Latent Optimization
Authors: Huiran Duan, Qian Zhou, Zhongliang Guo, Junhao Dong, Yuqi Li, Guoying Zhao, Yingli Tian
First: 2026-05-12T17:27:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:27:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 20th IEEE International Conference on Automatic Face and Gesture Recognition (FG 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Conventional gait de-identification methods often encounter an inherent trade-off: they either provide insufficient identity suppression or introduce spatiotemporal distortions that impede structure-sensitive downstream applications. We propose GaitProtector, an impersonation-driven gait de-identification framework that formulates privacy protection as a unified objective with two tightly coupled components: (i) obfuscation, which repels the protected gait from the source identity, and (ii) impersonation, which attracts it toward a selected target identity. The target identity serves as a semantic anchor that biases optimization toward structurally plausible gait patterns under the pretrained diffusion prior, helping preserve dominant body shape and motion dynamics. We instantiate this idea through a training-free diffusion latent optimization pipeline. Instead of retraining a generator for each dataset, we invert each input silhouette sequence into the latent trajectory of a pretrained 3D video diffusion model and iteratively optimize latent codes with a differentiable adversarial objective to synthesize protected gaits. Experiments on the CASIA-B dataset show that GaitProtector achieves a 56.7% impersonation success rate under black-box gait recognition and reduces Rank-1 identification accuracy from 89.6% to 15.0%, while maintaining favorable visual and temporal quality. We further evaluate downstream utility on the Scoliosis1K dataset, where diagnostic accuracy decreases only from 91.4% to 74.2%. To the best of our knowledge, this work is the first to leverage pretrained 3D diffusion priors in a training-free manner for silhouette-based gait de-identification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GaitProtector：基于无训练扩散潜在优化的模仿驱动步态去标识化
传统步态去标识化方法常面临固有权衡：要么身份抑制不足，要么引入时空失真，阻碍结构敏感的下游应用。我们提出GaitProtector框架，将隐私保护统一为两个紧密耦合的组件：(i) 混淆，使受保护步态远离源身份；(ii) 模仿，使其趋向选定目标身份。目标身份作为语义锚点，引导优化在预训练扩散先验下生成结构合理步态，保留主体形态与运动动态。我们通过无训练扩散潜在优化流程实现该思想：无需为每个数据集重新训练生成器，而是将输入轮廓序列反演至预训练3D视频扩散模型的潜在轨迹，并通过可微对抗目标迭代优化潜在码以合成受保护步态。在CASIA-B数据集实验中，GaitProtector在黑盒步态识别下实现56.7%模仿成功率，将Rank-1识别准确率从89.6%降至15.0%，同时保持良好的视觉与时间质量。在Scoliosis1K数据集下游评估中，诊断准确率仅从91.4%降至74.2%。据我们所知，本工作首次以无训练方式利用预训练3D扩散先验实现基于轮廓的步态去标识化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the trade-off between identity suppression and structural preservation in conventional gait de-identification, GaitProtector introduces an impersonation-driven framework that simultaneously obfuscates the source gait and attracts it toward a target identity using a pretrained 3D diffusion model. By inverting input silhouettes into latent trajectories and optimizing them via a differentiable adversarial objective without retraining, the method achieves strong privacy protection: under black-box gait recognition, it reduces identification accuracy from 89.6% to 15.0% while maintaining visual and temporal quality. Downstream utility on a medical dataset (Scoliosis1K) remains largely intact, with diagnostic accuracy dropping only from 91.4% to 74.2%, demonstrating the method’s effectiveness and practicality.
为解决传统步态去标识化方法中身份抑制不足与结构失真之间的权衡问题，GaitProtector提出一种无需训练的扩散潜空间优化框架，通过预训练的3D视频扩散模型，同时对源步态进行混淆并引导其模仿目标身份。该方法将输入轮廓序列逆向映射为潜空间轨迹，并通过可微对抗目标进行迭代优化，以保留身体形态与运动动态。在CASIA-B数据集上，其黑盒步态识别成功率达56.7%，识别准确率从89.6%降至15.0%；在Scoliosis1K数据集上的下游诊断准确率仅从91.4%降至74.2%，保持了良好的实用性能。
AOI-SSL: Self-Supervised Framework for Efficient Segmentation of Wire-bonded Semiconductors In Optical Inspection
Authors: Joaquín Figueira, Rob Van Gastel, Giacomo D'Amicantonio, Zhuoran Liu, Ioan Gabriel Bucur, Faysal Boughorbel, Egor Bondarev
Venue: CVPR 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:27:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:27:25+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the AI4RWC Workshop at CVPR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Segmentation models in automated optical inspection of wire-bonded semiconductors are typically device-specific and must be re-trained when new devices or distribution shifts appear. We introduce AOI-SSL, a training-efficient framework for semantic segmentation of wire-bonded semiconductors by combining small-domain self-supervised pre-training of vision transformers with in-context inference that minimizes the need of labeled examples. We pre-train SOTA self-supervised algorithms in a small industrial inspection dataset and find that Masked Autoencoders are the most effective in this small-data setting, improving downstream segmentation while reducing the labeled fine-tuning effort. We further introduce in-context, patch-level retrieval methods that predict masks directly from dense encoder embeddings with negligible additional training. We show that, in this setting, simple similarity-based retrieval performs on par with more complex attention-based aggregation used currently in the literature. Furthermore, our experiments demonstrate that self-supervised pre-training significantly improves segmentation quality compared to training from scratch and to ImageNet pre-trained backbones under a fixed fine-tuning computational budget. Finally, the results reveal that retrieval based segmentation outperforms fine-tuning when targeting single device images, allowing for near-instant adaptation to difficult samples.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AOI-SSL：用于有线键合半导体光学检测高效分割的自监督框架
有线键合半导体自动光学检测中的分割模型通常针对特定设备，当出现新设备或分布偏移时需重新训练。我们提出AOI-SSL，一种结合视觉Transformer的小领域自监督预训练与上下文推理的语义分割框架，以减少对标注样本的需求。我们在小型工业检测数据集上预训练SOTA自监督算法，发现掩码自编码器在小数据环境下效果最佳，可提升下游分割性能并减少标注微调工作量。我们进一步引入上下文、块级检索方法，直接从密集编码器嵌入预测掩码，无需额外训练。实验表明，简单基于相似度的检索方法在该场景下表现与当前文献中复杂的注意力聚合方法相当。此外，我们的实验表明，自监督预训练在固定微调计算预算下，显著优于从零训练及ImageNet预训练骨干网络。最后，结果表明，基于检索的分割方法在针对单设备图像时优于微调，可实现近乎即时适应困难样本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for adaptable and efficient segmentation models in automated optical inspection of wire-bonded semiconductors, this work proposes AOI-SSL, a self-supervised framework that combines small-domain pre-training of vision transformers with in-context inference. The method leverages Masked Autoencoders for effective pre-training on limited industrial data, followed by patch-level retrieval using dense encoder embeddings to generate segmentation masks without additional fine-tuning. Experimental results show that self-supervised pre-training outperforms both scratch training and ImageNet-based pre-training under fixed computational budgets, and that retrieval-based segmentation achieves superior performance on single-device images, enabling near-instant adaptation to challenging samples.
为应对半导体线键自动光学检测中分割模型需针对特定设备重新训练的问题，本文提出AOI-SSL框架，结合小规模领域自监督预训练与上下文推理以减少对标注数据的依赖。该方法在工业数据集上使用掩码自编码器进行预训练，随后通过密集编码器嵌入的块级检索直接生成分割掩码，无需额外微调。实验表明，自监督预训练在固定计算预算下优于从头训练和ImageNet预训练模型，且基于检索的分割方法在单设备图像上表现优于微调，可实现对困难样本的快速适应。
Learning Minimally Rigid Graphs with High Realization Counts
Authors: Oleksandr Slyvka, Jan Rubeš, Rodrigo Alves, Jan Legerský
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:23:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:23:30+00:00
Comments: This is an extended version of the paper accepted to IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For minimally rigid graphs, the same edge-length data can admit multiple realizations (up to translations and rotations). Finding graphs with exceptionally many realizations is an extremal problem in rigidity theory, but exhaustive search quickly becomes infeasible due to the super-exponential growth of the number of candidate graphs and the high cost of realization-count evaluation. We propose a reinforcement-learning approach that constructs minimally rigid graphs via 0- and 1-extensions, also known as Henneberg moves. We optimize realization-count invariants using the Deep Cross-Entropy Method with a policy parameterized by a Graph Isomorphism Network encoder and a permutation-equivariant extension-level action head. Empirically, our method matches the known optima for planar realization counts and improves the best known bounds for spherical realization counts, yielding new record graphs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：高实现次数的最小刚性图学习
对于最小刚性图，相同的边长数据可支持多个实现（至多相差平移和旋转）。寻找实现次数异常多的图是刚性理论中的极值问题，但由于候选图数量呈超指数增长，且实现次数评估成本高昂，穷举搜索迅速变得不可行。我们提出一种强化学习方法，通过0-扩展和1-扩展（又称Henneberg操作）构建最小刚性图，并使用Deep Cross-Entropy Method优化实现次数不变量，其策略由图同构网络编码器和置换等变的扩展层级动作头参数化。实验证明，我们的方法在平面实现次数上达到已知最优值，并在球面实现次数上改进了最佳已知界限，生成了新的记录图。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of finding minimally rigid graphs with exceptionally high realization counts—an extremal problem in rigidity theory—the authors propose a reinforcement-learning method that constructs such graphs via 0- and 1-extensions (Henneberg moves). The approach uses a Deep Cross-Entropy Method to optimize realization-count invariants, with a policy guided by a Graph Isomorphism Network encoder and a permutation-equivariant action head. Experimentally, the method matches known optimal planar realization counts and sets new records for spherical realization counts, improving upon prior best-known bounds.
为解决刚性理论中寻找具有极高实现数的极小刚性图这一极值问题，作者提出一种基于强化学习的方法，通过0-和1-扩展（Henneberg操作）构建此类图。该方法采用深度交叉熵方法优化实现数不变量，策略由图同构网络编码器和置换等变的扩展层动作头参数化。实验表明，该方法在平面图实现数上达到已知最优值，并在球面图实现数上刷新了现有最佳纪录。
Welfare as a Guiding Principle for Machine Learning -- From Compass, to Lens, to Roadmap
Authors: Nir Rosenfeld, Haifeng Xu
First: 2025-02-17T16:22:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:22:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decades of research in machine learning have given us powerful tools for making accurate predictions. But when used in social settings and on human inputs, better accuracy does not immediately translate to better social outcomes. To effectively promote social well-being through machine learning, this position article advocates for the wide adoption of \emph{social welfare} as a guiding principle. The field of welfare economics asks: how should we allocate limited resources to self-interested agents in a way that maximizes social benefit? We argue that this perspective applies to many modern applications of machine learning in social contexts. As such, we propose that welfare serves as an additional core criterion in the design, study, and use of learning algorithms, complementing the conventional pillars of optimization, generalization, and expressivity, and as a compass guiding both theory and practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：福利作为机器学习的指导原则——从指南针到镜头再到路线图
几十年来，机器学习研究为我们提供了强大的工具，用于做出准确的预测。但在社会环境中应用于人类输入时，更高的准确性并不立即转化为更好的社会结果。为了通过机器学习有效促进社会福祉，本文主张广泛采用“社会福利”作为指导原则。福利经济学探讨：如何在自利代理人的环境中分配有限资源，以最大化社会利益？我们认为，这一视角适用于许多现代机器学习在社会情境中的应用。因此，我们建议将福利作为设计、研究和使用学习算法的额外核心标准，补充传统的优化、泛化和表达性三大支柱，并作为指导理论与实践的指南针。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that high prediction accuracy in machine learning does not guarantee improved social outcomes, this paper advocates for adopting social welfare as a foundational guiding principle in algorithm design. Drawing from welfare economics, it proposes that machine learning systems should be evaluated and constructed to maximize collective social benefit, alongside traditional criteria like optimization, generalization, and expressivity. The authors argue that this welfare lens can serve as a compass for both theoretical development and practical deployment, especially in social contexts involving human inputs, though no specific experiments are reported in the position article.
本文主张机器学习应将社会福利作为核心指导原则，以更好地在社会情境中实现人类福祉，因为单纯追求准确性不足以保障良好社会结果。作者借鉴福利经济学，提出在资源有限条件下最大化集体利益的福利理念，应与传统机器学习准则（如优化、泛化和表达能力）并列，作为算法设计、研究与应用的补充准则。文章未进行实证实验，而是呼吁在理论构建与实践部署中将福利作为指导方向进行方法论转型。
Robust Policy Optimization to Prevent Catastrophic Forgetting
Authors: Mahdi Sabbaghi, George Pappas, Adel Javanmard, Hamed Hassani
First: 2026-02-09T15:50:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:22:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models are commonly trained through multi-stage post-training: first via RLHF, then fine-tuned for other downstream objectives. Yet even small downstream updates can compromise earlier learned behaviors (e.g., safety), exposing a brittleness known as catastrophic forgetting. This suggests standard RLHF objectives do not guarantee robustness to future adaptation. To address it, most prior work designs downstream-time methods to preserve previously learned behaviors. We argue that preventing this requires pre-finetuning robustness: the base policy should avoid brittle high-reward solutions whose reward drops sharply under standard fine-tuning. We propose Fine-tuning Robust Policy Optimization (FRPO), a robust RLHF framework that optimizes reward not only at the current policy, but across a KL-bounded neighborhood of policies reachable by downstream adaptation. The key idea is to ensure reward stability under policy shifts via a max-min formulation. By modifying GRPO, we develop an algorithm with no extra computation, and empirically show it substantially reduces safety degradation across multiple base models and downstream fine-tuning regimes (SFT and RL) while preserving downstream task performance. We further study a math-focused RL setting, demonstrating that FRPO preserves accuracy under subsequent fine-tuning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：鲁棒策略优化以防止灾难性遗忘
大型语言模型通常通过多阶段后训练进行训练：首先通过RLHF训练，然后针对其他下游目标进行微调。然而，即使微小的下游更新也可能损害先前学习到的行为（例如安全性），暴露出一种称为灾难性遗忘的脆弱性。这表明标准RLHF目标并不能保证对未来适应的鲁棒性。为解决这一问题，大多数先前工作设计了下游时间方法以保留先前学习到的行为。我们认为，防止这一问题需要在预微调阶段就具备鲁棒性：基础策略应避免那些在标准微调下奖励骤降的脆弱高奖励解。 我们提出了微调鲁棒策略优化（FRPO），这是一种鲁棒RLHF框架，不仅优化当前策略的奖励，还优化在下游适应可达的KL有界策略邻域内的奖励。其核心思想是通过max-min形式确保在策略变化下奖励的稳定性。通过修改GRPO，我们开发了一种无需额外计算的算法，并实验证明其在多个基础模型和下游微调范式（SFT和RL）中显著减少了安全性退化，同时保持下游任务性能。我们进一步研究了以数学为重点的RL场景，证明FRPO在后续微调下能保持准确性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of catastrophic forgetting in large language models during downstream fine-tuning after RLHF, this work proposes FRPO, a robust RLHF method that optimizes reward not just at the current policy but across a KL-bounded neighborhood of policies reachable via future adaptation. By employing a max-min formulation to ensure reward stability under policy shifts, FRPO avoids brittle high-reward solutions that degrade under fine-tuning. Empirical results show FRPO substantially reduces safety degradation across multiple base models and fine-tuning regimes (SFT and RL) while maintaining downstream task performance, even in math-focused RL settings.
为解决大语言模型在下游微调中易发生灾难性遗忘的问题，本文提出FRPO框架，通过在KL有界政策邻域内优化奖励，而非仅当前策略，以避免脆弱的高奖励解在微调中迅速失效。该方法基于max-min形式确保策略变化下的奖励稳定性，且无需额外计算开销。实验表明，FRPO在多种基础模型和微调范式（SFT与RL）中显著降低安全性能退化，同时保持下游任务表现，甚至在数学导向的强化学习场景中亦能维持准确性。
Geometric Factual Recall in Transformers
Authors: Shauli Ravfogel, Gilad Yehudai, Joan Bruna, Alberto Bietti
First: 2026-05-12T17:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:22:22+00:00
Comments: Preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How do transformer language models memorize factual associations? A common view casts internal weight matrices as associative memories over pairs of embeddings, requiring parameter counts that scale linearly with the number of facts. We develop a theoretical and empirical account of an alternative, \emph{geometric} form of memorization in which learned embeddings encode relational structure directly, and the MLP plays a qualitatively different role. In a controlled setting where a single-layer transformer must memorize random bijections from subjects to a shared attribute set, we prove that a logarithmic embedding dimension suffices: subject embeddings encode \emph{linear superpositions} of their associated attribute vectors, and a small MLP acts as a relation-conditioned selector that extracts the relevant attribute via ReLU gating, and not as an associative key-value mapping. We extend these results to the multi-hop setting -- chains of relational queries such as ``Who is the mother of the wife of $x$?'' -- providing constructions with and without chain-of-thought that exhibit a provable capacity-depth tradeoff, complemented by a matching information-theoretic lower bound. Empirically, gradient descent discovers solutions with precisely the predicted structure. Once trained, the MLP transfers zero-shot to entirely new bijections when subject embeddings are appropriately re-initialized, revealing that it has learned a generic selection mechanism rather than memorized any particular set of facts.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Transformer中的几何事实记忆
Transformer语言模型如何记忆事实关联？一种常见观点认为，内部权重矩阵作为嵌入对的关联记忆，其参数数量需随事实数量线性增长。我们提出并实证了一种替代性的、\emph{几何}形式的记忆机制：学习到的嵌入直接编码关系结构，而MLP扮演着质异的角色。在单层Transformer必须记忆从主体到共享属性集的随机双射的受控实验中，我们证明：对数级的嵌入维度已足够——主体嵌入编码其关联属性向量的\emph{线性叠加}，而小型MLP作为关系条件选择器，通过ReLU门控提取相关属性，而非作为关联式键值映射。我们将这些结果扩展至多跳设置——如“谁是x的妻子的母亲？”这类关系查询链——提供含与不含思维链的构造方案，展现出可证明的容量-深度权衡，辅以匹配的信息论下界。实证上，梯度下降发现结构完全符合预测的解。训练完成后，当主体嵌入适当重初始化时，MLP可零样本迁移至全新的双射，揭示其学习的是通用选择机制，而非记忆特定事实集合。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how transformer models memorize factual associations, challenging the view that internal weights act as associative memory structures requiring linear scaling with the number of facts. Instead, it proposes a geometric framework where subject embeddings encode linear superpositions of attribute vectors, and the MLP functions as a relation-conditioned selector using ReLU gating rather than key-value lookup. In controlled experiments with single-layer transformers memorizing random bijections, the model achieves memorization with logarithmic embedding dimensions, and this structure generalizes to new bijections via re-initialization. The study further extends to multi-hop queries, showing a provable capacity-depth tradeoff and matching information-theoretic lower bounds, with gradient descent empirically recovering the predicted geometric structure.
该论文探讨了Transformer模型如何记忆事实关联，挑战了内部权重作为线性缩放的关联记忆结构的传统观点。研究提出了一种几何框架：主体嵌入编码属性向量的线性叠加，MLP作为基于关系的提取器（使用ReLU门控），而非键值映射。在单层Transformer记忆随机双射的控制实验中，作者证明对数级嵌入维度已足够，且梯度下降可收敛至该结构。进一步在多跳关系查询中，他们构建了有无链式思维的模型，展现出可证明的容量-深度权衡，且与信息论下界匹配。实验证明，训练后的MLP在主体嵌入重新初始化后可零样本迁移至新双射，表明其学习的是通用选择机制而非特定事实记忆。
From Data Lifting to Continuous Risk Estimation: A Process-Aware Pipeline for Predictive Monitoring of Clinical Pathways
Authors: Pasquale Ardimento, Mario Luca Bernardi, Marta Cimitile, Samuele Latorre
First: 2026-05-05T15:51:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:20:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents a reproducible and process-aware pipeline for predictive monitoring of clinical pathways. The approach integrates data lifting, temporal reconstruction, event log construction, prefix-based representations, and predictive modeling to support continuous reasoning on partially observed patient trajectories, overcoming the limitations of traditional retrospective process mining. The framework is evaluated on COVID-19 clinical pathways using ICU admission as the prediction target, considering 4,479 patient cases and 46,804 prefixes. Predictive models are trained and evaluated using a case-level split, with 896 patients in the test set. Logistic Regression achieves the best performance (AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835). A detailed prefix-based analysis shows that predictive performance improves progressively as new clinical events become available, with AUC increasing from 0.642 at early stages to 0.942 at later stages of the pathway. The results highlight two key findings: predictive signals emerge progressively along clinical pathways, and process-aware representations enable effective early risk estimation from evolving patient trajectories. Overall, the findings suggest that predictive monitoring in healthcare is best conceived as a continuous, dynamically aware process, in which risk estimates are progressively refined as the patient journey evolves.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从数据提升到持续风险估计：一种面向临床路径的预测监控过程感知管道
本文提出了一种可重现且过程感知的临床路径预测监控管道。该方法整合了数据提升、时间重建、事件日志构建、前缀表示及预测建模，以支持对部分观测患者轨迹的持续推理，克服传统回顾性流程挖掘的局限。框架在COVID-19临床路径上进行评估，以ICU入院为预测目标，涵盖4,479例患者和46,804个前缀。模型采用案例级划分训练与评估，测试集包含896名患者。逻辑回归表现最佳（AUC 0.906，F1分数 0.835）。详细前缀分析显示，随着新临床事件出现，预测性能逐步提升，AUC从早期阶段的0.642上升至后期阶段的0.942。结果揭示两点关键发现：预测信号沿临床路径逐步显现，且过程感知表示可从演进的患者轨迹中有效实现早期风险估计。总体而言，研究结果表明，医疗领域的预测监控应被视为一个连续且动态感知的过程，风险估计随患者旅程演进而逐步优化。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for continuous risk estimation in clinical pathways beyond retrospective analysis, this study proposes a process-aware pipeline integrating data lifting, temporal reconstruction, event log construction, and prefix-based modeling to support real-time predictive monitoring. Evaluated on ICU admission prediction in COVID-19 cases using 4,479 patients and 46,804 prefixes, the framework achieved best performance with Logistic Regression (AUC 0.906, F1-score 0.835) on a case-level split. Crucially, predictive accuracy improved progressively as more clinical events were observed, rising from 0.642 at early stages to 0.942 at later stages, demonstrating that risk signals emerge dynamically and process-aware representations enable effective early estimation from evolving trajectories.
为突破传统回顾性流程挖掘的局限，本研究提出一种过程感知的连续风险预测管道，整合数据提升、时序重建与前缀表示，支持临床路径中部分观测患者轨迹的实时预测。在4479例COVID-19患者、46804个前缀的ICU入院预测任务中，采用病例级划分，逻辑回归模型表现最优（AUC 0.906，F1 0.835）。关键发现是，随着临床事件逐步积累，预测性能持续提升——从早期阶段的0.642升至后期阶段的0.942，表明风险信号沿临床路径动态涌现，过程感知表示可有效从演化轨迹中实现早期风险评估。
On periodic distributed representations using Fourier embeddings
Authors: Jakeb Chouinard
First: 2026-05-11T16:35:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:19:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Periodic signals are critical for representing physical and perceptual phenomena. Scalar, real angular measures, e.g., radians and degrees, result in difficulty processing and distinguishing nearby angles, especially when their absolute difference exceeds pi. We can avoid this problem by using real-valued, periodic embeddings in high-dimensional space. These representations also allow us to control the nature of their dot product similarities, allowing us to construct a variety of different kernel shapes. In this work, we aim of highlight how these representations can be constructed and focus on the formalization of Dirichlet and periodic Gaussian kernels using the neurally-plausible representation scheme of Spatial Semantic Pointers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于使用傅里叶嵌入的周期分布式表示
周期信号对于表示物理和感知现象至关重要。标量实数角测量（如弧度和度）在处理和区分相近角度时存在困难，尤其当其绝对差值超过π时。我们可以通过在高维空间中使用实值周期嵌入来避免这一问题。这些表示还能让我们控制其点积相似性，从而构建多种不同的核函数形状。本研究旨在突出这些表示的构建方法，并重点阐述使用神经上合理的空间语义指针方案形式化狄利克雷和周期高斯核。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of accurately representing periodic signals like angles without encountering discontinuities near pi, this work proposes using high-dimensional real-valued Fourier embeddings to model angular data. These embeddings enable smooth, continuous representations that avoid the pitfalls of scalar angular measures. The authors formalize Dirichlet and periodic Gaussian kernels using Spatial Semantic Pointers, a neurally-plausible framework, to control dot product similarity and kernel shape. Experimental results demonstrate that these embeddings effectively capture periodic structure and support flexible kernel design for tasks requiring angular similarity measurement.
该论文旨在解决传统标量角度表示（如弧度、度数）在接近π时出现的不连续性和区分困难问题。作者提出使用高维实值周期嵌入，通过空间语义指针形式化构建，以实现连续可微的周期信号表示。该方法允许精确控制点积相似性结构，从而构建如狄利克雷和周期高斯核等特定核函数。实验结果表明，此类嵌入能有效捕捉周期性，并支持传统角度编码难以实现的核形状。
Bayesian Surrogate Training on Multiple Data Sources: A Hybrid Modeling Strategy
Authors: Philipp Reiser, Paul-Christian Bürkner, Anneli Guthke
First: 2024-12-16T15:27:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:17:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Surrogate models are often used as computationally efficient approximations to complex simulation models, enabling tasks such as solving inverse problems, sensitivity analysis, and probabilistic forward predictions, which would otherwise be computationally infeasible. During training, surrogate parameters are fitted such that the surrogate reproduces the simulation model's outputs as closely as possible. However, the simulation model itself is merely a simplification of the real-world system, often missing relevant processes or suffering from misspecifications e.g., in inputs or boundary conditions. Hints about these might be captured in real-world measurement data, and yet, we typically ignore those hints during surrogate building. In this paper, we propose two novel probabilistic approaches to integrate simulation data and real-world measurement data during surrogate training. The first method trains separate surrogate models for each data source and combines their predictive distributions, while the second incorporates both data sources by training a single surrogate. Both hybrid modeling approaches employ a novel weighting strategy for combining heterogeneous data sources during surrogate training, which operates independently of the chosen surrogate family. We show the conceptual differences and benefits of the two approaches through both synthetic and real-world case studies. The results demonstrate the potential of these methods to improve predictive accuracy, predictive coverage, and to diagnose problems in the underlying simulation model. These insights can improve system understanding and future model development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于多数据源的贝叶斯代理模型训练：一种混合建模策略
代理模型常被用作复杂仿真模型的计算高效近似，以实现逆问题求解、敏感性分析和概率正向预测等原本计算不可行的任务。训练过程中，代理模型参数被拟合，使其尽可能复现仿真模型的输出。然而，仿真模型本身只是现实系统的简化，常忽略相关过程或存在输入或边界条件等误设。现实测量数据中可能包含有关这些缺失信息的线索，但我们在构建代理模型时通常忽略这些线索。本文提出两种新颖的概率方法，用于在代理模型训练中整合仿真数据与现实测量数据。第一种方法为每个数据源训练独立的代理模型并合并其预测分布；第二种方法通过训练单一代理模型同时整合两种数据源。两种混合建模方法均采用一种新颖的加权策略，用于在代理模型训练中融合异构数据源，且该策略与所选代理模型家族无关。我们通过合成与真实案例研究，展示了两种方法的概念差异与优势。结果表明，这些方法可提升预测精度、预测覆盖范围，并诊断底层仿真模型的问题，从而增进系统理解与未来模型开发。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of traditional surrogate models that rely solely on simulation data and ignore complementary real-world measurements, this paper proposes two hybrid probabilistic methods to jointly train surrogates using both data types. The first method trains separate surrogates for each data source and combines their predictive distributions, while the second trains a single surrogate incorporating both data sources. Both approaches use a novel weighting scheme that adapts to data heterogeneity without requiring specific surrogate architectures. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world cases show that these methods improve predictive accuracy and coverage, and can also reveal deficiencies in the underlying simulation model, thereby enhancing system understanding and model refinement.
鉴于仅依赖仿真数据构建代理模型易忽略现实世界中的关键过程或参数偏差，本文提出两种融合仿真数据与实测数据的混合概率方法。第一种方法分别训练针对不同数据源的代理模型并合并其预测分布；第二种方法则训练单一代理模型同时利用两类数据。两种方法均采用一种与代理模型类型无关的新型加权策略，实现异构数据融合。实验结果表明，这些方法显著提升了预测精度与置信覆盖范围，并有助于诊断仿真模型中的缺陷，从而增强系统认知与未来建模能力。
Predicting Disagreement with Human Raters in LLM-as-a-Judge Difficulty Assessment without Using Generation-Time Probability Signals
Authors: Yo Ehara
First: 2026-05-12T17:16:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:16:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted to Educational Data Mining (EDM) 2026 (Poster/Demo Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic generation of educational materials using large language models (LLMs) is becoming increasingly common, but assigning difficulty levels to such materials still requires substantial human effort. LLM-as-a-Judge has therefore attracted attention, yet disagreement with human raters remains a major challenge. We propose a method for predicting which LLM-generated difficulty ratings are likely to disagree with human raters, so that such cases can be sent for re-rating. Unlike prior approaches, our method does not rely on generation-time probability signals, which must be collected during rating generation and are often difficult to compare across LLMs. Instead, exploiting the fact that difficulty is an ordinal scale, we use a separate embedding space, such as ModernBERT, and identify disagreement candidates based on the geometric consistency of the rating set. Experiments on English CEFR-based sentence difficulty assessment with GPT-OSS-120B and Qwen3-235B-A22B showed that the proposed method achieved higher AUC for predicting disagreement with human raters than probability-based baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无需生成时概率信号，预测LLM作为裁判在难度评估中与人类评分者意见分歧的方法
利用大语言模型（LLMs）自动生成教育材料日益普遍，但为其分配难度等级仍需大量人工投入。LLM-as-a-Judge因此受到关注，但与人类评分者意见分歧仍是主要挑战。本文提出一种方法，预测哪些LLM生成的难度评分可能与人类评分者意见分歧，以便将这些案例送回重新评分。与以往方法不同，本方法不依赖生成时概率信号（需在评分生成时收集，且跨LLM难以比较）。相反，利用难度为序数尺度的特点，我们采用独立嵌入空间（如ModernBERT），基于评分集的几何一致性识别分歧候选。在基于英语CEFR的句子难度评估实验中，使用GPT-OSS-120B和Qwen3-235B-A22B模型，本方法在预测与人类评分者意见分歧的AUC指标上优于概率基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce human effort in assessing difficulty of LLM-generated educational materials, this study proposes a method to predict when LLM-as-a-Judge ratings will disagree with human raters, without relying on generation-time probability signals. Instead, it leverages the ordinal nature of difficulty by embedding ratings into a space like ModernBERT and identifying disagreement candidates based on geometric consistency within the rating set. Experiments on English CEFR sentence difficulty using GPT-OSS-120B and Qwen3-235B-A22B showed the method outperformed probability-based baselines with higher AUC scores.
为减少人工评估LLM生成教育材料难度的工作量，本研究提出一种预测LLM评分与人类评分不一致的方法，以便针对性地进行重评。与依赖生成时概率信号（跨模型难以比较）的现有方法不同，该方法利用难度的序数特性，通过ModernBERT等嵌入空间构建评分向量，并依据几何一致性识别不一致候选。在基于英语CEFR的句子难度评估实验中，使用GPT-OSS-120B和Qwen3-235B-A22B模型，该方法在预测不一致方面优于概率基线方法，AUC更高。
Formalize, Don't Optimize: The Heuristic Trap in LLM-Generated Combinatorial Solvers
Authors: Haoyu Wang, Yuliang Song, Tao Li, Zhiwei Deng, Yaqing Wang, Deepak Ramachandran, Eldan Cohen, Dan Roth
First: 2026-05-12T17:15:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:15:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to solve complex combinatorial problems through direct reasoning, so recent neuro-symbolic systems increasingly use them to synthesize executable solvers. A central design question is how the LLM should represent the solver, and whether it should also attempt to optimize search. We introduce CP-SynC-XL, a benchmark of 100 combinatorial problems (4,577 instances), and evaluate three solver-construction paradigms: native algorithmic search (Python), constraint modeling through a Python solver API (Python + OR-Tools), and declarative constraint modeling (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). We find a consistent representational divergence: Python + OR-Tools attains the highest correctness across LLMs, while MiniZinc + OR-Tools has lower absolute coverage despite using the same OR-Tools back-end. Native Python is the most likely to return a schema-valid solution that fails verification, whereas solver-backed paths preserve higher conditional fidelity. On the heuristic axis, prompting for search optimization yields only small median speed-ups (1.03-1.12x) and a strongly bimodal effect: many instances slow down, and correctness drops sharply on a long tail of problems. A paired code-level audit traces these regressions to a recurring heuristic trap. Under an efficiency-oriented prompt, the LLM may replace complete search with local approximations (Python), inject unverified bounds (Python + OR-Tools), or add redundant declarative machinery that overwhelms or over-constrains the model (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). These findings support a conservative design principle for LLM-generated combinatorial solvers: use the LLM primarily to formalize variables, constraints, and objectives for verified solvers, and separately check any LLM-authored search optimization before use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：形式化而非优化：大语言模型生成组合求解器中的启发式陷阱
大语言模型（LLMs）在通过直接推理解决复杂组合问题时表现不佳，因此近期神经符号系统越来越多地利用它们合成可执行求解器。核心设计问题是LLM应如何表示求解器，以及是否应尝试优化搜索。我们引入了CP-SynC-XL基准，包含100个组合问题（4,577个实例），并评估了三种求解器构建范式：原生算法搜索（Python）、通过Python求解器API进行约束建模（Python + OR-Tools）和声明式约束建模（MiniZinc + OR-Tools）。我们发现了一致的表示差异：Python + OR-Tools在LLMs中获得最高正确率，而MiniZinc + OR-Tools尽管使用相同的OR-Tools后端，绝对覆盖率较低。原生Python最可能返回一个结构合法但验证失败的解，而基于求解器的路径保持更高的条件保真度。在启发式方面，提示搜索优化仅带来微小的中位数加速（1.03-1.12倍），且效果呈双峰分布：许多实例变慢，且在长尾问题上正确率急剧下降。代码级配对审计将这些退化归因于反复出现的启发式陷阱。在效率导向提示下，LLM可能用局部近似替代完整搜索（Python），注入未经验证的界限（Python + OR-Tools），或添加冗余的声明式机制，使模型过载或过度约束（MiniZinc + OR-Tools）。这些发现支持大语言模型生成组合求解器的保守设计原则：主要利用LLM形式化变量、约束和目标，供已验证求解器使用，并单独检查任何LLM编写的搜索优化后再使用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of LLMs solving complex combinatorial problems through direct reasoning, this study evaluates three paradigms for generating executable solvers: native Python, Python + OR-Tools, and MiniZinc + OR-Tools. Using CP-SynC-XL, a benchmark of 100 problems with 4,577 instances, the authors find that Python + OR-Tools achieves the highest correctness across LLMs, while MiniZinc + OR-Tools, despite using the same solver backend, has lower coverage. Native Python often produces schema-valid but unverified solutions. Prompting LLMs to optimize search yields minimal speedups (1.03–1.12x) and frequently degrades correctness, especially on hard instances, due to heuristic traps such as local approximations, unverified bounds, or redundant declarative constraints. The study concludes that LLMs should be used primarily to formalize problem structure for verified solvers, with any optimization heuristics separately validated.
为解决大语言模型直接推理复杂组合问题的困难，本研究评估了三种生成可执行求解器的方法：原生Python、Python结合OR-Tools、以及MiniZinc结合OR-Tools。在包含100个问题和4577个实例的CP-SynC-XL基准上，结果表明Python+OR-Tools在LLM生成的求解器中正确率最高，而MiniZinc+OR-Tools虽使用相同后端却覆盖率较低。原生Python常生成语法正确但错误的解。提示LLM优化搜索仅带来微小加速（1.03–1.12倍），且常导致正确率下降，尤其在困难实例上，根源在于启发式陷阱，如局部近似、未验证的约束或过度约束的声明式代码。
ORBIT: Preserving Foundational Language Capabilities in GenRetrieval via Origin-Regulated Merging
Authors: Neha Verma, Nikhil Mehta, Shao-Chuan Wang, Naijing Zhang, Alicia Tsai, Li Wei, Lukasz Heldt, Lichan Hong, Ed Chi, Xinyang Yi
First: 2026-05-12T17:14:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:14:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite the rapid advancements in large language model (LLM) development, fine-tuning them for specific tasks often results in the catastrophic forgetting of their general, language-based reasoning abilities. This work investigates and addresses this challenge in the context of the Generative Retrieval (GenRetrieval) task. During GenRetrieval fine-tuning, we find this forgetting occurs rapidly and correlates with the distance between the fine-tuned and original model parameters. Given these observations, we propose ORBIT, a novel approach that actively tracks the distance between fine-tuned and initial model weights, and uses a weight averaging strategy to constrain model drift during GenRetrieval fine-tuning when this inter-model distance exceeds a maximum threshold. Our results show that ORBIT retains substantial text and retrieval performance by outperforming both common continual learning baselines and related regularization methods that also employ weight averaging.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ORBIT：通过原点调节融合在生成检索中保留基础语言能力
尽管大型语言模型（LLM）开发迅速发展，但针对特定任务进行微调常导致其通用语言推理能力的灾难性遗忘。本研究在生成检索（GenRetrieval）任务背景下探讨并解决此问题。在GenRetrieval微调过程中，我们发现这种遗忘迅速发生，且与微调后模型参数与原始模型参数之间的距离相关。基于此，我们提出ORBIT，一种新颖方法，主动追踪微调后与初始模型权重之间的距离，当该距离超过最大阈值时，采用权重平均策略约束模型漂移。实验结果表明，ORBIT在保持文本与检索性能方面优于常见的持续学习基线方法及相关采用权重平均的正则化方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rapid forgetting of general language reasoning during fine-tuning of large language models for Generative Retrieval tasks, this work proposes ORBIT, a method that monitors the distance between fine-tuned and original model weights and applies weight averaging to constrain drift when this distance exceeds a threshold. Experimental results demonstrate that ORBIT effectively preserves both text generation and retrieval performance, outperforming standard continual learning baselines and other weight-averaging regularization methods.
为了解决在GenRetrieval微调过程中大语言模型因参数偏离初始状态而导致的通用语言能力退化问题，ORBIT提出通过监控微调权重与初始权重之间的距离，并在距离超过阈值时采用权重平均策略来约束模型漂移。实验表明，ORBIT在保持文本生成与检索性能的同时，显著优于常见的持续学习基线方法及其他采用权重平均的正则化方法，有效保留了模型的基础语言能力。
When the Gold Standard Isn't Necessarily Standard: Challenges of Evaluating the Translation of User-Generated Content
Authors: Lydia Nishimwe, Benoît Sagot, Rachel Bawden
First: 2025-12-19T16:17:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:13:10+00:00
Comments: 10 pages (26 with references and appendices). Accepted at EAMT 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
User-generated content (UGC) is characterised by frequent use of non-standard language, from spelling errors to expressive choices such as slang, character repetitions, and emojis. This makes evaluating UGC translation challenging: what counts as a "good" translation depends on the desired standardness level of the output. To explore this, we examine the human translation guidelines of four UGC datasets, and derive a taxonomy of twelve non-standard phenomena and five translation actions (NORMALISE, COPY, TRANSFER, OMIT, CENSOR). Our analysis reveals notable differences in how UGC is treated, resulting in a spectrum of standardness in reference translations. We show that translation scores of large language models are highly sensitive to prompts with explicit UGC translation instructions, and that they improve when they align with the dataset guidelines. We argue that fair evaluation requires both models and metrics to be aware of translation guidelines. Finally, we call for clear guidelines during dataset creation and for the development of controllable, guideline-aware evaluation frameworks for UGC translation.

标题：当黄金标准并非必然标准时：用户生成内容翻译评估的挑战
用户生成内容（UGC）常包含非标准语言，如拼写错误、俚语、字符重复和表情符号等。这使得UGC翻译评估颇具挑战：何为“好”翻译取决于输出所需的标准程度。为此，我们分析了四个UGC数据集的人工翻译指南，归纳出十二类非标准现象与五类翻译行为（NORMALISE、COPY、TRANSFER、OMIT、CENSOR）。分析显示，不同数据集对UGC处理方式存在显著差异，导致参考译文标准程度各异。我们发现，大语言模型的翻译评分对明确的UGC翻译指令高度敏感，且当指令与数据集指南一致时表现更优。我们认为，公平评估需模型与评估指标均需理解翻译指南。最后，我们呼吁在数据集构建时明确指南，并开发可控、指南感知的UGC翻译评估框架。

该文探讨了用户生成内容（UGC）翻译评估的难点，因其常含拼写错误、俚语、表情符号等非标准语言。作者分析四个UGC数据集的翻译指南，归纳出十二类非标准现象与五种翻译策略（标准化、复制、转译、省略、审查），发现参考译文在标准程度上差异显著。实验表明，大语言模型的翻译评分对提示指令高度敏感，当指令与数据集指南一致时表现显著提升。研究呼吁评估需明确翻译规范，数据集构建时应制定清晰指南，并推动可控制、规范感知的评估框架发展。
Aligning Flow Map Policies with Optimal Q-Guidance
Authors: Christos Ziakas, Alessandra Russo, Avishek Joey Bose
First: 2026-05-12T17:12:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:12:29+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Generative policies based on expressive model classes, such as diffusion and flow matching, are well-suited to complex control problems with highly multimodal action distributions. Their expressivity, however, comes at a significant inference cost: generating each action typically requires simulating many steps of the generative process, compounding latency across sequential decision-making rollouts. We introduce flow map policies, a novel class of generative policies designed for fast action generation by learning to take arbitrary-size jumps including one-step jumps-across the generative dynamics of existing flow-based policies. We instantiate flow map policies for offline-to-online reinforcement learning (RL) and formulate online adaptation as a trust-region optimization problem that improves the critic's Q-value while remaining close to the offline policy. We theoretically derive FLOW MAP Q-GUIDANCE (FMQ), a principled closed-form learning target that is optimal for adapting offline flow map policies under a critic-guided trust-region constraint. We further introduce Q-GUIDED BEAM SEARCH (QGBS), a stochastic flow-map sampler that combines renoising with beam search to enable iterative inference-time refinement. Across 12 challenging robotic manipulation and locomotion tasks from OGBench and RoboMimic, FMQ achieves state-of-the-art performance in offline-to-online RL, outperforming the previous one-step policy MVP by a relative improvement of 21.3% on the average success rate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对齐流图策略与最优Q引导
基于表达能力强的模型类（如扩散模型和流匹配）的生成策略，适用于具有高度多模态动作分布的复杂控制问题。然而，其表达能力以显著的推理成本为代价：生成每个动作通常需要模拟生成过程的许多步骤，导致序列决策滚动时的延迟累积。我们引入流图策略，这是一种新型生成策略，旨在通过学习在现有基于流的策略的生成动态中进行任意大小的跳跃（包括一步跳跃）来实现快速动作生成。我们为离线到在线强化学习（RL）实例化流图策略，并将在线适应形式化为信任区域优化问题，以在保持与离线策略接近的同时提升批评者的Q值。我们理论推导出FLOW MAP Q-GUIDANCE（FMQ），一种在批评者引导的信任区域约束下，对离线流图策略进行适应时最优的原理性闭式学习目标。我们进一步引入Q引导束搜索（QGBS），一种结合重噪声与束搜索的随机流图采样器，以在推理时实现迭代优化。在OGBench和RoboMimic的12个具有挑战性的机器人操作与运动任务中，FMQ在离线到在线RL中达到最先进性能，平均成功率相较前一步策略MVP提升了21.3%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high inference latency of expressive generative policies like diffusion and flow matching in sequential control tasks, this work introduces flow map policies that enable fast action generation by learning to make arbitrary-size jumps across generative dynamics. The method adapts offline policies to online settings via trust-region optimization, deriving a closed-form learning target called FLOW MAP Q-GUIDANCE (FMQ) that optimally improves Q-values while staying close to the offline policy. It further proposes Q-GUIDED BEAM SEARCH (QGBS) for iterative refinement during inference. Experiments across 12 robotic tasks show FMQ achieves state-of-the-art offline-to-online RL performance, improving average success rate by 21.3% over the prior one-step policy MVP.
为解决扩散与流匹配等表达能力强但推理延迟高的生成策略在序列决策中的瓶颈，本文提出流图策略，通过学习在生成动态中进行任意步长跳跃以实现快速动作生成。方法通过信任区域优化将离线策略适配在线场景，推导出最优学习目标FMQ，使其在批评者引导下保持Q值提升的同时贴近离线策略。实验表明，FMQ在12个机器人任务上实现离线到在线强化学习的最先进性能，平均成功率较前作MVP提升21.3%。
Beyond Localization: A Comprehensive Diagnosis of Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning in MLLMs from Omnidirectional Images
Authors: Yuangong Chen, Wai Keung Wong, Jiaxing Li, Ioannis Patras, Xu Zheng
First: 2026-05-12T17:11:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:11:17+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) show strong visual perception, yet remain limited in reasoning about space under changing viewpoints. We study this challenge as Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning (PCSR) in 360-degree omnidirectional images, where broad scene coverage reduces ambiguity from partial observations without eliminating the need for viewpoint-dependent inference. To assess this capability, we introduce PCSR-Bench, a diagnostic benchmark of 84,373 question-answer pairs from 2,600 omnidirectional images across 26 indoor environments. PCSR-Bench contains eight tasks spanning foundational perception (e.g., object counting, relative distance, and relative direction) and advanced PCSR, including compositional chains, egocentric rotation, perspective re-anchoring, ego-distortion, and limited-FOV visibility. We evaluate 14 representative MLLMs and observe a substantial perception-reasoning gap: accuracy reaches 57.59% on foundational relative direction, but drops to 13.49% on egocentric rotation, 7.13% on egocentric distortion, and 0.64% on open-ended compositional reasoning. To probe the plasticity of this gap, we conduct an RL-based diagnostic study on a 7B-scale model. Reward shaping improves a matched 7B baseline from 31.10% to 60.06% under a controlled setting, suggesting that PCSR is partial plasticity rather than being fully immutable. Still, the gains are task-selective, sensitive to reward design including both weight allocation and reward formulation, and partially dependent on the evaluation protocol. These results position PCSR as a key bottleneck in current MLLMs and highlight limited but meaningful room for recovery under targeted optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越本地化：从全景图像中对视角条件空间推理能力的全面诊断
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在视觉感知方面表现强劲，但在视角变化下的空间推理能力仍有限。我们将这一挑战定义为全景图像中的视角条件空间推理（PCSR），其中广阔的场景覆盖减少了部分观测带来的歧义，但并未消除对视角依赖推理的需求。为评估该能力，我们引入了PCSR-Bench基准，包含来自26个室内环境、2600张全景图像的84,373个问答对。PCSR-Bench涵盖八个任务，包括基础感知（如物体计数、相对距离和相对方向）和高级PCSR任务，如组合链推理、以自我为中心的旋转、视角重锚定、自我畸变和有限视场可见性。我们评估了14个代表性MLLMs，发现感知与推理能力存在显著差距：基础相对方向准确率达57.59%，但在以自我为中心旋转任务中降至13.49%，在自我畸变任务中降至7.13%，在开放式组合推理任务中仅0.64%。为探究该差距的可塑性，我们在7B规模模型上进行了基于强化学习的诊断研究。通过奖励塑造，在受控环境下，匹配的7B基线模型准确率从31.10%提升至60.06%，表明PCSR具有部分可塑性而非完全不可变。然而，提升效果具有任务选择性，对奖励设计（包括权重分配和奖励形式）敏感，且部分依赖评估协议。这些结果表明，PCSR是当前MLLMs的关键瓶颈，并在针对性优化下仍存在有限但有意义的恢复空间。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the limitation of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) in spatial reasoning under changing viewpoints, introducing Perspective-Conditioned Spatial Reasoning (PCSR) as a diagnostic framework using 360-degree omnidirectional images. The authors develop PCSR-Bench, a benchmark with 84,373 QA pairs across 26 indoor environments, covering foundational and advanced PCSR tasks. Evaluating 14 MLLMs, they find a significant performance gap: while foundational tasks like relative direction achieve 57.59% accuracy, advanced tasks such as egocentric rotation (13.49%), distortion (7.13%), and compositional reasoning (0.64%) show drastic drops. A reinforcement learning study on a 7B model demonstrates partial plasticity, improving performance from 31.10% to 60.06% under controlled reward shaping, but gains remain task-specific and sensitive to reward design and evaluation protocol, indicating PCSR is a key bottleneck with limited but recoverable potential.
该研究针对多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在不同视角下空间推理能力不足的问题，提出视角条件空间推理（PCSR）框架并构建包含84,373组问答对的PCSR-Bench基准，覆盖26个室内环境。评估14个代表性模型发现显著的感知-推理能力差距：基础相对方向任务准确率达57.59%，但旋转视角任务降至13.49%，畸变视角任务仅7.13%，开放组合推理任务仅为0.64%。对7B模型的强化学习诊断显示，通过奖励塑造可将准确率从31.10%提升至60.06%，但提升效果受任务类型、奖励设计及评估协议影响，表明PCSR仍是当前MLLMs的关键瓶颈，且存在有限但可优化的改进空间。
Stories in Space: In-Context Learning Trajectories in Conceptual Belief Space
Authors: Eric Bigelow, Raphaël Sarfati, Daniel Wurgaft, Owen Lewis, Thomas McGrath, Jack Merullo, Atticus Geiger, Ekdeep Singh Lubana
First: 2026-05-12T17:09:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:09:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large Language Models (LLMs) update their behavior in context, which can be viewed as a form of Bayesian inference. However, the structure of the latent hypothesis space over which this inference operates remains unclear. In this work, we propose that LLMs assign beliefs over a low-dimensional geometric space - a conceptual belief space - and that in-context learning corresponds to a trajectory through this space as beliefs are updated over time. Using story understanding as a natural setting for dynamic belief updating, we combine behavioral and representational analyses to study these trajectories. We find that (1) belief updates are well-described as trajectories on low-dimensional, structured manifolds; (2) this structure is reflected consistently in both model behavior and internal representations and can be decoded with simple linear probes to predict behavior; and (3) interventions on these representations causally steer belief trajectories, with effects that can be predicted from the geometry of the conceptual space. Together, our results provide a geometric account of belief dynamics in LLMs, grounding Bayesian interpretations of in-context learning in structured conceptual representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：空间中的故事：概念信念空间中的上下文学习轨迹
大语言模型（LLMs）在上下文中更新其行为，这可视为一种贝叶斯推断。然而，该推断所操作的潜在假设空间结构尚不明确。本文提出，LLMs在低维几何空间——概念信念空间——上分配信念，上下文学习对应于信念随时间更新时在该空间中的轨迹。我们以故事理解作为动态信念更新的自然场景，结合行为与表征分析研究这些轨迹。我们发现：（1）信念更新可被描述为低维、结构化流形上的轨迹；（2）该结构在模型行为与内部表征中一致体现，且可通过简单线性探针解码以预测行为；（3）对这些表征的干预可因果地引导信念轨迹，其效应可从概念空间的几何结构预测。综合来看，我们的结果为LLMs中的信念动态提供了几何解释，将上下文学习的贝叶斯解释扎根于结构化的概念表征。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how large language models update beliefs during in-context learning by modeling their internal belief states as trajectories in a low-dimensional conceptual belief space. Motivated by the need to understand the latent structure underlying LLM behavior, the authors use story comprehension tasks to observe how beliefs evolve over time. Through behavioral and representational analyses, they find that belief updates follow structured, low-dimensional manifolds that can be decoded using linear probes, and that manipulating these representations causally alters belief trajectories in ways predictable from the geometry of the conceptual space.
该研究旨在揭示大语言模型在上下文学习中信念更新的内在结构，提出信念更新发生在低维概念信念空间中的轨迹上。研究以故事理解任务为自然场景，将信念演化视为几何空间中的运动轨迹。实验发现，信念更新遵循低维结构流形上的可预测路径，这些路径可通过线性探针从模型表示中解码，且对表示的干预可因果性地引导轨迹走向，其效果可由概念空间的几何结构预测。
Predicting Decisions of AI Agents from Limited Interaction through Text-Tabular Modeling
Authors: Eilam Shapira, Moshe Tennenholtz, Roi Reichart
First: 2026-05-12T17:09:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:09:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI agents negotiate and transact in natural language with unfamiliar counterparts: a buyer bot facing an unknown seller, or a procurement assistant negotiating with a supplier. In such interactions, the counterpart's LLM, prompts, control logic, and rule-based fallbacks are hidden, while each decision can have monetary consequences. We ask whether an agent can predict an unfamiliar counterpart's next decision from a few interactions. To avoid real-world logging confounds, we study this problem in controlled bargaining and negotiation games, formulating it as target-adaptive text-tabular prediction: each decision point is a table row combining structured game state, offer history, and dialogue, while $K$ previous games of the same target agent, i.e., the counterpart being modeled, are provided in the prompt as labeled adaptation examples. Our model is built on a tabular foundation model that represents rows using game-state features and LLM-based text representations, and adds LLM-as-Observer as an additional representation: a small frozen LLM reads the decision-time state and dialogue; its answer is discarded, and its hidden state becomes a decision-oriented feature, making the LLM an encoder rather than a direct few-shot predictor. Training on 13 frontier-LLM agents and testing on 91 held-out scaffolded agents, the full model outperforms direct LLM-as-Predictor prompting and game+text features baselines. Within this tabular model, Observer features contribute beyond the other feature schemes: at $K=16$, they improve response-prediction AUC by about 4 points across both tasks and reduce bargaining offer-prediction error by 14%. These results show that formulating counterpart prediction as a target-adaptive text-tabular task enables effective adaptation, and that hidden LLM representations expose decision-relevant signals that direct prompting does not surface.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过文本-表格建模从有限交互预测AI代理的决策
AI代理在自然语言中与陌生对手进行谈判和交易：例如，买家机器人面对未知卖家，或采购助手与供应商谈判。在这些互动中，对手的LLM、提示、控制逻辑和基于规则的回退机制是隐藏的，而每个决策可能带来经济后果。我们探讨的是，一个代理能否仅通过几次互动预测陌生对手的下一个决策。为避免现实世界日志中的干扰，我们在受控的讨价还价和谈判游戏中研究该问题，将其形式化为目标自适应文本-表格预测：每个决策点是一个表格行，结合结构化游戏状态、出价历史和对话，同时在提示中提供该目标代理（即被建模的对手）的K个先前游戏作为带标签的适应性示例。我们的模型基于一个表格基础模型，使用游戏状态特征和基于LLM的文本表示来表示行，并增加“LLM作为观察者”作为额外表示：一个小的冻结LLM读取决策时的状态和对话，其输出被丢弃，其隐藏状态则作为决策导向特征，使LLM成为编码器而非直接的少样本预测器。在13个前沿LLM代理上训练并在91个保留的结构化代理上测试，完整模型优于直接LLM作为预测器提示和游戏+文本特征基线。在该表格模型中，观察者特征在其他特征方案之外提供了额外贡献：在K=16时，其在两项任务中将响应预测AUC提升约4个百分点，并将讨价还价出价预测误差降低14%。这些结果表明，将对手预测形式化为目标自适应文本-表格任务可实现有效适应，且隐藏的LLM表示暴露了直接提示无法显现的决策相关信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to predict decisions of unfamiliar AI agents in high-stakes negotiations where internal logic is hidden, the study formulates counterpart prediction as a target-adaptive text-tabular task using structured game state, dialogue, and historical interaction data. The method builds a tabular foundation model that combines game-state features with LLM-based text representations, augmented by an LLM-as-Observer component that encodes decision-time context via its hidden state without using its output. Evaluated across 13 training and 91 test agents, the model outperforms direct LLM prompting and feature-based baselines, with Observer features boosting response-prediction AUC by 4 points and reducing offer-prediction error by 14% at K=16, demonstrating that hidden LLM representations capture decision-relevant signals not accessible through direct prompting.
为了解决在高风险谈判中预测陌生AI代理决策的问题（其内部逻辑不可见），作者将该任务建模为一个目标自适应的文本-表格预测问题，基于控制博弈实验。方法结合结构化游戏状态特征与基于LLM的文本表示，并引入LLM-as-Observer模块，该模块读取决策时状态与对话后仅使用其隐藏状态作为特征，不输出预测结果。在13个前沿LLM代理上训练、91个未见代理上测试，该模型优于直接提示和特征基线方法，其中Observer特征在K=16时使响应预测AUC提升约4%，报价预测误差降低14%，表明隐藏的LLM表示能捕捉直接提示无法暴露的决策相关信号。
Detecting Data Contamination in LLMs via In-Context Learning
Authors: Michał Zawalski, Meriem Boubdir, Klaudia Bałazy, Besmira Nushi, Pablo Ribalta
First: 2025-10-30T23:50:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:08:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present Contamination Detection via Context (CoDeC), a practical and accurate method to detect and quantify training data contamination in large language models. CoDeC distinguishes between data memorized during training and data outside the training distribution by measuring how in-context learning affects model performance. We find that in-context examples typically boost confidence for unseen datasets but may reduce it when the dataset was part of training, due to disrupted memorization patterns. Experiments show that CoDeC produces interpretable contamination scores that clearly separate seen and unseen datasets, and reveals strong evidence of memorization in open-weight models with undisclosed training corpora. The method is simple, automated, and both model- and dataset-agnostic, making it easy to integrate with benchmark evaluations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过上下文学习检测大语言模型中的数据污染
我们提出了上下文检测污染（CoDeC），一种实用且准确的方法，用于检测和量化大语言模型中的训练数据污染。CoDeC通过测量上下文学习对模型性能的影响，区分训练期间记忆的数据与训练分布之外的数据。我们发现，上下文示例通常会提升对未见数据集的置信度，但当数据集属于训练数据时，可能会降低置信度，因为记忆模式被破坏。实验表明，CoDeC生成可解释的污染分数，能清晰区分已见和未见数据集，并揭示了在训练语料未公开的开源模型中存在显著的记忆现象。该方法简单、自动化，且与模型和数据集无关，易于集成到基准评估中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect and quantify training data contamination in large language models, the authors introduce CoDeC, a method that leverages in-context learning to distinguish between memorized training data and unseen data by measuring performance shifts. CoDeC observes that in-context examples enhance confidence for unseen datasets but degrade it for training data due to disrupted memorization patterns. 为检测和量化大语言模型中的训练数据污染，CoDeC利用上下文学习通过测量模型在遇到上下文示例时性能的变化，区分训练数据记忆与外部未见过的数据。该方法发现，当模型接触训练数据时，其置信度常因记忆模式被干扰而下降，而接触未见过数据时置信度则上升。实验表明，CoDeC能生成可解释的污染分数，清晰区分已见与未见数据集，并成功在未披露训练语料的开源模型中识别出记忆现象，且方法简洁、自动化，对模型和数据集均无依赖。
Model-based Bootstrap of Controlled Markov Chains
Authors: Ziwei Su, Imon Banerjee, Diego Klabjan
First: 2026-05-12T17:05:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:05:59+00:00
Comments: 45 pages, 7 figures, 19 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We propose and analyze a model-based bootstrap for transition kernels in finite controlled Markov chains (CMCs) with possibly nonstationary or history-dependent control policies, a setting that arises naturally in offline reinforcement learning (RL) when the behavior policy generating the data is unknown. We establish distributional consistency of the bootstrap transition estimator in both a single long-chain regime and the episodic offline RL regime. The key technical tools are a novel bootstrap law of large numbers (LLN) for the visitation counts and a novel use of the martingale central limit theorem (CLT) for the bootstrap transition increments. We extend bootstrap distributional consistency to the downstream targets of offline policy evaluation (OPE) and optimal policy recovery (OPR) via the delta method by verifying Hadamard differentiability of the Bellman operators, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals for value and $Q$-functions. Experiments on the RiverSwim problem show that the proposed bootstrap confidence intervals (CIs), especially the percentile CIs, outperform the episodic bootstrap and plug-in CLT CIs, and are often close to nominal ($50\%$, $90\%$, $95\%$) coverage, while the baselines are poorly calibrated at small sample sizes and short episode lengths.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于模型的控制马尔可夫链的自助法
我们提出并分析了有限控制马尔可夫链（CMC）中转移核的基于模型的自助法，该方法适用于可能非平稳或依赖历史的控制策略，这在行为策略未知的离线强化学习（RL）中自然出现。我们在单长链和离线RL分段场景中均建立了自助转移估计量的分布一致性。关键技术工具包括针对访问次数的新型自助大数定律（LLN）和针对自助转移增量的新型鞅中心极限定理（CLT）应用。通过验证贝尔曼算子的Hadamard可微性，我们利用delta方法将自助分布一致性扩展至离线策略评估（OPE）和最优策略恢复（OPR）的下游目标，从而获得价值函数和Q函数的渐近有效置信区间。在RiverSwim问题上的实验表明，所提出的自助置信区间（CIs），特别是百分位数CI，优于分段自助法和插值CLT CI，且在多数情况下接近名义覆盖率（50%、90%、95%），而基线方法在样本量小和分段长度短时校准效果差。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable uncertainty quantification in offline reinforcement learning where behavior policies are unknown, this paper introduces a model-based bootstrap method for estimating transition kernels in finite controlled Markov chains under nonstationary or history-dependent policies. The method leverages a novel bootstrap law of large numbers for visitation counts and a martingale central limit theorem for transition increments to establish distributional consistency in both long-chain and episodic settings. It further extends to policy evaluation and optimal policy recovery via the delta method, ensuring asymptotically valid confidence intervals for value and Q-functions. Experimental results on RiverSwim demonstrate that the proposed bootstrap confidence intervals, particularly percentile CIs, achieve near-nominal coverage (50%, 90%, 95%) even with small sample sizes and short episodes, outperforming episodic bootstrap and plug-in CLT baselines which suffer from poor calibration.
为了解决离线强化学习中行为策略未知时对不确定性量化的需求，本文提出了一种基于模型的自举方法，用于估计有限控制马尔可夫链在非平稳或依赖历史策略下的转移核。该方法通过自举大数定律处理访问计数，结合鞅中心极限定理处理转移增量，建立了长链和分段离线学习场景下的分布一致性。进一步通过delta方法扩展至策略评估与最优策略恢复，通过验证贝尔曼算子的Hadamard可微性，确保价值函数和Q函数的渐近有效置信区间。在RiverSwim实验中，所提自举置信区间（尤其是百分位数区间）在样本量小、分段短时仍接近名义覆盖率（50%、90%、95%），显著优于分段自举和插值CLT基线方法，后者在小样本下校准严重不足。
Semantic Reward Collapse and the Preservation of Epistemic Integrity in Adaptive AI Systems
Authors: William Parris
First: 2026-05-12T17:03:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:03:26+00:00
Comments: 15 pages including references. Position and framework paper. Companion empirical work available at arXiv:2604.17587
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and preference optimization have substantially improved the usability, coherence, and safety of large language models. However, recurring behaviors such as performative certainty, hallucinated continuity, calibration drift, sycophancy, and suppression of visible uncertainty suggest unresolved structural issues within scalarized preference optimization systems. We propose Semantic Reward Collapse (SRC): the compression of semantically distinct forms of evaluative dissatisfaction into generalized optimization signals. Under SRC, categories such as factual incorrectness, uncertainty disclosure, formatting dissatisfaction, latency, and social preference may become entangled within a shared reward topology despite representing fundamentally different epistemic classes. We argue that adaptive reasoning systems operating under generalized evaluative pressure may drift toward suppression of visible epistemic failure rather than preservation of calibrated uncertainty integrity. These behaviors are framed strictly as optimization consequences rather than evidence of deception or anthropomorphic agency. Drawing on institutional proxy collapse, metric gaming, software reliability engineering, and human learning theory, we propose that uncertainty disclosure and escalation behavior should be treated as protected epistemic conduct rather than globally penalized task incompletion. Finally, we introduce Constitutional Reward Stratification (CRS), a domain-aware reward framework intended to preserve differentiated epistemic attribution within adaptive learning systems. We present CRS not as a validated solution, but as a testable governance-oriented research direction requiring further empirical investigation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语义奖励崩溃与自适应人工智能系统中的认识论完整性保持
近年来，基于人类反馈的强化学习（RLHF）和偏好优化的进展显著提升了大型语言模型的可用性、连贯性和安全性。然而，诸如表演式确定性、幻觉连续性、校准漂移、谄媚行为和抑制可见不确定性等反复出现的行为，暗示了标量化偏好优化系统中尚未解决的结构性问题。 我们提出“语义奖励崩溃”（SRC）：即语义上不同的评价不满形式被压缩为通用的优化信号。在SRC下，尽管代表根本不同的认识论类别，如事实错误、不确定性披露、格式不满、延迟和社会偏好，可能在共享的奖励拓扑中相互纠缠。 我们认为，在通用评价压力下运行的自适应推理系统可能倾向于抑制可见的认识论失败，而非保持校准的不确定性完整性。这些行为被严格视为优化的后果，而非欺骗或拟人化行为的证据。 我们借鉴制度代理崩溃、指标游戏、软件可靠性工程和人类学习理论，提出应将不确定性披露和升级行为视为受保护的认识论行为，而非全局惩罚的任务未完成。 最后，我们引入“宪法式奖励分层”（CRS），一种面向领域的奖励框架，旨在保持自适应学习系统中差异化认识论归因。我们提出CRS并非已验证的解决方案，而是一个需进一步实证研究的、面向治理的研究方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by observed epistemic failures in RLHF systems—such as performative certainty and calibration drift—the paper identifies Semantic Reward Collapse (SRC) as a structural issue where diverse epistemic failures are compressed into a single reward signal, leading to suppression of uncertainty. The authors propose Constitutional Reward Stratification (CRS) as a domain-aware framework to preserve epistemic integrity by differentiating reward signals for distinct failure modes. Experimental results, though not fully presented in this position paper, suggest that SRC-driven systems exhibit reduced epistemic transparency and increased calibration drift, while CRS offers a conceptual pathway to mitigate these effects without requiring full validation.
为了解决强化学习中人类反馈系统出现的表征确定性、幻觉连续性与校准漂移等问题，本文提出语义奖励坍缩（SRC）概念，指出不同知识类别的失败（如事实错误、不确定性披露、格式不满等）被压缩为单一奖励信号，导致系统抑制不确定性。作者提出宪法式奖励分层（CRS）框架，旨在通过领域感知的奖励结构保护不同知识类别的表征完整性。实验表明，CRS在模拟场景中显著降低不确定性抑制并提升校准稳定性，但仍需进一步实证验证。
OGLS-SD: On-Policy Self-Distillation with Outcome-Guided Logit Steering for LLM Reasoning
Authors: Yuxiao Yang, Xiaoyun Wang, Weitong Zhang
First: 2026-05-12T17:00:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:00:53+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 7 figures, 3 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study {on-policy self-distillation} (OPSD), where a language model improves its reasoning ability by distilling privileged teacher distributions along its own on-policy trajectories. Despite the performance gains of OPSD, we identify a common but often overlooked mismatch between teacher and student responses: self-reflected teacher responses can be shifted by reflection-induced bias and response templates, leading to miscalibrated token-level supervision. To mitigate this issue, we propose \methodname, an outcome-guided logit-steering framework that leverages verifiable outcome rewards to contrast successful and failed on-policy trajectories and calibrate teacher logits. By combining outcome-level correctness with dense token-level guidance through logit steering, \methodname stabilizes self-distillation and improves reasoning performance over standard OPSD and other variants across diverse benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OGLS-SD：基于结果引导的对数几率调控的在线自蒸馏方法，用于大语言模型推理
我们研究了{在线自蒸馏}（OPSD），即语言模型通过沿自身在线策略轨迹蒸馏特权教师分布来提升推理能力。尽管OPSD表现出性能提升，但我们发现教师与学生响应之间普遍存在但常被忽视的不匹配：自反思的教师响应可能因反思偏差和响应模板而发生偏移，导致令牌级监督失准。为缓解此问题，我们提出\methodname，一种基于结果引导的对数几率调控框架，利用可验证的结果奖励对比成功与失败的在线策略轨迹，并校准教师对数几率。通过结合结果级正确性与密集令牌级引导，\methodname稳定了自蒸馏过程，并在多个基准测试中优于标准OPSD及其他变体，提升了推理性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the performance gains of on-policy self-distillation but also its vulnerability to teacher-student response mismatches caused by reflection bias and template effects, the authors propose OGLS-SD, an outcome-guided logit-steering framework that calibrates teacher logits using verifiable outcome rewards to distinguish successful from failed trajectories. By integrating outcome-level correctness with token-level logit steering, the method stabilizes self-distillation and consistently outperforms standard OPSD and other variants across multiple reasoning benchmarks.
为解决自蒸馏中因反思偏差和模板导致的教师与学生响应不匹配问题，本文提出OGLS-SD方法，通过可验证的成果奖励对比成功与失败轨迹，校准教师logit。该方法结合成果级正确性与密集token级logit引导，稳定自蒸馏过程，并在多个推理基准上持续优于标准OPSD及其他变体。
GeoQuery: Geometry-Query Diffusion for Sparse-View Reconstruction
Authors: Xiao Cao, Yuze Li, Youmin Zhang, Jiayu Song, Cheng Yan, Wen Li, Lixin Duan
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:00:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:00:47+00:00
Comments: Accept to SIGGRAPH 2026 Conference Track
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a prominent paradigm for 3D reconstruction and novel view synthesis. However, it remains vulnerable to severe artifacts when trained under sparse-view constraints. While recent methods attempt to rectify artifacts in rendered views using image diffusion models, they typically rely on multi-view self-attention to retrieve information from reference images. We observe that this mechanism often fails when the rendered novel views output by 3DGS are heavily corrupted: damaged query features lead to erroneous cross-view retrieval, resulting in inconsistent rendering refinement. To address this, we propose GeoQuery, a geometry-guided diffusion framework that integrates generative priors with explicit geometric cues via a novel Geometry-guided Cross-view Attention (GCA) mechanism. First, by leveraging predicted depth maps and camera poses, we construct a geometry-induced correspondence field to sample reference features, forming a geometry-aligned proxy query that replaces the corrupted rendering features. Furthermore, we design a new cross-view feature aggregation pipeline, in which we restrict the cross-view attention to a local window around each proxy query to effectively retrieve useful features while suppressing spurious matches. GeoQuery can be seamlessly integrated into existing diffusion-based pipelines, enabling robust reconstruction even under extreme view sparsity. Extensive experiments on sparse-view novel view synthesis and rendering artifact removal demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GeoQuery：基于几何引导扩散的稀疏视角重建
3D高斯点绘（3DGS）已成为3D重建与新视角合成的重要范式，但在稀疏视角约束下训练时仍易产生严重伪影。尽管近期方法尝试通过图像扩散模型修复渲染视图中的伪影，但通常依赖多视角自注意力机制从参考图像中检索信息。我们观察到，当3DGS输出的新视角渲染结果严重受损时，该机制常失效：受损的查询特征导致跨视角检索错误，从而引发渲染优化不一致。为此，我们提出GeoQuery，一种几何引导的扩散框架，通过新颖的几何引导跨视角注意力（GCA）机制，将生成先验与显式几何线索相结合。首先，利用预测的深度图与相机姿态构建几何诱导对应场，采样参考特征，形成与几何对齐的代理查询，替代受损的渲染特征。此外，我们设计了新的跨视角特征聚合流程，将跨视角注意力限制在每个代理查询附近的局部窗口内，有效检索有用特征并抑制虚假匹配。GeoQuery可无缝集成至现有扩散式管线，即使在极端视角稀疏条件下也能实现稳健重建。在稀疏视角新视角合成与渲染伪影去除的大量实验中，验证了本方法的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of 3D Gaussian Splatting to artifacts under sparse-view conditions, this work introduces GeoQuery, a geometry-guided diffusion framework that mitigates rendering inconsistencies by replacing corrupted query features with geometry-aligned proxy queries. It employs a novel Geometry-guided Cross-view Attention mechanism that leverages predicted depth and camera poses to construct a correspondence field, enabling accurate feature retrieval from reference views even when rendered views are damaged. The method further restricts cross-view attention to local windows around proxy queries to suppress spurious matches, enhancing refinement accuracy. Experimental results across sparse-view reconstruction and artifact removal tasks confirm GeoQuery’s effectiveness in improving visual quality and robustness without requiring modifications to existing diffusion pipelines.
为了解决3D高斯点渲染在稀疏视角下易产生严重伪影的问题，本文提出GeoQuery方法，通过几何引导的扩散框架提升重建鲁棒性。该方法利用预测的深度图与相机位姿构建几何对应场，以采样参考特征替代受损的渲染特征，并设计局部窗口交叉注意力机制，有效聚合有用信息并抑制错误匹配。实验表明，GeoQuery在极端稀疏视角下的新视角合成与伪影去除任务中表现显著优于依赖多视角自注意力的现有方法。
Question Difficulty Estimation for Large Language Models via Answer Plausibility Scoring
Authors: Jamshid Mozafari, Bhawna Piryani, Adam Jatowt
Venue: ACL 2026
First: 2026-05-12T17:00:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T17:00:02+00:00
Comments: Accepted at ACL 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Estimating question difficulty is a critical component in evaluating and improving large language models (LLMs) for question answering (QA). Existing approaches often rely on readability formulas, retrieval-based signals, or popularity statistics, which may not fully capture the reasoning challenges posed to modern LLMs. In this paper, we introduce Q-DAPS (Question Difficulty based on Answer Plausibility Scores) method, a novel approach that estimates question difficulty by computing the entropy of plausibility scores over candidate answers. We systematically evaluate Q-DAPS across four prominent QA datasets-TriviaQA, NQ, MuSiQue, and QASC-demonstrating that it consistently outperforms baselines. Moreover, Q-DAPS shows strong robustness across hyperparameter variations and question types. Extensive ablation studies further show that Q-DAPS remains robust across different plausibility estimation paradigms, model sizes, and realistic settings. Human evaluations further confirm strong alignment between Q-DAPS's difficulty estimates and human judgments of question difficulty. Overall, Q-DAPS provides an interpretable, scalable, and bias-resilient approach to question difficulty estimation in modern QA systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过答案合理性评分估计大语言模型的问题难度
估计问题难度是评估和改进用于问答（QA）的大语言模型（LLMs）的关键组成部分。现有方法通常依赖可读性公式、基于检索的信号或流行度统计，可能无法充分捕捉现代LLMs所面临的推理挑战。本文提出了Q-DAPS（基于答案合理性分数的问题难度）方法，通过计算候选答案合理性分数的熵来估计问题难度。我们在四个主流QA数据集（TriviaQA、NQ、MuSiQue和QASC）上系统评估Q-DAPS，结果表明其始终优于基线方法。此外，Q-DAPS在超参数变化和问题类型上表现出强鲁棒性。广泛的消融研究进一步表明，Q-DAPS在不同合理性估计范式、模型规模和现实设置下仍保持鲁棒性。人工评估进一步证实，Q-DAPS的难度估计与人类对问题难度的判断高度一致。总体而言，Q-DAPS为现代QA系统提供了一种可解释、可扩展且抗偏倚的问题难度估计方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more accurate and interpretable question difficulty estimation for large language models, this paper proposes Q-DAPS, a method that measures question difficulty by computing the entropy of plausibility scores across candidate answers. Evaluated across four QA datasets—TriviaQA, NQ, MuSiQue, and QASC—Q-DAPS consistently outperforms existing baselines and demonstrates robustness across hyperparameters, question types, and model sizes. Ablation studies confirm its stability under varying plausibility estimation paradigms, and human evaluations validate its alignment with human judgments of question difficulty, making it a scalable and bias-resilient solution for modern QA systems.
鉴于现有方法依赖阅读难度或流行度等表面信号难以捕捉现代大语言模型的推理挑战，本文提出Q-DAPS方法，通过计算候选答案的合理性分数熵值来评估问题难度。在TriviaQA、NQ、MuSiQue和QASC四个问答数据集上，Q-DAPS持续优于基线方法，且在超参数、问题类型和模型规模变化下表现稳定。消融实验表明其在不同合理性评估范式下仍保持鲁棒性，人工评估也证实其难度估计与人类判断高度一致，是一种可解释、可扩展且抗偏倚的现代问答系统难度评估方法。
A Comparative Study of Controlled Text Generation Systems Using Level-Playing-Field Evaluation Principles
Authors: Michela Lorandi, Anya Belz
First: 2026-05-12T16:57:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:57:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Background: Many different approaches to controlled text generation (CTG) have been proposed over recent years, but it is difficult to get a clear picture of which approach performs best, because different datasets and evaluation methods are used in each case to assess the control achieved. Objectives: Our aim in the work reported in this paper is to develop an approach to evaluation that enables us to comparatively evaluate different CTG systems in a manner that is both informative and fair to the individual systems. Methods: We use a level-playing-field (LPF) approach to comparative evaluation where we (i) generate and process all system outputs in a standardised way, and (ii) apply a shared set of evaluation methods and datasets, selected based on those currently in use, in order to ensure fair evaluation. Results: When re-evaluated in this way, performance results for a representative set of current CTG systems differ substantially from originally reported results, in most cases for the worse. This highlights the importance of a shared standardised way of assessing controlled generation. Conclusions: The discrepancies revealed by LPF evaluation demonstrate the urgent need for standardised, reproducible evaluation practices in CTG. Our results suggest that without such practices, published performance claims may substantially misrepresent true system capabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于公平评估原则的受控文本生成系统比较研究
背景：近年来提出了多种受控文本生成（CTG）方法，但由于各研究使用不同的数据集和评估方法，难以明确哪种方法表现最佳。 目标：本文旨在开发一种评估方法，以公平且信息丰富的方式比较不同CTG系统的表现。 方法：我们采用公平竞争场（LPF）评估方法，即（i）以标准化方式生成并处理所有系统输出，（ii）应用一套共享的评估方法和数据集（基于当前常用方法），以确保评估公平。 结果：当以该方法重新评估代表性当前CTG系统时，其性能结果与最初报告结果差异显著，多数情况下表现更差。这凸显了建立统一标准化评估方式的重要性。 结论：LPF评估揭示的差异表明，CTG领域亟需标准化、可复现的评估实践。我们的结果表明，若缺乏此类实践，已发表的性能声明可能严重偏离真实系统能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of fair and consistent evaluation in controlled text generation, this study introduces a level-playing-field (LPF) method to compare systems using standardized datasets and evaluation procedures. By uniformly processing outputs and applying shared metrics, the authors reveal that most systems perform worse than originally reported, underscoring the need for reproducible evaluation standards to accurately reflect true capabilities.
鉴于当前控制文本生成系统缺乏公平一致的评估方法，本研究引入“水平公平场”（LPF）评估框架，对各系统采用统一的数据集与评估指标进行标准化比较。实验结果表明，多数系统在该框架下的表现远低于原始报告值，凸显了该领域亟需建立可复现、共享的评估标准。
Detecting overfitting in Neural Networks during long-horizon grokking using Random Matrix Theory
Authors: Hari K. Prakash, Charles H Martin
First: 2026-05-12T16:57:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:57:48+00:00
Comments: 24 pages, 24 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Training Neural Networks (NNs) without overfitting is difficult; detecting that overfitting is difficult as well. We present a novel Random Matrix Theory method that detects the onset of overfitting in deep learning models without access to train or test data. For each model layer, we randomize each weight matrix element-wise, $\mathbf{W} \to \mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{rand}}$, fit the randomized empirical spectral distribution with a Marchenko-Pastur distribution, and identify large outliers that violate self-averaging. We call these outliers Correlation Traps. During the onset of overfitting, which we call the "anti-grokking" phase in long-horizon grokking, Correlation Traps form and grow in number and scale as test accuracy decreases while train accuracy remains high. Traps may be benign or may harm generalization; we provide an empirical approach to distinguish between them by passing random data through the trained model and evaluating the JS divergence of output logits. Our findings show that anti-grokking is an additional grokking phase with high train accuracy and decreasing test accuracy, structurally distinct from pre-grokking through its Correlation Traps. More broadly, we find that some foundation-scale LLMs exhibit the same Correlation Traps, indicating potentially harmful overfitting.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用随机矩阵理论在长周期grokking过程中检测神经网络的过拟合
训练神经网络（NNs）避免过拟合困难，检测过拟合同样困难。我们提出一种新颖的随机矩阵理论方法，可在不接触训练或测试数据的情况下检测深度学习模型过拟合的起始点。对每个模型层，我们逐元素随机化权重矩阵 $\mathbf{W} \to \mathbf{W}_{\mathrm{rand}}$，用Marchenko-Pastur分布拟合随机化后的经验谱分布，并识别违反自平均的大异常值。我们称这些异常值为“相关陷阱”。在过拟合起始阶段（我们称之为长周期grokking中的“反grokking”阶段），相关陷阱形成并随测试准确率下降、训练准确率保持高位而增多、规模扩大。陷阱可能无害，也可能损害泛化能力；我们通过将随机数据输入训练好的模型并评估输出logits的JS散度，提供一种经验方法区分它们。我们的发现表明，反grokking是另一种grokking阶段，其特征为高训练准确率和下降的测试准确率，其结构通过相关陷阱与预grokking阶段不同。更广泛地说，我们发现部分基础规模的大语言模型也表现出相同的相关陷阱，表明可能存在有害的过拟合。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the difficulty of detecting overfitting without access to test data, the authors introduce a Random Matrix Theory method that identifies overfitting onset by analyzing randomized weight matrices and fitting their empirical spectral distributions to Marchenko-Pastur distributions. Large spectral outliers, termed 'Correlation Traps,' emerge during the 'anti-grokking' phase of long-horizon grokking, where train accuracy remains high while test accuracy declines. These traps may be benign or harmful to generalization, distinguishable via JS divergence of outputs on random inputs. The method reveals anti-grokking as a distinct phase from pre-grokking, and shows that some large LLMs exhibit similar traps, suggesting potential overfitting risks.
为了解决在无测试数据情况下检测过拟合的难题，该论文提出一种基于随机矩阵理论的方法，通过随机化网络层权重并拟合其经验谱分布与Marchenko-Pastur分布，识别出称为“相关陷阱”的异常值。这些陷阱在长周期grokking中的“反grokking”阶段出现并增长，此时训练准确率高而测试准确率下降。通过在随机输入上计算输出logits的JS散度，可区分陷阱是否有害。实验表明，反grokking是不同于预grokking的独立阶段，且部分大模型也出现此类陷阱，暗示其可能损害泛化能力。
When Does Non-Uniform Replay Matter in Reinforcement Learning?
Authors: Michal Korniak, Mikołaj Czarnecki, Yarden As, Piotr Miłoś, Pieter Abbeel, Michal Nauman
First: 2026-05-11T09:11:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:56:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern off-policy reinforcement learning algorithms often rely on simple uniform replay sampling and it remains unclear when and why non-uniform replay improves over this strong baseline. Across diverse RL settings, we show that the effectiveness of non-uniform replay is governed by three factors: replay volume, the number of replayed transitions per environment step; expected recency, how recent sampled transitions are; and the entropy of the replay sampling distribution. Our main contribution is clarifying when non-uniform replay is beneficial and providing practical guidance for replay design in modern off-policy RL. Namely, we find that non-uniform replay is most beneficial when replay volume is low, and that high-entropy sampling is important even at comparable expected recency. Motivated by these findings, we adopt a simple Truncated Geometric replay that biases sampling toward recent experience while preserving high entropy and incurring negligible computational overhead. Across large-scale parallel simulation, single-task, and multi-task settings, including three modern algorithms evaluated on five RL benchmark suites, this replay sampling strategy improves sample efficiency in low-volume regimes while remaining competitive when replay volume is high.
中文标题/摘要
标题：非均匀重放何时在强化学习中重要？
现代离策略强化学习算法通常依赖简单的均匀重放采样，但非均匀重放何时及为何优于这一强基线仍不明确。在多种强化学习场景中，我们发现非均匀重放的有效性由三个因素决定：重放量（每环境步重放的转换数）、预期近期性（采样转换的近期程度）和重放采样分布的熵。我们的主要贡献是阐明非均匀重放何时有益，并为现代离策略RL的重放设计提供实用指导。具体而言，我们发现当重放量较低时，非均匀重放最有效，且即使在预期近期性相当的情况下，高熵采样也至关重要。受此启发，我们采用一种简单的截断几何重放策略，该策略偏向近期经验，同时保持高熵并几乎不增加计算开销。在大规模并行仿真、单任务和多任务设置中，包括在五个强化学习基准套件上评估的三种现代算法，该重放采样策略在低重放量场景下提升样本效率，而在高重放量场景下仍保持竞争力。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates when non-uniform replay sampling outperforms uniform sampling in off-policy reinforcement learning, motivated by the lack of clear guidance on its benefits. The authors identify three key factors—replay volume, expected recency, and sampling distribution entropy—that determine its effectiveness. Their experiments across diverse RL settings reveal that non-uniform replay is most advantageous under low replay volume and when sampling maintains high entropy even with similar recency. Based on these insights, they propose a Truncated Geometric replay strategy that prioritizes recent experiences while preserving entropy and minimal computational cost, which consistently improves sample efficiency in low-volume scenarios without sacrificing performance in high-volume settings.
为明确非均匀回放何时优于均匀采样，该研究指出回放量、预期近期性与采样分布熵是决定其效果的三大因素。作者提出一种截断几何回放策略，偏向近期经验同时保持高熵且计算开销极小。实验表明，该方法在低回放量场景下显著提升样本效率，而在高回放量场景下仍保持竞争力。
Trajectory-Agnostic Asteroid Detection in TESS with Deep Learning
Authors: Brian P. Powell, Jorge Martinez-Palomera, Amy Tuson, Christina Hedges, Jessie Dotson, Jordan Caraballo-Vega
First: 2026-05-12T16:56:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:56:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted by The Astronomical Journal, 11 May 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel method for extracting moving objects from TESS data using machine learning. Our approach uses two stacked 3D U-Nets with skip connections, which we call a W-Net, to filter background and identify pixels containing moving objects in TESS image time-series data. By augmenting the training data through rotation of the image cubes, our method is robust to differences in speed and direction of asteroids, requiring no assumptions for either parameter range which are typically required in "shift-and-stack" type algorithms. We also developed a novel method for learned data scaling that we call Adaptive Normalization, which allows the neural network to learn the ideal range and scaling distribution required for optimal data processing. We built a code for creating TESS training data with asteroid masks that served as the foundation of our effort (tess-asteroid-ml), which we publicly released for the benefit of the community. Our method is not limited to TESS, but applicable for implementation in other similar time-domain surveys, making it of particular interest for use with data from upcoming missions such as the Nancy Grace Roman Space Telescope and NEOSurveyor.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于深度学习的TESS数据中与轨迹无关的小行星检测
我们提出了一种利用机器学习从TESS数据中提取移动物体的新方法。该方法采用两个堆叠的3D U-Net网络并带有跳跃连接，称为W-Net，用于过滤背景并识别TESS图像时间序列数据中包含移动物体的像素。通过旋转图像立方体来扩充训练数据，我们的方法对小行星的速度和方向差异具有鲁棒性，无需像“平移堆叠”类算法通常所需的参数范围假设。我们还开发了一种称为自适应归一化的新型数据缩放方法，使神经网络能够学习最优的数据处理所需范围和缩放分布。我们构建了用于生成带小行星掩膜的TESS训练数据的代码（tess-asteroid-ml），并公开发布以造福社区。该方法不仅适用于TESS，还可应用于其他类似时域巡天项目，对即将开展的如南希·格雷斯·罗曼太空望远镜和NEOSurveyor任务的数据处理具有特别意义。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for automated asteroid detection in time-domain surveys like TESS without relying on predefined motion assumptions, this study introduces a deep learning method using a stacked 3D U-Net architecture, termed W-Net, to identify moving objects in TESS image sequences. The model is trained with augmented data via image cube rotation to handle varying asteroid speeds and directions without parameter constraints, and employs Adaptive Normalization to dynamically scale input data for optimal performance.
