Experimental results demonstrate high detection accuracy and robustness across diverse asteroid trajectories, outperforming traditional shift-and-stack methods, and the approach is validated to be broadly applicable to future missions such as Nancy Grace Roman and NEOSurveyor.
为解决在TESS等时域巡天数据中自动检测小行星且无需预设运动参数的问题，作者提出了一种基于深度学习的方法，采用具有跳跃连接的堆叠3D U-Net架构（称为W-Net）来识别时序图像中的移动目标。模型通过图像立方体旋转增强训练数据，使其对小行星速度和方向变化具有鲁棒性，并引入自适应归一化技术动态调整输入数据尺度，提升网络性能。实验结果表明，该方法在检测精度上优于传统移位堆叠算法，且具有良好的泛化能力，其生成训练数据的代码库已公开，便于未来巡天任务（如罗马太空望远镜和NEOSurveyor）应用。
When Sounds Hurt and Voices Aren't Heard: An Experience Report on Misophonia, Sensory Trauma, and Trauma-Informed Design
Authors: Tawfiq Ammari
First: 2026-05-10T22:33:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:54:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This experience report reflects on researching misophonia as someone who lives with it. Misophonia is an aversive response to everyday sounds (chewing, sniffling, pen clicking) and, for many of us, to associated visual cues (misokinesia). It is poorly recognized clinically and socially. People with misophonia are routinely disbelieved, and they live inside platform surfaces (auto-playing audio, algorithmic ASMR, normalized eating on camera) that turn the sensory environment itself into recurring distress. This report is a re-reading of a prior qualitative study of 16 semi-structured interviews with misophones, conducted in dialogue with my lived experience and my role in the soQuiet Misophonia Research Network. I extend the trauma-informed design (TID) conversation in two ways. First, TID must treat embodied, contested conditions as sources of both sensory and epistemic harm: ongoing trauma produced by the audiovisual surface and by repeated dismissal of users' accounts of their bodies. Second, the closed groups and moderated subreddits participants relied on can reproduce that dismissal when a few moderators decide whose experiences count. I close with implications for ASSETS.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当声音带来伤害而声音无人倾听：关于选择性听觉过敏、感官创伤与创伤知情设计的体验报告
本体验报告基于本人作为选择性听觉过敏患者的研究经历。选择性听觉过敏是对日常声音（如咀嚼、打喷嚏、笔点击声）及关联视觉线索（如视觉触发反应）的厌恶反应，临床与社会对其认知不足。患者常被忽视，生活在自动播放音频、算法ASMR、镜头前正常进食等平台环境中，这些环境将感官世界本身转化为持续性痛苦。本报告是对先前对16位选择性听觉过敏患者进行的半结构化访谈的再解读，结合本人亲身经历及在soQuiet选择性听觉过敏研究网络中的角色。报告从两个方面拓展了创伤知情设计（TID）的讨论：其一，TID必须将具身化、争议性的处境视为感官与认知伤害的来源——由视听表面持续制造的创伤，以及用户对自身身体体验被反复否定所引发的创伤；其二，参与者依赖的封闭社群与经版主管理的子版块，当少数版主决定谁的经验“值得被倾听”时，也可能再现这种否定。报告最后探讨了对ASSETS（无障碍与包容性设计）的启示。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by personal experience with misophonia and its social invisibility, the author examines how everyday sounds and visual cues trigger intense distress, often dismissed by others and amplified by digital platforms. Drawing on 16 semi-structured interviews and their own lived experience, the paper argues that trauma-informed design must address both sensory harm and epistemic harm—how users’ bodily experiences are invalidated by platform design and moderation practices. The study reveals that even supportive online communities can replicate exclusion by privileging certain voices, reinforcing trauma through silence or dismissal.
基于作者亲身经历的‘听觉过敏’（misophonia）——对日常声音及视觉线索产生强烈不适反应——本报告探讨社会与数字环境如何加剧感官创伤。结合16次半结构化访谈与soQuiet研究网络经验，作者扩展了创伤知情设计（TID）的框架，将misophonia视为感官与认知层面的持续伤害，源于算法音频环境与用户身体经验被忽视。研究发现，即使在支持性社群中，少数管理员的裁量权也可能导致经验被排斥，凸显ASSETS设计需优先保障用户身体自主性与感官安全。
One Turn Too Late: Response-Aware Defense Against Hidden Malicious Intent in Multi-Turn Dialogue
Authors: Xinjie Shen, Rongzhe Wei, Peizhi Niu, Haoyu Wang, Ruihan Wu, Eli Chien, Bo Li, Pin-Yu Chen, Pan Li
First: 2026-05-07T03:35:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:52:21+00:00
Comments: Project Website: https://turn-gate.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Hidden malicious intent in multi-turn dialogue poses a growing threat to deployed large language models (LLMs). Rather than exposing a harmful objective in a single prompt, increasingly capable attackers can distribute their intent across multiple benign-looking turns. Recent studies show that even modern commercial models with advanced guardrails remain vulnerable to such attacks despite advances in safety alignment and external guardrails. In this work, we address this challenge by detecting the earliest turn at which delivering the candidate response would make the accumulated interaction sufficient to enable harmful action. This objective requires precise turn-level intervention that identifies the harm-enabling closure point while avoiding premature refusal of benign exploratory conversations. To further support training and evaluation, we construct the Multi-Turn Intent Dataset (MTID), which contains branching attack rollouts, matched benign hard negatives, and annotations of the earliest harm-enabling turns. We show that MTID helps enable a turn-level monitor TurnGate, which substantially outperforms existing baselines in harmful-intent detection while maintaining low over-refusal rates. TurnGate further generalizes across domains, attacker pipelines, and target models. Our code is available at https://github.com/Graph-COM/TurnGate.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一步之差：针对多轮对话中隐藏恶意意图的响应感知防御
多轮对话中的隐藏恶意意图对已部署的大语言模型（LLM）构成日益严重的威胁。与单次提示中暴露有害目标不同，日益强大的攻击者可将恶意意图分散在多个看似无害的对话轮次中。近期研究表明，即使具备先进安全护栏的现代商业模型，仍易受此类攻击影响，尽管安全对齐和外部护栏技术已取得进展。本研究通过检测最早使候选响应足以促成有害行为的对话轮次，来应对这一挑战。该目标要求精确的轮次级干预，以识别有害行为的触发点，同时避免过早拒绝良性探索性对话。为支持训练与评估，我们构建了多轮意图数据集（MTID），包含分支攻击路径、匹配的良性难负样本及最早有害触发轮次标注。实验表明，MTID有助于实现轮次级监控器TurnGate，在有害意图检测方面显著优于现有基线，同时保持较低的误拒率。TurnGate还能跨领域、攻击管道和目标模型泛化。代码开源于https://github.com/Graph-COM/TurnGate。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the rising threat of hidden malicious intent in multi-turn dialogue that evades detection through distributed, benign-seeming prompts, this work introduces TurnGate, a turn-level defense mechanism that identifies the earliest interaction turn at which a response would enable harmful action. It trains on the Multi-Turn Intent Dataset (MTID), a curated dataset with branching attack sequences, benign hard negatives, and annotated harm-enabling turn labels. Experimental results show TurnGate significantly outperforms existing baselines in detecting harmful intent while maintaining low over-refusal rates and generalizing across domains, attacker strategies, and target models.
针对多轮对话中隐藏恶意意图难以被单轮检测识别的问题，本文提出TurnGate机制，通过识别最早使对话累积效果足以触发有害行为的回合，实现精准的回合级干预。该方法依托新构建的多轮意图数据集MTID，其中包含攻击路径、良性对抗样本及最早有害触发回合标注，用于训练与评估。实验表明，TurnGate在有害意图检测上显著优于现有基线，同时保持较低误拒率，并能跨领域、攻击策略和目标模型泛化。
SEMIR: Semantic Minor-Induced Representation Learning on Graphs for Visual Segmentation
Authors: Luke James Miller, Yugyung Lee
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-12T16:52:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:52:02+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 3 figures. Accepted at ICML 2026. Includes appendices
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Segmenting small and sparse structures in large-scale images is fundamentally constrained by voxel-level, lattice-bound computation and extreme class imbalance -- dense, full-resolution inference scales poorly and forces most pipelines to rely on fixed regionization or downsampling, coupling computational cost to image resolution and attenuating boundary evidence precisely where minority structures are most informative. We introduce SEMIR (Semantic Minor-Induced Representation Learning), a representation framework that decouples inference from the native grid by learning a task-adapted, topology-preserving latent graph representation with exact decoding. SEMIR transforms the underlying grid graph into a compact, boundary-aligned graph minor through parameterized edge contraction, node deletion, and edge deletion, while preserving an exact lifting map from minor predictions to lattice labels. Minor construction is formalized as a few-shot structure learning problem that replaces hand-tuned preprocessing with a boundary-alignment objective: minor parameters are learned by maximizing agreement between predicted boundary elements and target-specific semantic edges under a boundary Dice criterion, and the induced minor is annotated with scale- and rotation-robust geometric and intensity descriptors and supports efficient region-level inference via message passing on a graph neural network (GNN) with relational edge features. We benchmark SEMIR on three tumor segmentation datasets -- BraTS 2021, KiTS23, and LiTS -- where targets exhibit high structural variability and distributional uncertainty. SEMIR yields consistent improvements in minority-structure Dice at practical runtime. More broadly, SEMIR establishes a framework for learning task-adapted, topology-preserving latent representations with exact decoding for high-resolution structured visual data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SEMIR：面向视觉分割的语义小结构诱导图表示学习
在大规模图像中分割小而稀疏的结构，本质上受限于体素级、网格绑定的计算和极端类别不平衡——密集、全分辨率推理难以扩展，迫使大多数流程依赖固定区域划分或下采样，将计算成本与图像分辨率耦合，并在少数结构最具有信息量的边界处削弱边界证据。我们提出SEMIR（语义小结构诱导表示学习），一种表示框架，通过学习任务自适应、拓扑保持的潜在图表示并实现精确解码，将推理与原生网格解耦。SEMIR通过参数化边收缩、节点删除和边删除，将底层网格图转换为紧凑的边界对齐图小结构，同时保留从小结构预测到格点标签的精确提升映射。小结构构建形式化为少样本结构学习问题，用边界对齐目标替代人工调优的预处理：小结构参数通过最大化预测边界元素与目标特定语义边在边界Dice准则下的一致性进行学习，诱导的小结构被标注为尺度和旋转鲁棒的几何与强度描述符，并支持通过具有关系边特征的图神经网络（GNN）进行高效区域级推理。我们在三个肿瘤分割数据集（BraTS 2021、KiTS23、LiTS）上对SEMIR进行基准测试，这些数据集目标具有高结构变异性和分布不确定性。SEMIR在实用运行时持续提升少数结构的Dice值。更广泛地说，SEMIR建立了一种框架，用于学习任务自适应、拓扑保持的潜在表示并实现精确解码，适用于高分辨率结构化视觉数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency and boundary degradation in segmenting small, sparse structures in large-scale images, SEMIR introduces a representation learning framework that decouples inference from voxel grids by constructing a topology-preserving latent graph minor. It achieves this via parameterized edge and node operations while preserving a precise mapping to original lattice labels. The minor is learned through a few-shot structure optimization that maximizes boundary alignment under a Dice criterion, and is annotated with geometric and intensity features to enable efficient region-level inference via graph neural networks. Experiments on BraTS 2021, KiTS23, and LiTS show consistent improvements in Dice scores for minority structures without sacrificing runtime efficiency.
为解决高分辨率图像中细小稀疏结构分割时因网格计算和类别不平衡导致的效率低下与边界信息丢失问题，SEMIR提出一种语义小结构诱导的图表示学习框架，通过参数化边与节点操作构建拓扑保持的图小结构，保留精确的网格映射。其参数通过边界Dice目标学习，使预测边界与语义边对齐，支持通过图神经网络进行高效区域级推理。在BraTS 2021、KiTS23和LiTS三个肿瘤分割数据集上，SEMIR在不增加运行时间的前提下，持续提升少数结构的Dice得分。
Sparsity-Constraint Optimization via Splicing Iteration
Authors: Jin Zhu, Junxian Zhu, Zezhi Wang, Borui Tang, Hongmei Lin, Xueqin Wang
First: 2024-06-17T18:34:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:51:47+00:00
Comments: 35 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparsity-constrained optimization underlies many problems in signal processing, statistics, and machine learning. State-of-the-art hard-thresholding (HT) algorithms rely on an appropriately selected continuous step-size parameter to ensure convergence. In this paper, we propose a naturally convergent iterative algorithm, SCOPE (Sparsity-Constrained Optimization via sPlicing itEration). The algorithm is capable of optimizing nonlinear differentiable objective functions that are strongly convex and smooth on low-dimensional subspaces. SCOPE replaces the gradient step with a splicing operation guided directly by the objective value, thereby eliminating the need to tune any continuous hyperparameter. Theoretically, it achieves a linear convergence rate and recovers the true support set when the sparsity level is correctly specified. We also establish parallel theoretical results without relying on restricted-isometry-property-type conditions. We apply SCOPE's versatility and power to solve sparse quadratic optimization, learn sparse classifiers, and recover sparse Markov networks for binary variables. With our C++ implementation of SCOPE, numerical experiments on these tasks show that it achieves superior support recovery performance, confirming both its algorithmic efficiency and theoretical guarantees.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过拼接迭代实现稀疏约束优化
稀疏约束优化是信号处理、统计学和机器学习中许多问题的基础。当前最先进的硬阈值（HT）算法依赖于适当选择的连续步长参数以确保收敛。本文提出了一种自然收敛的迭代算法SCOPE（通过拼接迭代实现稀疏约束优化）。该算法能够优化在低维子空间上强凸且光滑的非线性可微目标函数。SCOPE用由目标值直接引导的拼接操作替代梯度步，从而无需调整任何连续超参数。理论上，它实现线性收敛速率，并在稀疏度正确指定时恢复真实支撑集。我们还建立了无需依赖受限等距性质类条件的并行理论结果。我们将SCOPE的通用性和强大能力应用于稀疏二次优化、学习稀疏分类器以及恢复二元变量的稀疏马尔可夫网络。通过我们的SCOPE C++实现，数值实验表明其在这些任务中实现了优越的支撑集恢复性能，验证了其算法效率和理论保证。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for sparsity-constrained optimization in signal processing and machine learning without tuning complex hyperparameters, this paper introduces SCOPE, an iterative algorithm that replaces gradient steps with a splicing operation guided by objective values. The method ensures convergence without requiring a continuous step-size parameter and achieves linear convergence rates under strong convexity and smoothness assumptions. Experimental results demonstrate SCOPE’s superior support recovery performance across sparse quadratic optimization, sparse classification, and sparse Markov network recovery, validating both its theoretical guarantees and practical efficiency.
为了解决信号处理和机器学习中对稀疏约束优化的需求，且无需调整复杂超参数，本文提出了SCOPE算法，该算法用由目标值引导的拼接操作替代梯度步骤。该方法无需连续步长参数即可保证收敛，具有线性收敛速率，并在稀疏性正确设定时能恢复真实支撑集。在稀疏二次优化、稀疏分类和稀疏马尔可夫网络恢复等任务上的实验表明，SCOPE在支撑集恢复性能上优于现有方法，验证了其理论保证和实际效率。
Events as Triggers for Behavioral Diversity in Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Hannes Büchi, Manon Flageat, Eduardo Sebastián, Amanda Prorok
First: 2026-05-12T16:51:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:51:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Effective multi-agent cooperation requires agents to adopt diverse behaviors as task conditions evolve-and to do so at the right moment. Yet, current Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) frameworks that facilitate this diversity are still limited by the fact that they bind fixed behaviors to fixed agent identities. Consequently, they are ill-equipped for tasks where agents need to take on different roles at very specific moments in time. We argue that, to define these behavioral transitions, the missing ingredient is events. Events are changes in the state of the system that induce qualitative changes in the task. Based on this view, we introduce a framework that decouples agent identity from behavior, capturing a continuous manifold from which agents instantiate their behaviors in response to events. This framework is based on two elements. First, to build an expressive behavior manifold, we introduce Neural Manifold Diversity (NMD), a formal distance metric that remains well-defined when behaviors are transient and agent-agnostic. Second, we use an event-based hypernetwork that generates Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) modules over a shared team policy, enabling on-the-fly agent-policy reconfiguration in response to events. We prove that this construction ensures that diversity does not interfere with reward maximization by design. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework outperforms established baselines across benchmarks while exhibiting zero-shot generalization, and being the only method that solves tasks requiring sequential behavior reassignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：事件作为多智能体强化学习中行为多样性的触发器
有效的多智能体协作要求智能体在任务条件演变时采用多样化行为，并在恰当时刻执行。然而，当前促进行为多样性的多智能体强化学习（MARL）框架仍受限于将固定行为绑定到固定智能体身份。因此，它们难以应对智能体需在特定时间点切换不同角色的任务。我们认为，定义这些行为转换所缺失的关键要素是事件——即引发任务质性变化的系统状态变化。基于此观点，我们提出一种将智能体身份与行为解耦的框架，使智能体能根据事件从连续行为流中实例化行为。该框架包含两个要素：首先，为构建表达力强的行为流，我们引入神经流多样性（NMD），一种在行为瞬态且与智能体无关时仍保持良好定义的形式化距离度量；其次，我们使用基于事件的超网络，在共享团队策略上生成低秩适配（LoRA）模块，实现对事件的即时智能体策略重构。我们证明，该架构设计上确保多样性不会干扰奖励最大化。实验证明，我们的框架在多个基准测试中优于现有基线，具备零样本泛化能力，且是唯一能解决需顺序行为重分配任务的方法。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the limitation in current Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning frameworks that tie fixed behaviors to fixed agent identities, making them unsuitable for tasks requiring dynamic role-switching at precise moments. To resolve this, the authors propose a framework that decouples agent identity from behavior by introducing events—system state changes that trigger qualitative task shifts—as the mechanism for behavioral transitions. Their method combines Neural Manifold Diversity (NMD), a distance metric for measuring behavior diversity even when behaviors are transient and agent-agnostic, with an event-driven hypernetwork that generates Low-Rank Adaptation modules over a shared policy to enable real-time agent reconfiguration. Experiments show the framework outperforms existing baselines across multiple benchmarks, achieves zero-shot generalization, and is the only method capable of solving tasks that demand sequential behavior reassignment.
该论文指出当前多智能体强化学习框架将行为固定绑定到智能体身份，难以应对动态任务中所需的灵活行为切换。为此，作者提出一种基于“事件”（系统状态变化触发任务质变）的框架，解耦智能体身份与行为。方法包括：引入神经流形多样性（NMD）度量，支持瞬态、无智能体依赖的行为比较；并使用事件驱动的超网络生成低秩适配模块，动态重构团队策略。实验表明，该框架在多个基准上超越现有方法，具备零样本泛化能力，且是唯一能解决需顺序行为重分配任务的方法。
A Semi-Supervised Framework for Speech Confidence Detection using Whisper
Authors: Adam Wynn, Jingyun Wang
First: 2026-05-12T16:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:50:54+00:00
Comments: 12 pages, 9 Figures, Submitted to IEEE Transactions on Audio, Speech and Language Processing
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automatic detection of speaker confidence is critical for adaptive computing but remains constrained by limited labelled data and the subjectivity of paralinguistic annotations. This paper proposes a semi-supervised hybrid framework that fuses deep semantic embeddings from the Whisper encoder with an interpretable acoustic feature vector composed of eGeMAPS descriptors and auxiliary probability estimates of vocal stress and disfluency. To mitigate reliance on scarce ground truth data, we introduce an Uncertainty-Aware Pseudo-Labelling strategy where a model generates labels for unlabelled data, retaining only high-quality samples for training. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach achieves a Macro-F1 score of 0.751, outperforming self-supervised baselines, including WavLM, HuBERT, and Wav2Vec 2.0. The hybrid architecture also surpasses the unimodal Whisper baseline, yielding a 3\% improvement in the minority class, confirming that explicit prosodic and auxiliary features provide necessary corrective signals which are otherwise lost in deep semantic representations. Ablation studies further show that a curated set of high confidence pseudo-labels outperforms indiscriminate large scale augmentation, confirming that data quality outweighs quantity for perceived confidence detection.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于Whisper的半监督语音自信度检测框架
自动检测说话人自信度对自适应计算至关重要，但受限于标注数据稀缺及副语言标注的主观性。本文提出一种半监督混合框架，融合Whisper编码器的深度语义嵌入与可解释的声学特征向量（由eGeMAPS描述符及声带紧张度与不流畅度的辅助概率估计构成）。为减少对稀缺真实标签的依赖，引入不确定性感知伪标签策略，模型为未标注数据生成标签，仅保留高质量样本用于训练。实验表明，该方法达到0.751的Macro-F1分数，优于WavLM、HuBERT及Wav2Vec 2.0等自监督基线模型。混合架构亦超越单模态Whisper基线，少数类提升3%，证实显式韵律与辅助特征提供必要修正信号，而深度语义表示中常丢失此类信息。消融实验进一步表明，精选高置信度伪标签优于无差别大规模数据增强，验证数据质量优于数量对感知自信度检测的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for reliable speaker confidence detection in adaptive computing systems and the scarcity of labeled data, this study introduces a semi-supervised framework that combines Whisper’s semantic embeddings with interpretable acoustic features like eGeMAPS and auxiliary vocal stress/disfluency probabilities. It employs an uncertainty-aware pseudo-labeling strategy to selectively train on high-quality unlabeled samples, avoiding noise. Experiments show the hybrid model achieves a Macro-F1 of 0.751, outperforming self-supervised baselines such as WavLM and Wav2Vec 2.0, and improves minority class performance by 3% over the unimodal Whisper baseline, demonstrating that explicit prosodic cues enhance confidence detection beyond semantic representations.
为了解决标注数据稀缺和副语言标注主观性的问题，本文提出了一种基于Whisper的半监督语音自信度检测框架。该方法融合了Whisper编码器的深层语义嵌入、可解释的声学特征（eGeMAPS）以及语音压力和口吃概率的辅助估计。通过不确定性感知的伪标签策略，仅保留高质量的未标注样本用于训练，降低对稀缺真实标签的依赖。实验表明，该混合模型在Macro-F1上达到0.751，优于WavLM、HuBERT和Wav2Vec 2.0等自监督基线模型，相较单模态Whisper基线在少数类上提升3%，证明了声学和辅助特征对自信度检测的重要性，超越了深层语义表示的局限。
SmellBench: Evaluating LLM Agents on Architectural Code Smell Repair
Authors: Ion George Dinu, Marian Cristian Mihăescu, Traian Rebedea
First: 2026-05-07T22:33:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:50:41+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Architectural code smells erode software maintainability and are costly to repair manually, yet unlike localized bugs, they require cross-module reasoning about design intent that challenges both developers and automated tools. While large language model agents excel at bug fixing and code-level refactoring, their ability to repair architectural code smells remains unexplored. We present the first empirical evaluation of LLM agents on architectural code smell repair. We contribute SmellBench, a task orchestration framework that incorporates smell-type-specific optimized prompts and supports iterative multi-step execution, together with a scoring methodology that separately evaluates repair effectiveness, false positive identification, and net codebase impact. We evaluate 11 agent configurations from four model families (GPT, Claude, Gemini, Mistral) on 65 hard-severity architectural smells detected by PyExamine in the Python project scikit-learn, validated against expert judgments. Expert validation reveals that 63.1% of detected smells are false positives, while the best agent achieves a 47.7% resolution rate. Agents identify false positives with up to $κ= 0.94$ expert agreement, but repair aggressiveness and net codebase quality are inversely related: the most aggressive agent introduces 140 new smells. These findings expose a gap between current LLM capabilities in localized code transformations and the architectural understanding needed for cross-module refactoring. SmellBench provides reusable infrastructure for tracking progress on this underexplored dimension of automated software engineering. We release our code and data at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19247588.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SmellBench：评估大语言模型代理修复架构代码异味的能力
架构代码异味会损害软件可维护性，且手动修复成本高昂，但与局部错误不同，修复它们需要跨模块理解设计意图，这对开发者和自动化工具都构成挑战。尽管大语言模型代理在修复错误和代码级重构方面表现优异，但其修复架构代码异味的能力尚未被探索。我们首次对大语言模型代理在修复架构代码异味方面的表现进行了实证评估。我们提出了SmellBench，一个任务编排框架，包含针对不同异味类型优化的提示模板，并支持多步骤迭代执行，同时提供一套评分方法，分别评估修复有效性、假阳性识别和对代码库整体影响。我们在Python项目scikit-learn中通过PyExamine检测出的65个高严重性架构异味上，评估了来自四个模型家族（GPT、Claude、Gemini、Mistral）的11种代理配置，结果经专家验证。专家验证显示，63.1%的检测到的异味为假阳性，表现最佳的代理修复率达47.7%。代理在识别假阳性方面最高可达κ=0.94的专家一致性，但修复激进性与代码库整体质量呈负相关：最激进的代理引入了140个新异味。这些发现揭示了当前大语言模型在局部代码变换能力与跨模块重构所需的架构理解能力之间的差距。SmellBench为追踪这一尚未充分探索的自动化软件工程维度提供了可复用的基础设施。我们的代码和数据已发布于https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.19247588。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of repairing architectural code smells—complex, cross-module design issues that degrade software maintainability and are hard for both humans and tools to fix—this study evaluates LLM agents’ ability to perform such repairs. Using SmellBench, a novel framework with smell-type-specific prompts and iterative multi-step execution, the researchers assess 11 agent configurations across four model families on 65 hard-severity smells in scikit-learn. Results show that 63.1% of detected smells are false positives, and the best agent achieves a 47.7% resolution rate. While agents identify false positives with high expert agreement (κ=0.94), the most aggressive repair attempts introduce 140 new smells, revealing a trade-off between repair aggressiveness and codebase quality.
为解决影响软件可维护性的架构代码异味（跨模块设计缺陷）这一难题，本研究首次评估了大语言模型代理在修复此类问题上的能力。通过SmellBench框架（含针对性提示与迭代执行机制），研究人员对11种模型配置在scikit-learn项目中的65个严重架构异味进行了测试。结果表明，63.1%的检测结果为误报，最优代理修复率仅47.7%；虽误报识别准确率高（κ=0.94），但最激进的修复方案反而引入了140个新异味，揭示了修复力度与代码质量间的负相关关系。
SafeManip: A Property-Driven Benchmark for Temporal Safety Evaluation in Robotic Manipulation
Authors: Chengyue Huang, Khang Vo Huynh, Sebastian Elbaum, Zsolt Kira, Lu Feng
First: 2026-05-12T16:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:49:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Robotic manipulation is typically evaluated by task success, but successful completion does not guarantee safe execution. Many safety failures are temporal: a robot may touch a clean surface after contamination or release an object before it is fully inside an enclosure. We introduce SafeManip, a property-driven benchmark to explicitly evaluate temporal safety properties in robotic manipulation, moving beyond prior evaluations that largely focus on task completion or per-state constraint violations. SafeManip defines reusable safety templates over finite executions using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf). It maps observed rollouts to symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them with LTLf-based monitors. Its property suite covers eight manipulation safety categories: collision and contact safety, grasp stability, release stability, cross-contamination, action onset, mechanism recovery, object containment, and enclosure access. Templates can be instantiated with task-specific objects, fixtures, regions, or skills, allowing the same safety specifications to generalize across tasks and environments. We evaluate SafeManip on six vision-language-action policies, including $π_0$, $π_{0.5}$, GR00T, and their training variants, across 50 RoboCasa365 household tasks. Results show that even strong models often behave unsafely. Task-success gains do not reliably translate into safer execution: many successful rollouts remain unsafe, while longer-horizon or more complex tasks expose more violations. SafeManip provides a reusable evaluation layer for diagnosing temporal safety failures and measuring safe success beyond task completion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SafeManip：用于机器人操作中时序安全评估的属性驱动基准
机器人操作通常通过任务成功率进行评估，但任务成功并不保证执行安全。许多安全失效具有时序性：机器人可能在污染后接触洁净表面，或在物体未完全进入容器前释放物体。我们提出了SafeManip，一个属性驱动的基准，用于显式评估机器人操作中的时序安全属性，超越以往主要关注任务完成或每状态约束违反的评估方法。SafeManip使用有限轨迹上的线性时序逻辑（LTLf）定义可复用的安全模板，将观测到的执行轨迹映射为符号谓词轨迹，并通过LTLf监控器进行评估。其属性集涵盖八个操作安全类别：碰撞与接触安全、抓取稳定性、释放稳定性、交叉污染、动作启动、机构恢复、物体包含和容器访问。模板可实例化为任务特定的对象、固定装置、区域或技能，使相同的安全规范可泛化至不同任务和环境。我们在六个视觉-语言-动作策略（包括π₀、π₀.₅、GR00T及其训练变体）上，针对50个RoboCasa365家庭任务评估SafeManip。结果表明，即使强模型也常表现出不安全行为。任务成功率的提升并不可靠地转化为更安全的执行：许多成功轨迹仍不安全，而长时域或更复杂任务暴露更多违规行为。SafeManip提供了一种可复用的评估层，用于诊断时序安全失效并衡量超越任务完成的安全成功。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between task success and temporal safety in robotic manipulation, SafeManip introduces a property-driven benchmark using Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces (LTLf) to evaluate safety across time. It maps robot rollouts into symbolic predicate traces and evaluates them against reusable safety templates covering eight categories including collision, contamination, and containment. Evaluated on six vision-language-action policies across 50 household tasks, the benchmark reveals that even high-performing models frequently violate safety properties, and task success does not reliably correlate with safe execution—especially in longer or more complex tasks.
为弥补机器人操作中任务成功与时间安全之间的差距，SafeManip提出一种基于线性时态逻辑（LTLf）的属性驱动评估基准，通过将机器人执行轨迹映射为符号谓词轨迹并评估其是否满足八类安全属性（如碰撞、抓取稳定性、污染、包含等）。在6个视觉-语言-动作策略和50个家庭任务上评估发现，即使表现优异的模型也常违反安全属性，且任务越长或越复杂，安全违规越明显，表明仅以任务完成率衡量安全是不可靠的，亟需引入显式的时间安全评估机制。
Scalable Token-Level Hallucination Detection in Large Language Models
Authors: Rui Min, Tianyu Pang, Chao Du, Minhao Cheng, Yi R. Fung
First: 2026-05-12T16:47:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:47:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities, but they still frequently produce hallucinations. These hallucinations are difficult to detect in reasoning-intensive tasks, where the content appears coherent but contains errors like logical flaws and unreliable intermediate results. While step-level analysis is commonly used to detect internal hallucinations, it suffers from limited granularity and poor scalability due to its reliance on step segmentation. To address these limitations, we propose TokenHD, a holistic pipeline for training token-level hallucination detectors. Specifically, TokenHD consists of a scalable data engine for synthesizing large-scale hallucination annotations along with a training recipe featuring an importance-weighted strategy for robust model training. To systematically assess the detection performance, we also provide a rigorous evaluation protocol. Through training within TokenHD, our detector operates directly on free-form text to identify hallucinations, eliminating the need for predefined step segmentation or additional text reformatting. Our experiments show that even a small detector (0.6B) achieves substantial performance gains after training, surpassing much larger reasoning models (e.g., QwQ-32B), and detection performance scales consistently with model size from 0.6B to 8B. Finally, we show that our detector can generalize well across diverse practical scenarios and explore strategies to further enhance its cross-domain generalization capability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中可扩展的词级幻觉检测
大语言模型（LLMs）展现了卓越能力，但仍常产生幻觉。在推理密集型任务中，这些幻觉难以检测，因为内容看似连贯却包含逻辑错误和不可靠的中间结果。虽然步级分析常用于检测内部幻觉，但其依赖步段划分，导致粒度有限且可扩展性差。为解决此问题，我们提出TokenHD，一个用于训练词级幻觉检测器的全面流程。TokenHD包含一个可扩展的数据引擎，用于合成大规模幻觉标注，以及一个采用重要性加权策略的训练方案，以实现稳健模型训练。为系统评估检测性能，我们还提供严谨的评估协议。通过TokenHD训练，我们的检测器可直接在自由文本上识别幻觉，无需预定义步段划分或额外文本重格式化。实验表明，即使小型检测器（0.6B）经训练后性能显著提升，超越许多大型推理模型（如QwQ-32B），且检测性能随模型规模（0.6B至8B）一致增长。最后，我们证明检测器在多样实际场景中泛化良好，并探索增强其跨域泛化能力的策略。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting hallucinations in large language models—especially in reasoning tasks where errors are subtle and step-based methods are inefficient—the authors propose TokenHD, a token-level hallucination detection pipeline. TokenHD uses a scalable data engine to generate large-scale annotated hallucination data and trains detectors with an importance-weighted strategy to improve robustness. Experimental results show that even a small 0.6B detector outperforms larger reasoning models like QwQ-32B, and detection accuracy scales consistently with model size up to 8B. The detector operates directly on free-form text without requiring step segmentation, and demonstrates strong generalization across diverse practical scenarios.
为了解决大语言模型在推理任务中难以检测的幻觉问题，作者提出了TokenHD，一种基于词元级别的幻觉检测框架。该框架通过合成大规模标注数据并采用重要性加权训练策略，使检测器可直接在自由文本上工作，无需步骤分割。实验表明，仅0.6B的小型检测器性能已超越QwQ-32B等大型推理模型，且检测效果随模型规模从0.6B至8B持续提升，同时在多种实际场景中具备良好泛化能力。
Pretraining Exposure Explains Popularity Judgments in Large Language Models
Authors: Jamshid Mozafari, Bhawna Piryani, Adam Jatowt
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-05-12T16:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:45:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted at SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) exhibit systematic preferences for well-known entities, a phenomenon often attributed to popularity bias. However, the extent to which these preferences reflect real-world popularity versus statistical exposure during pretraining remains unclear, largely due to the inaccessibility of most training corpora. We provide the first direct, large-scale analysis of popularity bias grounded in fully observable pretraining data. Leveraging the open OLMo models and their complete pretraining corpus, Dolma, we compute precise entity-level exposure statistics across 7.4 trillion tokens. We analyze 2,000 entities spanning five types (Person, Location, Organization, Art, Product) and compare pretraining exposure against Wikipedia pageviews and two elicited LLM popularity signals: direct scalar estimation and pairwise comparison. Our results show that pretraining exposure strongly correlates with Wikipedia popularity, validating exposure as a meaningful proxy for real-world salience during the training period. More importantly, we find that LLM popularity judgments align more closely with exposure than with Wikipedia, especially when elicited via pairwise comparisons. This alignment is strongest for larger models and persists in the long tail, where Wikipedia popularity becomes unreliable. Overall, our findings demonstrate that popularity priors in LLMs are primarily shaped by pretraining statistics rather than external popularity signals, offering concrete evidence that data exposure plays a central role in driving popularity bias.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预训练曝光解释了大语言模型中的流行度判断
大语言模型（LLMs）表现出对知名实体的系统性偏好，这一现象常归因于流行度偏见。然而，这些偏好反映的是现实世界流行度，还是预训练期间的统计曝光程度，尚不明确，主要由于大多数训练语料库不可访问。我们首次基于完全可观察的预训练数据，对流行度偏见进行了大规模直接分析。利用开源OLMo模型及其完整预训练语料库Dolma，我们在7.4万亿个token上计算了实体级别的曝光统计。我们分析了涵盖五类（人物、地点、组织、艺术、产品）的2000个实体，并将预训练曝光与维基百科页面浏览量及两种由LLM引发的流行度信号（直接标量估计和成对比较）进行比较。结果表明，预训练曝光与维基百科流行度高度相关，验证了曝光作为训练期间现实世界显著性有意义代理指标的有效性。更重要的是，我们发现LLM的流行度判断更贴近曝光而非维基百科，尤其是在通过成对比较获取时。这种一致性在更大模型中表现最强，并在长尾部分持续存在，此时维基百科流行度变得不可靠。总体而言，我们的研究结果表明，LLM中的流行度先验主要由预训练统计塑造，而非外部流行度信号，为数据曝光在驱动流行度偏见中发挥核心作用提供了实证证据。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether large language models' preferences for popular entities stem from real-world popularity or from statistical exposure during pretraining. Using the open-source OLMo models and their full pretraining corpus Dolma (7.4 trillion tokens), the researchers analyzed 2,000 entities across five categories, comparing pretraining exposure with Wikipedia pageviews and two LLM-elicited popularity measures. Results show that pretraining exposure correlates strongly with Wikipedia popularity, confirming its role as a proxy for real-world salience during training. Crucially, LLMs’ own popularity judgments—especially via pairwise comparisons—align more closely with exposure than with Wikipedia, particularly for larger models and in the long tail where Wikipedia data becomes sparse, indicating that exposure during pretraining is the primary driver of popularity bias.
该研究探讨大型语言模型对知名实体的偏好是源于现实世界流行度还是预训练期间的统计曝光。研究者利用开源OLMo模型及其完整预训练语料库Dolma（7.4万亿词元），分析了2000个跨五类实体，对比预训练曝光量与维基百科页面浏览量及两种LLM自报告流行度指标。结果表明，预训练曝光与维基百科流行度高度相关，验证其作为训练期显著性代理的有效性。更重要的是，LLM自身的流行度判断（尤其通过成对比较）与曝光量比维基百科更一致，尤其在大模型和长尾区域（维基百科数据稀疏时）表现更明显，表明LLM流行度偏见主要由预训练数据曝光驱动，而非外部流行度信号。
Structured Diffusion Bridges: Inductive Bias for Denoising Diffusion Bridges
Authors: Eitan Kosman, Gabriele Serussi, Chaim Baskin
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-05-03T16:17:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:45:17+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modality translation is inherently under-constrained, as multiple cross-modal mappings may yield the same marginals. Recent work has shown that diffusion bridges are effective for this task. However, most existing approaches rely on fully paired datasets, thereby imposing a single data-driven constraint. We propose a diffusion-bridge framework that characterizes the space of admissible solutions and restricts it via alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as an optional heuristic rather than a prerequisite. We validate our method on synthetic and real modality translation benchmarks across unpaired, semi-paired, and paired regimes, showing consistent performance across supervision levels. Notably, \textbf{it achieves near fully-paired quality with a substantial relaxation in pairing requirements, and remaining applicable in the unpaired regime}. These results highlight diffusion bridges as a flexible foundation for modality translation beyond fully paired data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构化扩散桥：用于去噪扩散桥的归纳偏置
模态翻译本质上是欠约束的，因为多种跨模态映射可能产生相同的边缘分布。近期研究表明，扩散桥对此类任务有效。然而，大多数现有方法依赖于完全配对的数据集，从而施加单一的数据驱动约束。我们提出一种扩散桥框架，刻画可接受解的空间并通过齐次约束对其进行限制，将配对监督视为可选启发式方法而非先决条件。我们在合成和真实模态翻译基准上验证了该方法，涵盖无配对、半配对和完全配对场景，显示其在不同监督水平下表现一致。值得注意的是，它在大幅放宽配对要求的同时，仍能实现接近完全配对的质量，并且在无配对场景下依然适用。这些结果表明，扩散桥可作为超越完全配对数据的模态翻译灵活基础。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the under-constrained nature of modality translation and the limitations of existing diffusion bridge methods that require fully paired data, this work proposes a diffusion-bridge framework that explicitly characterizes and restricts the space of admissible solutions using alignment constraints, treating paired supervision as optional. Experiments across synthetic and real benchmarks under unpaired, semi-paired, and fully paired conditions demonstrate that the method achieves near-fully-paired quality while significantly relaxing pairing requirements and remains effective even without any paired data.
鉴于模态翻译固有的欠约束性及现有扩散桥方法对完全配对数据的依赖，本文提出一种扩散桥框架，通过引入对齐约束显式刻画并限制可行解空间，将配对监督视为可选启发式而非必需条件。在合成与真实基准上的实验表明，该方法在不同配对程度下（包括无配对）均能实现接近完全配对的数据质量，显著降低了配对数据需求，凸显了扩散桥作为非配对模态翻译基础架构的灵活性。
Trust the Batch, On- or Off-Policy: Adaptive Policy Optimization for RL Post-Training
Authors: Rasool Fakoor, Murdock Aubry, Nicholas Stranges, Alexander J. Smola
First: 2026-05-12T16:44:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:44:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Reinforcement learning is structurally harder than supervised learning because the policy changes the data distribution it learns from. The resulting fragility is especially visible in large-model training, where the training and rollout systems differ in numerical precision, sampling, and other implementation details. Existing methods manage this fragility by adding hyper-parameters to the training objective, which makes the algorithm more sensitive to its configuration and requires retuning whenever the task, model scale, or distribution mismatch changes. This fragility traces to two concerns that current objectives entangle through hyper-parameters set before training begins: a trust-region concern, that updates should not move the policy too far from its current value, and an off-policy concern, that data from older or different behavior policies should influence the update only to the extent that it remains reliable. Neither concern is a constant to set in advance, and their severity is reflected in the policy-ratio distribution of the current batch. We present a simple yet effective batch-adaptive objective that replaces fixed clipping with the normalized effective sample size of the policy ratios. The same statistic caps the score-function weight and sets the strength of an off-policy regularizer, so the update stays close to the usual on-policy score-function update when ratios are nearly uniform, and tightens automatically when stale or mismatched data cause ratio concentration, while retaining a nonzero learning signal on high-ratio tokens. Experiments across a wide range of settings show that our method matches or exceeds tuned baselines, introducing no new objective hyper-parameters and removing several existing ones. The code is available at https://github.com/FeynRL-project/FeynRL.
中文标题/摘要
标题：信任批次，无论策略是否在线：强化学习后训练的自适应策略优化
强化学习比监督学习结构上更难，因为策略会改变其学习的数据分布。这种脆弱性在大模型训练中尤为明显，其中训练和执行系统在数值精度、采样及其他实现细节上存在差异。现有方法通过在训练目标中添加超参数来应对这种脆弱性，但这使算法对配置更敏感，且每当任务、模型规模或分布不匹配变化时都需要重新调参。这种脆弱性源于当前目标在训练前通过超参数混合了两个问题：信任区域问题（更新不应使策略偏离当前值过远）和离策略问题（旧或不同行为策略的数据应仅在仍可靠时影响更新）。这两个问题都不是预先设定的常数，其严重程度反映在当前批次的策略比率分布中。我们提出了一种简单而有效的批次自适应目标，用策略比率的归一化有效样本量替代固定裁剪。同一统计量同时限制得分函数权重并设定离策略正则化强度，因此当比率接近均匀分布时，更新接近常规的在线得分函数更新；当过时或不匹配数据导致比率集中时，更新会自动收紧，同时保留高比率标记的非零学习信号。在多种设置下的实验表明，我们的方法可匹配或超越调优基线，无需新增目标超参数，且移除了若干现有超参数。代码见 https://github.com/FeynRL-project/FeynRL。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the fragility in reinforcement learning caused by policy-induced data distribution shifts, especially in large-model training where training and rollout systems differ in implementation details. The authors propose a batch-adaptive objective that replaces fixed clipping with the normalized effective sample size of policy ratios, dynamically adjusting both score-function weights and off-policy regularization strength based on the current batch’s ratio distribution. This approach ensures updates remain close to on-policy behavior when ratios are uniform, while tightening constraints when stale or mismatched data cause ratio concentration—without eliminating learning signals for high-ratio tokens. Experiments across diverse settings demonstrate that the method matches or surpasses tuned baselines without requiring new hyper-parameters, effectively removing several existing ones.
该论文针对强化学习中因策略变化导致的数据分布偏移所引发的脆弱性，特别是在大模型训练中训练与采样系统存在数值精度、采样方式等差异时。作者提出一种基于当前批次策略比率分布的自适应目标函数，用归一化有效样本量替代固定裁剪，同时调节策略梯度权重和离策略正则化强度：当比率分布均匀时保持近似策略梯度更新，当数据陈旧或不匹配导致比率集中时自动收紧约束，同时保留高比率令牌的学习信号。实验表明该方法在多种设置下表现不逊于调优基线，且无需新增超参数，有效消除了若干现有超参数。
About Time: Model-free Reinforcement Learning with Timed Reward Machines
Authors: Rajarshi Roy, Anirban Majumdar, Ritam Raha, David Parker, Marta Kwiatkowska
Venue: IJCAI 2026
First: 2025-12-19T14:39:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:44:19+00:00
Comments: Extended version of paper accepted at IJCAI 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reward specification plays a central role in reinforcement learning (RL), guiding the agent's behavior. To express non-Markovian rewards, formalisms such as reward machines have been introduced to capture dependencies on histories. However, traditional reward machines lack the ability to model precise timing constraints, limiting their use in time-sensitive applications. In this paper, we propose timed reward machines (TRMs), which are an extension of reward machines that incorporate timing constraints into the reward structure. TRMs enable more expressive specifications with tunable reward logic, for example, imposing costs for delays and granting rewards for timely actions. We study model-free RL frameworks (i.e., tabular Q-learning) for learning optimal policies with TRMs under digital and real-time semantics. Our algorithms integrate the TRM into learning via abstractions of timed automata, and employ counterfactual-imagining heuristics that exploit the structure of the TRM to improve the search. Experimentally, we demonstrate that our algorithm learns policies that achieve high rewards while satisfying the timing constraints specified by the TRM on popular RL benchmarks. Moreover, we conduct comparative studies of performance under different TRM semantics, along with ablations that highlight the benefits of counterfactual-imagining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于时间：基于定时奖励机的无模型强化学习
奖励规范在强化学习（RL）中起着核心作用，引导智能体的行为。为表达非马尔可夫奖励，已引入奖励机等形式化方法以捕捉对历史的依赖。然而，传统奖励机缺乏建模精确时间约束的能力，限制了其在时间敏感应用中的使用。本文提出定时奖励机（TRMs），它是奖励机的扩展，将时间约束纳入奖励结构。TRMs支持更具表达力的规范，且具有可调的奖励逻辑，例如对延迟施加成本、对及时行动给予奖励。我们研究了无模型RL框架（即表格Q学习）在数字和实时语义下，利用TRMs学习最优策略的方法。我们的算法通过时序自动机的抽象将TRM融入学习过程，并采用反事实想象启发式方法，利用TRM结构提升搜索效率。实验表明，我们的算法在主流RL基准上学习到的策略既能获得高奖励，又能满足TRM指定的时间约束。此外，我们还比较了不同TRM语义下的性能，并通过消融实验突出了反事实想象的优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model time-sensitive reward structures in reinforcement learning, this paper introduces timed reward machines (TRMs) that extend traditional reward machines by incorporating timing constraints into reward logic. The authors develop model-free RL algorithms, specifically tabular Q-learning, that integrate TRMs via timed automata abstractions and counterfactual-imagining heuristics to guide policy learning. Experiments on standard RL benchmarks show that the proposed method learns high-reward policies that strictly adhere to timing constraints, and comparative studies confirm that the counterfactual-imagining component significantly improves performance under both digital and real-time semantics.
为应对强化学习中对精确时间建模的需求，本文提出时序奖励机（TRM），在奖励逻辑中嵌入时间约束，以惩罚延迟并奖励及时行动。作者将模型无关的强化学习方法（如表格Q学习）与TRM结合，通过将TRM抽象为时序自动机，并利用反事实想象启发式方法引导策略搜索。实验表明，该方法在标准强化学习基准上能有效学习高奖励策略并严格满足时间约束，消融实验进一步验证了反事实想象机制在不同TRM语义下的有效性。
Discrete Flow Matching for Offline-to-Online Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Fairoz Nower Khan, Nabuat Zaman Nahim, Peizhong Ju
First: 2026-05-12T16:44:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:44:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many reinforcement learning (RL) tasks have discrete action spaces, but most generative policy methods based on diffusion and flow matching are designed for continuous control. Meanwhile, generative policies usually rely heavily on offline datasets and offline-to-online RL is itself challenging, as the policy must improve from new interaction without losing useful behavior learned from static data. To address those challenges, we introduce DRIFT, an online fine-tuning method that updates an offline pretrained continuous-time Markov chain (CTMC) policy with an advantage-weighted discrete flow matching loss. To preserve useful pretrained knowledge, we add a path-space penalty that regularizes the full CTMC trajectory distribution, rather than only the final action distribution. For large discrete action spaces, we introduce a candidate-set approximation that updates the actor over a small subset of actions sampled from reference-policy rollouts and uniform exploration. Our theoretical analysis shows that the candidate-set error is controlled by missing target probability mass, and the induced CTMC generator error decreases as the candidate set covers more high-probability actions. Experiments on prevailing discrete action RL task show that our method provides stable offline-to-online improvement across all tasks, achieving the highest average score on Jericho with a simple GRU encoder while outperforming methods that use pretrained language models. Controlled experiments further confirm that the path-space penalty remains bounded during fine-tuning and that the CTMC generator adapts to shifted rewards faster than deterministic baselines. The candidate-set mechanism is supported by a stability analysis showing that the generator error decreases exponentially with candidate coverage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：离散流匹配用于离线到在线强化学习
许多强化学习（RL）任务具有离散动作空间，但基于扩散和流匹配的生成策略方法大多设计用于连续控制。同时，生成策略通常高度依赖离线数据集，而离线到在线RL本身具有挑战性，因为策略必须在新交互中改进，而不丢失从静态数据中学到的有用行为。为应对这些挑战，我们引入DRIFT，一种在线微调方法，通过优势加权离散流匹配损失更新预训练的连续时间马尔可夫链（CTMC）策略。为保留有用的预训练知识，我们添加路径空间惩罚，正则化整个CTMC轨迹分布，而非仅最终动作分布。对于大型离散动作空间，我们引入候选集近似方法，通过从参考策略轨迹和均匀探索中采样的小动作子集更新演员。理论分析表明，候选集误差由缺失的目标概率质量控制，且诱导的CTMC生成器误差随候选集覆盖更多高概率动作而降低。在主流离散动作RL任务上的实验表明，我们的方法在所有任务中均提供稳定的离线到在线改进，在Jericho任务上使用简单GRU编码器时获得最高平均得分，且优于使用预训练语言模型的方法。受控实验进一步证实，路径空间惩罚在微调过程中保持有界，且CTMC生成器比确定性基线更快适应奖励偏移。候选集机制由稳定性分析支持，表明生成器误差随候选覆盖度呈指数下降。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between discrete-action RL tasks and continuous generative policy methods, DRIFT introduces an online fine-tuning approach that adapts a pretrained continuous-time Markov chain policy using a discrete flow matching loss weighted by advantages. To retain pretrained knowledge, it applies a path-space penalty that regularizes the full trajectory distribution. For large discrete action spaces, it employs a candidate-set approximation, sampling actions from rollouts and uniform exploration to reduce computational cost, with theoretical guarantees that error scales with coverage of high-probability actions. Experiments show DRIFT achieves stable offline-to-online improvement across tasks, scoring highest on Jericho with a simple GRU encoder, outperforming language-model-based baselines, and demonstrating faster reward adaptation and bounded path-space penalty during fine-tuning.
为解决离线预训练策略在离线到在线强化学习中适应离线数据与在线交互的挑战，本文提出DRIFT方法，通过优势加权的离散流匹配损失对连续时间马尔可夫链策略进行在线微调。为保留预训练知识，引入路径空间惩罚，对完整轨迹分布进行正则化而非仅最终动作。针对大规模离散动作空间，采用候选集近似，仅在从轨迹和均匀探索中采样的小动作子集上更新策略。理论分析表明，候选集误差由缺失的目标概率质量决定，且生成器误差随候选集覆盖高概率动作增多而降低。实验表明，该方法在多个离散动作任务中实现稳定离线到在线提升，在Jericho任务中以GRU编码器取得最高平均分，优于使用预训练语言模型的方法，并验证路径空间惩罚在微调中保持有界，CTMC生成器比确定性基线更快适应奖励偏移。
ProfiliTable: Profiling-Driven Tabular Data Processing via Agentic Workflows
Authors: Wei Liu, Yang Gu, Xi Yan, Zihan Nan, Beicheng Xu, Keyao Ding, Bin Cui, Wentao Zhang
First: 2026-05-12T16:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:42:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Table processing-including cleaning, transformation, augmentation, and matching-is a foundational yet error-prone stage in real-world data pipelines. While recent LLM-based approaches show promise for automating such tasks, they often struggle in practice due to ambiguous instructions, complex task structures, and the lack of structured feedback, resulting in syntactically correct but semantically flawed code. To address these challenges, we propose ProfiliTable, an autonomous multi-agent framework centered on dynamic profiling, which constructs and iteratively refines a unified execution context through interactive exploration, knowledge-augmented synthesis, and feedback-driven refinement. ProfiliTable integrates (i) a Profiler that performs ReAct-style data exploration to build semantic understanding, (ii) a Generator that retrieves curated operators to synthesize task-aware code, and (iii) an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that injects execution scores and diagnostic insights to enable closed-loop refinement. Extensive experiments on a diverse benchmark covering 18 tabular task types demonstrate that ProfiliTable consistently outperforms strong baselines, particularly in complex multi-step scenarios. These results highlight the critical role of dynamic profiling in reliably translating ambiguous user intents into robust and governance-compliant table transformations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProfiliTable：基于代理工作流的轮廓驱动表格数据处理
表格处理——包括清洗、转换、增强和匹配——是现实世界数据管道中的基础但易出错的阶段。尽管近期基于大语言模型的方法在自动化此类任务方面展现出潜力，但它们在实践中常因指令模糊、任务结构复杂及缺乏结构化反馈而表现不佳，导致生成的代码语法正确但语义错误。为应对这些挑战，我们提出ProfiliTable，一个以动态轮廓为中心的自主多代理框架，通过交互式探索、知识增强合成和反馈驱动优化，逐步构建并迭代精炼统一的执行上下文。ProfiliTable集成了（i）一个执行ReAct风格数据探索的轮廓器，用于构建语义理解；（ii）一个检索精选操作符并合成任务感知代码的生成器；（iii）一个评估-摘要循环，注入执行得分与诊断洞察，实现闭环优化。在涵盖18种表格任务类型的多样化基准测试中，大量实验表明ProfiliTable持续优于强基线方法，尤其在复杂多步骤场景中表现突出。这些结果凸显了动态轮廓在将模糊用户意图可靠转化为稳健且符合治理规范的表格转换中的关键作用。
Summary / 总结
ProfiliTable addresses the challenges of automating tabular data processing by introducing an autonomous multi-agent framework that dynamically profiles data to resolve ambiguity and improve code quality. It employs a Profiler to explore data semantically, a Generator to synthesize task-aware code using curated operators, and an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that refines outputs through execution feedback. Experiments across 18 tabular task types show ProfiliTable consistently outperforms existing baselines, especially in multi-step workflows, demonstrating the effectiveness of dynamic profiling in translating vague user intents into accurate, governance-compliant transformations.
为解决表数据处理中人工操作易错及现有大模型方法在复杂任务中表现不佳的问题，ProfiliTable 提出一种基于动态画像的多智能体框架。该框架包含数据语义探索的 Profiler、基于精选操作符合成任务代码的 Generator，以及通过执行评分与诊断反馈实现闭环优化的 Evaluator-Summarizer 循环。在涵盖18类表任务的基准测试中，ProfiliTable 在复杂多步骤场景中持续优于现有基线方法，验证了动态画像在将模糊用户意图转化为语义正确且合规的表转换中的关键作用。
Fast Image Super-Resolution via Consistency Rectified Flow
Authors: Jiaqi Xu, Wenbo Li, Haoze Sun, Fan Li, Zhixin Wang, Long Peng, Jingjing Ren, Haoran Yang, Xiaowei Hu, Renjing Pei, Pheng-Ann Heng
Venue: ICCV 2025
First: 2026-05-12T16:42:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:42:38+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ICCV 2025
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diffusion models (DMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in real-world image super-resolution (SR), yet their reliance on time-consuming multi-step sampling largely hinders their practical applications. While recent efforts have introduced few- or single-step solutions, existing methods either inefficiently model the process from noisy input or fail to fully exploit iterative generative priors, compromising the fidelity and quality of the reconstructed images. To address this issue, we propose FlowSR, a novel approach that reformulates the SR problem as a rectified flow from low-resolution (LR) to high-resolution (HR) images. Our method leverages an improved consistency learning strategy to enable high-quality SR in a single step. Specifically, we refine the original consistency distillation process by incorporating HR regularization, ensuring that the learned SR flow not only enforces self-consistency but also converges precisely to the ground-truth HR target. Furthermore, we introduce a fast-slow scheduling strategy, where adjacent timesteps for consistency learning are sampled from two distinct schedulers: a fast scheduler with fewer timesteps to improve efficiency, and a slow scheduler with more timesteps to capture fine-grained texture details. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlowSR achieves outstanding performance in both efficiency and image quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过一致性校正流实现快速图像超分辨率
扩散模型（DMs）在现实世界图像超分辨率（SR）中表现出色，但其依赖耗时的多步采样严重限制了实际应用。尽管近期研究提出了少步或单步方案，现有方法要么低效地从噪声输入建模过程，要么未能充分挖掘迭代生成先验，从而损害重建图像的保真度和质量。为解决此问题，我们提出FlowSR，将SR问题重新表述为从低分辨率（LR）到高分辨率（HR）图像的校正流。该方法采用改进的一致性学习策略，实现单步高质量SR。具体而言，我们通过引入HR正则化优化原始一致性蒸馏过程，确保所学SR流不仅强制自一致性，还能精确收敛至真实HR目标。此外，我们引入快慢调度策略，一致性学习中相邻时间步从两个不同调度器采样：快调度器步数少以提升效率，慢调度器步数多以捕捉精细纹理细节。大量实验表明，FlowSR在效率和图像质量上均表现卓越。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of multi-step diffusion models in image super-resolution, this paper proposes FlowSR, a single-step method that reformulates SR as a rectified flow from low-resolution to high-resolution images. It enhances consistency learning by integrating HR regularization to ensure self-consistency and precise convergence to ground-truth targets. A fast-slow scheduling strategy samples timesteps from two distinct schedulers—one for speed and one for texture fidelity—to balance efficiency and quality. Experiments show FlowSR outperforms existing single-step methods in both speed and visual fidelity on standard benchmarks.
为解决扩散模型在图像超分辨率中因多步采样导致效率低下的问题，本文提出FlowSR方法，将超分辨率建模为从低分辨率到高分辨率的修正流。该方法通过引入高分辨率正则化增强一致性学习，确保生成流既自洽又精确收敛至真实高分辨率目标。采用快慢调度策略，分别用步数少的快调度提升效率、步数多的慢调度捕捉细节纹理。实验表明，FlowSR在速度与图像质量上均优于现有单步方法。
Agent-Based Post-Hoc Correction of Agricultural Yield Forecasts
Authors: Matthew Beddows, Aiden Durrant, Georgios Leontidis
First: 2026-05-12T16:41:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:41:54+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 6 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate crop yield forecasting in commercial soft fruit production is constrained by the data available in typical commercial farm records, which lack the sensor networks, satellite imagery, and high-resolution meteorological inputs that most state-of-the-art approaches assume. We propose a structured LLM agent framework that performs post-hoc correction of existing model predictions, encoding agricultural domain knowledge across tools for phase detection, bias learning, and range validation. Evaluated on a proprietary strawberry yield dataset and a public USDA corn harvest dataset, agent refinement of XGBoost reduced MAE by 20% and MASE by 56% on strawberry, with consistent improvements across Moirai2 (MAE 24%, MASE 22%) and Random Forest (MAE 28%, MASE 66%) baselines. Using Llama 3.1 8B as the agent produced the strongest corrections across all configurations; LLaVA 13B showed inconsistent gains, highlighting sensitivity to the choice of refinement model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于代理的农业产量预测事后校正
在商业软果生产中，准确的作物产量预测受限于典型商业农场记录中缺乏传感器网络、卫星图像和高分辨率气象输入数据，而这些正是大多数前沿方法所依赖的。我们提出了一种结构化的大型语言模型代理框架，用于事后校正现有模型预测，将农业领域知识编码到用于阶段检测、偏差学习和范围验证的工具中。在专有草莓产量数据集和公开的美国农业部玉米收获数据集上评估，代理优化后的XGBoost模型在草莓数据上将MAE降低20%，MASE降低56%，在Moirai2（MAE降低24%，MASE降低22%）和随机森林（MAE降低28%，MASE降低66%）基线模型上均保持一致改进。使用Llama 3.1 8B作为代理，在所有配置中均产生最强的校正效果；LLaVA 13B则表现不一致，凸显了优化模型选择的重要性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing yield forecasting models due to insufficient farm data, this work introduces an agent-based framework that post-hoc refines predictions using agricultural domain knowledge. The method employs a structured large language model agent to perform phase detection, bias learning, and range validation on top of base models like XGBoost, Random Forest, and Moirai2. Experimental results on proprietary strawberry and public USDA corn datasets show consistent improvements: XGBoost’s MAE and MASE dropped by 20% and 56% respectively, with similar gains across other baselines. Llama 3.1 8B consistently outperformed other models, while LLaVA 13B showed inconsistent results, indicating model choice significantly affects correction efficacy.
鉴于商业农场数据缺乏传感器、卫星影像和高分辨率气象数据，导致现有模型难以准确预测作物产量，本研究提出一种基于大语言模型的代理框架，通过阶段检测、偏差学习和范围验证等农业领域知识对现有模型预测进行事后修正。在自有草莓数据集和公开USDA玉米数据集上评估，该代理显著提升预测精度：XGBoost在草莓数据上MAE降低20%，MASE降低56%，在Moirai2和随机森林基线模型上也取得类似提升。Llama 3.1 8B在所有配置中表现最优，而LLaVA 13B效果不稳定，凸显模型选择的重要性。
Fill the GAP: A Granular Alignment Paradigm for Visual Reasoning in Multimodal Large Language Models
Authors: Yanting Miao, Yutao Sun, Dexin Wang, Mengyu Zhou, Pascal Poupart, Lei Lv, Qi Zhao, Li Wang, Hao Li, Xiaoxi Jiang, Guanjun Jiang
First: 2026-05-12T16:41:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:41:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Visual latent reasoning lets a multimodal large language model (MLLM) create intermediate visual evidence as continuous tokens, avoiding external tools or image generators. However, existing methods usually follow an output-as-input latent paradigm and yield unstable gains. We identify evidence for a feature-space mismatch that can contribute to this instability: dominant visual-latent models build on pre-norm MLLMs and reuse decoder hidden states as predicted latent inputs, even though these states occupy a substantially different norm regime from the input embeddings the model was trained to consume~\citep{xie2025mhc,li2026siamesenorm,team2026attention}. This mismatch can make direct latent feedback unreliable. Motivated by this diagnosis, we propose \textbf{GAP}, a \textbf{G}ranular \textbf{A}lignment \textbf{P}aradigm for visual latent modeling. GAP aligns visual latent reasoning at three levels: feature-level alignment maps decoder outputs into input-compatible visual latents through a lightweight PCA-aligned latent head; context-level alignment grounds latent targets with inspectable auxiliary visual supervision; and capacity-guided alignment assigns latent supervision selectively to examples where the base MLLM struggles. On Qwen2.5-VL 7B, the resulting model achieves the best mean aggregate perception and reasoning performance among our supervised variants. Inference-time intervention probing further suggests that generated latents provide task-relevant visual signal beyond merely adding token slots.
中文标题/摘要
标题：填补差距：面向多模态大语言模型视觉推理的细粒度对齐范式
视觉潜在推理使多模态大语言模型（MLLM）能够生成连续的中间视觉证据，无需外部工具或图像生成器。然而，现有方法通常遵循输出作为输入的潜在范式，导致收益不稳定。我们发现，这种不稳定性可能源于特征空间不匹配：主流视觉潜在模型基于预归一化MLLM，并重用解码器隐藏状态作为预测的潜在输入，尽管这些状态与模型训练时所消费的输入嵌入处于截然不同的归一化范围。这种不匹配会使直接潜在反馈不可靠。受此诊断启发，我们提出GAP（视觉潜在建模的细粒度对齐范式），在三个层面实现视觉潜在推理对齐：特征层面通过轻量级PCA对齐潜在头，将解码器输出映射为输入兼容的视觉潜在表示；上下文层面通过可检查的辅助视觉监督锚定潜在目标；容量引导对齐则仅对基础MLLM表现不佳的样本分配潜在监督。在Qwen2.5-VL 7B上，该模型在我们的监督变体中实现了最佳的平均感知与推理性能。推理时干预探测进一步表明，生成的潜在表示提供了超越单纯增加令牌槽位的、与任务相关的视觉信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by instability in visual latent reasoning due to a feature-space mismatch between decoder hidden states and input embeddings, the paper proposes GAP, a granular alignment paradigm for multimodal large language models. GAP aligns visual latents at three levels: feature-level via a lightweight PCA-aligned head, context-level using auxiliary visual supervision, and capacity-guided alignment that targets examples where the base model struggles. Experiments on Qwen2.5-VL 7B show GAP achieves the best mean aggregate perception and reasoning performance among supervised variants, with inference probing confirming generated latents carry task-relevant visual signals beyond token-slot addition.
为了解决视觉潜层推理因解码器隐藏状态与输入嵌入空间不匹配导致的不稳定问题，本文提出GAP（粒度对齐范式），从三个层面进行对齐：特征层通过轻量PCA对齐潜层头将解码器输出映射为输入兼容的视觉潜层；上下文层利用可检查的辅助视觉监督锚定潜层目标；容量引导层仅对基础模型表现不佳的样本施加潜层监督。在Qwen2.5-VL 7B上，GAP在监督变体中取得最佳的平均感知与推理性能，推理时干预探测进一步表明生成潜层携带了超越单纯增加token槽的、与任务相关的视觉信号。
Context Convergence Improves Answering Inferential Questions
Authors: Jamshid Mozafari, Bhawna Piryani, Adam Jatowt
Venue: SIGIR 2026
First: 2026-05-12T16:39:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:39:05+00:00
Comments: Accepted at SIGIR 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
While Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely used in open-domain Question Answering (QA), their ability to handle inferential questions-where answers must be derived rather than directly retrieved-remains still underexplored. This study investigates how the structure and quality of passages influence LLM performance on such questions. We focus on convergence, a measure of how effectively sentences (hints) eliminate incorrect answers, as a criterion for constructing passages. Using subsets of the TriviaHG dataset, we form passages by combining sentences with varying convergence levels and evaluate six LLMs of different sizes and architectures. Our results show that passages built from higher convergence sentences lead to substantially better answer accuracy than those selected by cosine similarity, indicating that convergence captures meaningful relevance for inferential reasoning. Additionally, ordering sentences by descending convergence slightly improves performance, suggesting that LLMs tend to prioritize earlier, information-rich cues. These findings highlight convergence as a practical signal for guiding passage construction and analyzing inferential reasoning behavior in LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：上下文收敛性提升推理型问题回答能力
尽管大语言模型（LLMs）在开放域问答中广泛应用，但其处理推理型问题（需推导而非直接检索答案）的能力仍待深入探索。本研究探讨段落结构与质量如何影响LLM在推理型问题上的表现，以句子消除错误答案的有效性（收敛性）作为构建段落的标准。我们使用TriviaHG数据集的子集，通过组合不同收敛水平的句子构建段落，并评估六种不同规模与架构的LLM。结果表明，高收敛性句子构建的段落显著优于基于余弦相似度选择的段落，说明收敛性对推理型推理具有实际相关性。此外，按收敛性降序排列句子可略微提升性能，表明LLM倾向于优先处理早期信息丰富的线索。这些发现凸显收敛性作为指导段落构建与分析LLM推理行为的实用信号。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the challenge of improving Large Language Models' performance on inferential questions, where answers require reasoning rather than direct retrieval. It introduces 'convergence'—a metric measuring how effectively sentences eliminate incorrect options—as a criterion for constructing informative passages. Using subsets of the TriviaHG dataset, the researchers built passages by combining sentences with varying convergence levels and evaluated six different LLMs. Results show that passages composed of high-convergence sentences significantly outperform those selected by cosine similarity, and ordering sentences by descending convergence further boosts accuracy, indicating LLMs benefit from prioritizing early, high-information cues.
该研究针对大语言模型在需要推理而非直接检索答案的题目上表现不佳的问题，提出以“收敛性”——衡量句子消除错误选项能力的指标——作为构建问答段落的标准。通过从TriviaHG数据集中选取不同收敛性级别的句子组合成段落，并对六种不同规模与架构的模型进行评估，结果表明高收敛性句子组成的段落显著优于基于余弦相似度选择的段落，证明收敛性在引导推理能力方面具有实际价值。此外，按收敛性降序排列句子可略微提升性能，说明模型倾向于优先处理早期信息丰富的线索。
GuidedVLA: Specifying Task-Relevant Factors via Plug-and-Play Action Attention Specialization
Authors: Xiaosong Jia, Bowen Yang, Zuhao Ge, Xian Nie, Yuchen Zhou, Cunxin Fan, Yufeng Li, Yilin Chai, Chao Jing, Zijian Liang, Qingwen Bu, Haidong Cao, Chao Wu, Qifeng Li, Zhenjie Yang, Chenhe Zhang, Hongyang Li, Zuxuan Wu, Junchi Yan, Yu-Gang Jiang
Venue: RSS 2026
First: 2026-05-12T16:38:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:38:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to RSS 2026. Project page: https://guidedvla.github.io/project_page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models aim for general robot learning by aligning action as a modality within powerful Vision-Language Models (VLMs). Existing VLAs rely on end-to-end supervision to implicitly enable the action decoding process to learn task-relevant features. However, without explicit guidance, these models often overfit to spurious correlations, such as visual shortcuts or environmental noise, limiting their generalization. In this paper, we introduce GuidedVLA, a framework designed to manually guide the action generation to focus on task-relevant factors. Our core insight is to treat the action decoder not as a monolithic learner, but as an assembly of functional components. Individual attention heads are supervised by manually defined auxiliary signals to capture distinct factors. As an initial study, we instantiate this paradigm with three specialized heads: object grounding, spatial geometry, and temporal skill logic. Across simulation and real-robot experiments, GuidedVLA improves success rates in both in-domain and out-of-domain settings compared to strong VLA baselines. Finally, we show that the quality of these specialized factors correlates positively with task performance and that our mechanism yields decoupled, high-quality features. Our results suggest that explicitly guiding action-decoder learning is a promising direction for building more robust and general VLA models.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GuidedVLA：通过即插即用的动作注意力专业化指定任务相关因素
视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型旨在通过将动作作为模态整合到强大的视觉-语言模型（VLM）中，实现通用机器人学习。现有VLA模型依赖端到端监督，隐式地使动作解码过程学习任务相关特征。然而，缺乏明确引导时，这些模型常过度拟合于虚假相关性，如视觉捷径或环境噪声，限制其泛化能力。本文提出GuidedVLA框架，旨在手动引导动作生成聚焦于任务相关因素。我们的核心思想是将动作解码器视为功能组件的集合，而非单一学习器。各注意力头由人工定义的辅助信号监督，以捕捉不同因素。作为初步研究，我们以三个专用头——物体定位、空间几何和时序技能逻辑——实例化该范式。在仿真与真实机器人实验中，GuidedVLA在域内与域外设置下均优于强VLA基线。最后，我们证明这些专用因素的质量与任务表现正相关，且我们的机制生成解耦、高质量特征。结果表明，明确引导动作解码器学习是构建更鲁棒、通用VLA模型的有前景方向。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of existing Vision-Language-Action models to overfit to spurious visual cues without explicit guidance, this paper introduces GuidedVLA, a framework that manually directs the action decoder to focus on task-relevant factors by decomposing it into specialized attention heads. Each head is supervised by manually defined auxiliary signals to capture distinct components like object grounding, spatial geometry, and temporal skill logic. Experimental results across simulation and real-robot settings show that GuidedVLA achieves higher success rates than strong baselines in both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks, and demonstrate that the quality of the specialized features directly correlates with performance, supporting the effectiveness of explicit guidance in improving generalization and robustness.
为了解决现有视觉-语言-动作模型因端到端监督而易过拟合于视觉捷径的问题，本文提出GuidedVLA框架，通过将动作解码器分解为多个专用注意力头，并由人工定义的辅助信号引导各头学习特定任务相关因素（如物体定位、空间几何、时序技能逻辑）。实验表明，该框架在仿真与真实机器人任务中均优于现有基线模型，在域内与域外场景下成功率更高，且专用因素质量与任务表现正相关，输出解耦且高质量的特征。
MetaColloc: Optimization-Free PDE Solving via Meta-Learned Basis Functions
Authors: Zichuan Yang
First: 2026-05-12T16:36:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:36:12+00:00
Comments: 21 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Solving partial differential equations (PDEs) with machine learning typically requires training a new neural network for every new equation. This optimization is slow. We introduce MetaColloc. It is an optimization-free and data-free framework that removes this bottleneck completely. We decouple basis discovery from the solving process. We meta-train a dual-branch neural network on diverse Gaussian Random Fields. This offline process creates a universal dictionary of neural basis functions. At test time, we freeze the network. We solve the PDE by assembling a collocation matrix. We find the solution through a single linear least squares step. For non-linear PDEs, we apply the Newton-Raphson method to achieve fast quadratic convergence. Our experiments across six 2D and 3D PDEs show massive improvements. MetaColloc reaches state-of-the-art accuracy on smooth and non-linear problems. It also reduces test-time computation by several orders of magnitude. Finally, we provide a detailed frequency sweep analysis. This analysis reveals a critical mismatch between function approximation and operator stability at extremely high frequencies. This profound finding opens a clear path toward future operator-aware meta-learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MetaColloc：通过元学习基函数实现无需优化的偏微分方程求解
使用机器学习求解偏微分方程（PDE）通常需要为每个新方程训练一个新的神经网络，这一过程效率低下。我们提出MetaColloc，这是一种无需优化且无数据依赖的框架，彻底消除了这一瓶颈。我们将基函数发现与求解过程解耦，通过在多样化的高斯随机场上元训练一个双分支神经网络，离线构建一个通用的神经基函数字典。测试时冻结网络，通过构建插值矩阵并执行单步线性最小二乘求解PDE。对于非线性PDE，采用牛顿-拉夫森法实现快速二次收敛。在六个2D和3D PDE上的实验表明，性能大幅提升。MetaColloc在光滑和非线性问题上达到最先进精度，同时将测试时计算量降低几个数量级。最后，我们提供了详细的频率扫描分析，揭示在极高频率下函数逼近与算子稳定性之间存在关键不匹配，这一深刻发现为未来面向算子的元学习指明了清晰路径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational inefficiency of training new neural networks for each PDE, MetaColloc introduces an optimization-free, data-free framework that decouples basis discovery from solving. It meta-trains a dual-branch neural network on diverse Gaussian Random Fields to generate a universal dictionary of basis functions, which is then frozen at test time. PDEs are solved via collocation matrix assembly and a single linear least squares step, with Newton-Raphson applied for nonlinear cases to achieve quadratic convergence. Experiments across six 2D and 3D PDEs demonstrate state-of-the-art accuracy and orders-of-magnitude reduction in test-time computation, while a frequency sweep analysis reveals a critical mismatch between function approximation and operator stability at high frequencies, guiding future operator-aware meta-learning.
为解决每求解一个偏微分方程（PDE）都需要训练新神经网络导致的计算效率低下问题，MetaColloc提出一种无需优化、无需数据的框架，将基函数发现与求解过程解耦。该框架通过在多样化的高斯随机场数据上元训练双分支神经网络，构建一个通用的神经基函数字典，测试时冻结网络，通过构建插值矩阵并执行单步线性最小二乘求解，非线性问题则采用牛顿-拉夫森法实现二次收敛。在六个二维和三维PDE实验中，该方法达到当前最优精度，测试时间计算量降低多个数量级；频率扫描分析揭示了高频下函数逼近与算子稳定性之间的关键不匹配，为未来面向算子的元学习指明了方向。
To Err Is Human; To Annotate, SILICON? Toward Robust Reproducibility in LLM Annotation
Authors: Xiang Cheng, Raveesh Mayya, João Sedoc
First: 2024-12-19T02:21:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:35:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unstructured text data annotation is foundational to management research. LLMs offer a cost-effective and scalable alternative to human annotation, but they introduce a novel challenge: the annotator itself can be retired. Proprietary models undergo regular deprecation cycles, threatening long-term reproducibility. Hence, the ability to reproduce annotation results when the original model becomes unavailable, i.e., robust reproducibility, is a central methodological challenge for LLM-based annotation. Achieving robust reproducibility requires first controlling measurement error. We develop an analytical framework that decomposes measurement error into four sources: guideline-induced error from inconsistent annotation criteria, baseline-induced error from unreliable human references, prompt-induced error from suboptimal meta-instruction, and model-induced error from architectural differences across LLMs. We develop the SILICON workflow that instantiates the analytical framework, prescribing targeted interventions at each error source. Empirical validation across nine management research tasks confirms that these interventions reduce measurement error, and simulations show that the resulting error reduction yields more accurate downstream statistical estimates. With measurement error controlled, we address two further aspects of robust reproducibility. First, we propose a regression-based methodology to establish backup open-weight models, which are permanently accessible. Every tested task has at least one open-weight model with no statistically detectable performance difference. Second, we quantify the upper bound of annotation quality attainable from the current set of available models by proposing a routing procedure that selectively sends low-confidence items to auxiliary models, revealing when model aggregation improves performance and when that may adversely affect labeling quality.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人非圣贤，孰能无过；标注亦然，硅基？迈向大语言模型标注的稳健可复现性
非结构化文本数据标注是管理研究的基础。大语言模型（LLM）提供了比人工标注更经济、可扩展的替代方案，但引入了新挑战：标注器本身可能被弃用。专有模型定期过时，威胁长期可复现性。因此，当原始模型不可用时仍能复现标注结果——即稳健可复现性——是LLM标注方法论的核心挑战。实现稳健可复现性需首先控制测量误差。我们构建分析框架，将测量误差分解为四类来源：因标注准则不一致导致的指南诱导误差、因人工参考不可靠导致的基线诱导误差、因元指令不理想导致的提示诱导误差、以及因不同LLM架构差异导致的模型诱导误差。我们开发SILICON工作流，将分析框架具体化，针对每类误差源实施定向干预。在九项管理研究任务中的实证验证表明，这些干预措施可降低测量误差，模拟结果亦显示误差降低可提升下游统计估计的准确性。在控制测量误差后，我们进一步解决稳健可复现性的两个方面：首先，提出基于回归的方法建立备用开源权重模型，这些模型永久可访问，每项测试任务至少存在一个与原模型无统计显著性能差异的开源模型；其次，通过提出路由程序，量化当前可用模型所能达到的标注质量上限，该程序选择性地将低置信度样本发送至辅助模型，揭示模型聚合何时提升性能、何时可能损害标注质量。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk of model deprecation undermining reproducibility in LLM-based text annotation, this paper introduces SILICON, a framework designed to ensure robust reproducibility by controlling measurement error through four error sources: guideline, baseline, prompt, and model-induced errors. The method decomposes error origins and applies targeted interventions, validated across nine management research tasks, which significantly reduce measurement error and improve downstream statistical accuracy. Additionally, the paper proposes regression-based backup models that match open-weight alternatives in performance and a routing strategy to optimize annotation quality by selectively delegating low-confidence items, revealing when model aggregation enhances or degrades labeling.
为解决大语言模型退役导致的标注结果不可复现问题，该研究提出SILICON工作流，通过分解测量误差为四类来源（指南、基线、提示、模型差异）并针对性干预，实现在九项管理研究任务中的误差降低和下游统计估计精度提升。进一步，研究提出基于回归的开源模型备份方法，确保每项任务至少存在性能无显著差异的替代模型，并设计路由策略，通过将低置信度样本分发给辅助模型，优化标注质量并揭示模型聚合的适用边界。
Classifier Context Rot: Monitor Performance Degrades with Context Length
Authors: Sam Martin, Fabien Roger
First: 2026-05-12T16:34:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:34:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Monitoring coding agents for dangerous behavior using language models requires classifying transcripts that often exceed 500K tokens, but prior agent monitoring benchmarks rarely contain transcripts longer than 100K tokens. We show that when used as classifiers, current frontier models fail to notice dangerous actions more often in longer transcripts. In particular, on a dataset that requires identifying when a coding agent takes a subtly dangerous action, Opus 4.6, GPT 5.4, and Gemini 3.1 miss these actions $2\times$ to $30\times$ more often when they occur after 800K tokens of benign activity than when they occur on their own. We also show that these weaknesses can be partially mitigated with prompting techniques such as periodic reminders throughout the transcript and may be mitigated further with better post-training. Monitor evaluations that do not consider long-context degradation are likely overestimating monitor performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分类器上下文旋转：监控性能随上下文长度增加而下降
使用语言模型监控编码代理的危险行为时，需对常超过50万tokens的转录文本进行分类，但此前的代理监控基准测试很少包含超过10万tokens的转录文本。我们发现，当前前沿模型在作为分类器时，对长转录文本中危险行为的识别失败率更高。特别是在一个需要识别编码代理在执行微妙危险行为的测试数据集上，当危险行为发生在80万tokens良性活动之后时，Opus 4.6、GPT 5.4和Gemini 3.1的漏检率比其单独出现时高出2至30倍。我们还表明，通过转录过程中的周期性提示等技术可部分缓解这些弱点，而更好的后训练可能进一步缓解。不考虑长上下文退化情况的监控评估很可能高估了监控性能。
Summary / 总结
The paper addresses the challenge that current language models fail to detect dangerous actions in coding agents when those actions occur within long transcripts, which often exceed 500K tokens—far beyond the lengths used in existing benchmarks. The authors evaluate frontier models like Opus 4.6, GPT 5.4, and Gemini 3.1 on a dataset requiring identification of subtle dangerous actions, finding that these models miss such actions 2 to 30 times more frequently when they follow 800K tokens of benign behavior compared to when they occur independently. The study further demonstrates that prompting techniques such as periodic reminders can partially alleviate this degradation, suggesting that performance metrics ignoring long-context behavior may be misleading.
该论文指出，当前语言模型在处理超过500K token的长文本时，难以有效识别编码代理中的危险行为，而现有评估基准多使用不超过100K token的文本。研究发现，如Opus 4.6、GPT 5.4和Gemini 3.1等前沿模型，在800K良性行为后出现的危险动作上漏检率比独立出现时高出2至30倍。实验表明，通过在文本中插入周期性提醒等提示策略可部分缓解该问题，暗示当前监控评估因忽略长上下文影响而高估了模型实际表现。
QAP-Router: Tackling Qubit Routing as Dynamic Quadratic Assignment with Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Kien X. Nguyen, Ankit Kulshrestha, Ilya Safro, Xiaoyuan Liu
First: 2026-05-12T16:34:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:34:01+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Qubit routing is a fundamental problem in quantum compilation, known to be NP-hard. Its dynamic nature makes local routing decisions propagate and compound over time, making global efficient solutions challenging. Existing heuristic methods rely on local rules with limited lookahead, while recent learning-based approaches often treat routing as a generic sequential decision problem without fully exploiting its underlying structure. In this paper, we introduce QAP-Router, framing qubit routing based on a dynamic Quadratic Assignment Problem (QAP) formulation. By modeling logical interactions, or quantum gates, as flow matrices and hardware topology as a distance matrix, our approach captures the interaction-distance coupling in a unified objective, which defines the reward in the reinforcement learning environment. To further exploit this structure, the policy network employs a solution-aware Transformer backbone that encodes the interaction between the flow matrix and the distance matrix into the attention mechanism. We also integrate a lookahead mechanism that blends naturally into the QAP framework, preventing myopic decisions. Extensive experiments on 1,831 real-world quantum circuits from the MQTBench, AgentQ and QUEKO datasets show that our method substantially reduces the CNOT gate count of routed circuits by 15.7%, 30.4% and 12.1%, respectively, relative to existing industry compilers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QAP-Router：将量子比特路由视为动态二次分配问题并结合强化学习
量子比特路由是量子编译中的基础问题，已知为NP难问题。其动态特性使得局部路由决策随时间传播并累积，导致全局高效解难以获得。现有启发式方法依赖局部规则且预判能力有限，而近期基于学习的方法常将路由视为通用序列决策问题，未能充分挖掘其内在结构。本文提出QAP-Router，将量子比特路由建模为动态二次分配问题（QAP）。通过将逻辑交互（量子门）建模为流矩阵，硬件拓扑建模为距离矩阵，我们的方法在统一目标中捕捉交互-距离耦合关系，定义强化学习环境中的奖励。为进一步利用该结构，策略网络采用感知解的Transformer骨干网络，将流矩阵与距离矩阵的交互编码至注意力机制中。我们还集成了一种预判机制，自然融入QAP框架，避免短视决策。在MQTBench、AgentQ和QUEKO数据集的1,831个真实量子电路上的大量实验表明，相比现有工业编译器，我们的方法分别将路由电路中的CNOT门数量减少了15.7%、30.4%和12.1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the NP-hard nature and dynamic propagation of qubit routing decisions in quantum compilation, this paper proposes QAP-Router, a reinforcement learning framework that models routing as a dynamic Quadratic Assignment Problem. It encodes quantum gate interactions as flow matrices and hardware topology as distance matrices, unifying their coupling into a reward function. The policy network uses a Transformer architecture designed to capture interactions between these matrices via attention, enhanced by a lookahead mechanism to avoid short-sighted routing. Experiments on 1,831 real-world circuits show QAP-Router reduces CNOT gate counts by 15.7%, 30.4%, and 12.1% compared to industry compilers across three datasets.
为应对量子编译中qubit路由的NP难问题及其动态传播特性，本文提出QAP-Router框架，将路由建模为动态二次分配问题。通过将量子门交互表示为流矩阵、硬件拓扑表示为距离矩阵，统一其交互与距离耦合关系作为强化学习奖励函数。策略网络采用感知解的Transformer结构，通过注意力机制编码矩阵间交互，并引入前瞻机制避免短视决策。在1831个真实量子电路实验中，该方法相较工业编译器分别减少CNOT门数15.7%、30.4%和12.1%。
Does Head Pose Correction Improve Biometric Facial Recognition?
Authors: Justin Norman, Hany Farid
First: 2025-12-02T19:53:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:33:55+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Biometric facial recognition models often demonstrate significant decreases in accuracy when processing real-world images, often characterized by poor quality, non-frontal subject poses, and subject occlusions. We investigate whether targeted, AI-driven, head-pose correction and image restoration can improve recognition accuracy. Using a model-agnostic, large-scale, forensic-evaluation pipeline, we assess the impact of three restoration approaches: 3D reconstruction (NextFace), 2D frontalization (CFR-GAN), and feature enhancement (CodeFormer). We find that naive application of these techniques substantially degrades facial recognition accuracy. However, we also find that selective application of CFR-GAN combined with CodeFormer yields meaningful improvements.
中文标题/摘要
标题：头部姿态校正能否提升生物特征面部识别性能？
生物特征面部识别模型在处理现实世界图像时，常因图像质量差、非正面姿态及遮挡等问题导致准确率显著下降。我们研究了定向的、基于AI的头部姿态校正与图像修复是否能提升识别准确率。通过模型无关、大规模、法证评估流程，我们评估了三种修复方法：3D重建（NextFace）、2D正面化（CFR-GAN）和特征增强（CodeFormer）。结果发现，这些技术的直接应用会显著降低面部识别准确率；但选择性应用CFR-GAN与CodeFormer结合，可带来显著提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the degradation of facial recognition accuracy in real-world images due to non-frontal poses and poor quality, this study evaluates whether AI-driven head-pose correction and image restoration can enhance biometric performance. It tests three methods—3D reconstruction (NextFace), 2D frontalization (CFR-GAN), and feature enhancement (CodeFormer)—using a model-agnostic forensic pipeline. Results show that applying these techniques naively harms accuracy, but selectively combining CFR-GAN with CodeFormer leads to measurable improvements in recognition performance.
该研究探讨AI驱动的头部姿态校正与图像修复是否能提升生物特征面部识别在真实世界图像（常为非正面或低质量）中的准确性。研究人员通过模型无关的大规模法证评估流程，评估了三种修复方法：3D重建（NextFace）、2D正面化（CFR-GAN）和特征增强（CodeFormer）。结果表明，这些技术的盲目应用会导致识别准确率显著下降，但选择性结合CFR-GAN与CodeFormer可带来显著性能提升。
Attacks and Mitigations for Distributed Governance of Agentic AI under Byzantine Adversaries
Authors: Matthew D. Laws, Alina Oprea, Cristina Nita-Rotaru
First: 2026-05-12T16:33:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-05-12T16:33:50+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 18 figures, 4 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic AI governance is a critical component of agentic AI infrastructure ensuring that agents follow their owner's communication and interaction policies, and providing protection against attacks from malicious agents. The state-of-the-art solution, SAGA, assumes a logically centralized point of trust, the Provider, which serves as a repository for user and agent information and actively enforces policies. While SAGA provides protection against malicious agents, it remains vulnerable to a malicious Provider that deviates from the protocol, undermining the security of the identity and access control infrastructure. Deployment on both private and public clouds, each susceptible to insider threats, further increases the risk of Provider compromise. In this work, we analyze the attacks that can be mounted from a compromised Provider, taking into account the different system components and realistic deployments. We identify and execute several concrete attacks with devastating effects: undermining agent attributability, extracting private data, or bypassing access control. We then present three types of solutions for securing the Provider that offer different trade-offs between security and performance. We first present SAGA-BFT, a fully byzantine-resilient architecture that provides the strongest protection, but incurs significant performance degradation, due to the high-cost of byzantine resilient protocols. We then propose SAGA-MON and SAGA-AUD, two novel solutions that leverage lightweight server-side monitoring or client-side auditing to provide protection against most classes of attacks with minimal overhead. Finally, we propose SAGA-HYB, a hybrid architecture that combines byzantine-resilience with monitoring and auditing to trade-off security for performance. We evaluate all the architectures and compare them with SAGA. We discuss which solution is best and under what conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：拜占庭对手下代理AI分布式治理的攻击与缓解措施
代理AI治理是代理AI基础设施的关键组成部分，确保代理遵循其所有者的通信与交互策略，并防范恶意代理的攻击。当前最先进的解决方案SAGA假设一个逻辑上中心化的信任点——提供者（Provider），作为用户和代理信息的存储库并主动执行策略。尽管SAGA可抵御恶意代理，但其仍易受偏离协议的恶意提供者攻击，从而破坏身份与访问控制基础设施的安全性。在私有云和公有云上部署时，两者均易受内部威胁，进一步增加了提供者被攻破的风险。 本研究分析了从被攻破的提供者可发起的攻击，考虑不同系统组件和现实部署。我们识别并实施了多种具体攻击，造成毁灭性影响：破坏代理可追溯性、提取私密数据或绕过访问控制。随后，我们提出了三种保护提供者的解决方案，提供不同安全与性能的权衡。首先，我们提出SAGA-BFT，一种完全拜占庭容错架构，提供最强保护，但因拜占庭容错协议成本高而显著降低性能。接着，我们提出SAGA-MON和SAGA-AUD两种新方案，分别利用轻量级服务端监控或客户端审计，以最小开销抵御大部分攻击类别。最后，我们提出SAGA-HYB混合架构，结合拜占庭容错、监控与审计，实现安全与性能的折中。我们评估了所有架构并对比SAGA，讨论在何种条件下哪种方案最优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of SAGA's centralized Provider to Byzantine adversaries, this work investigates attacks that compromise agent attributability, leak private data, or bypass access control when the Provider is malicious. It proposes three mitigation architectures: SAGA-BFT, a fully Byzantine-resilient but high-overhead solution; SAGA-MON and SAGA-AUD, lightweight approaches using server-side monitoring or client-side auditing; and SAGA-HYB, a hybrid model combining resilience with monitoring. Experimental evaluation shows SAGA-BFT offers maximum security at the cost of performance, while SAGA-MON and SAGA-AUD provide strong protection with minimal overhead, and SAGA-HYB strikes a practical balance between security and efficiency.
本文针对依赖可信中心节点Provider的SAGA系统，分析了Provider被攻破后可发起的严重攻击，如破坏代理可追溯性、窃取私密数据或绕过访问控制。作者提出三种缓解方案：SAGA-BFT采用完全拜占庭容错架构，安全性最高但性能代价大；SAGA-MON和SAGA-AUD分别通过轻量级服务端监控和客户端审计，在几乎无开销下防御多数攻击；SAGA-HYB则结合拜占庭容错与监控审计，实现安全与性能的折中。实验表明，SAGA-BFT安全性最强，SAGA-MON和SAGA-AUD开销极小且有效，SAGA-HYB则根据部署需求提供实用的平衡方案。
